A poll the user may not vote in shows its answers beside disabled buttons and why in `bodyMedium` `secondaryContent` ("Only members of the chat can vote", not in your country or region, not sent yet), never a tap that does nothing. A quiz's explanation, once answered or closed, on the bubble's block color with `medium` corners: "Explanation" in `labelMedium` accent over its formatted text. Message actions: "Retract vote" (Material Symbols "history"; Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-23): "undo" is not in the project's symbols yet) where the poll allows revoting, and "Stop poll" ("block") where TDLib lets the user edit the message, behind an `AlertDialog` ("Stop poll?", the confirm button in `error`). Quizzes answer with `Confirm` / `Reject` once Telegram took the answer. Screen readers hear the poll in the bubble's description, as every bubble (§9): each answer as one entry ("Autumn, 41%, 41 votes, Your answer", "correct answer" / "wrong answer", "chosen" before "Vote"), why they cannot vote, the votes, the explanation; and vote through the bubble's custom actions ("Vote for Summer", "Choose Summer" and "Vote", "View votes"). Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-23): the brief asked for each answer as its own item; the bubble is one item (§9), so answers are entries of its description and voting its custom actions, as bot buttons are. **Since 2026** (the owner's boards 4a, 4b): above the question a kicker in `labelMediumEmphasized` capitals, the secondary content color: "POLL · ANONYMOUS", "QUIZ · PUBLIC", "POLL · MULTIPLE ANSWERS", "POLL · CLOSED" (the kind, then closed, several answers or who sees the votes); a poll's photo or video (td_api.tl `messagePoll.media`) above it as wide as the content, as tall as its aspect ratio makes it within `mediaMinSize` and `mediaMaxHeight`, `medium` corners, dimmed in dark theme (§10), a video with its still and the play badge, a tap opening the media viewer at it with a container transform (§5, by the rich messages' media ids, `richMediaId`); the poll's description under the question (`bodyMedium`, user content, its links working) rather than as the bubble's text. An answer with a link (`pollOption.media` `pollMediaLink`) shows it under its text, its title or address in `labelMedium` in the accent, a link that opens the browser. A poll that closes by itself shows, after its answers, "Ends in 2 h" on a pill of the block color (full corners, `labelMedium` with tabular figures after the timer symbol), counting down with the minute (`ItemInputs.relativeNow`, nothing per second). Voted where the results stay hidden until the end (`poll.can_see_results` false while open): "Results hidden until the end" in `bodyMedium` secondary in place of the bars. Results: the answer or answers with the most votes in the emphasized weight once closed (`bodyMediumEmphasized`, the percentage `labelLargeEmphasized`); a quiz with several correct answers tags each "Your answer · correct", "Your answer · wrong" or "Correct · missed" in `labelSmall` and heads its explanation with "You got 1 of 2". Where members may add answers (`messagePoll.can_add_option`), "Add option" (a `TextButton` in the accent with the add symbol, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`) closes the answers and opens an inline `OutlinedTextField` ("New option", the bubble's content colors, at most 100 characters, user content) between a cancel `IconButton` and an "Add option" check; the keyboard's Done adds too; while Telegram takes it a `LoadingIndicator` stands in the check's place and the field waits; a refusal is a snackbar and the field stays (`addPollOption`). New service messages word an answer added or removed ("Ada added the option “Piranesi”"). Screen readers hear the kicker, the description, the links, the quiz tags, "Ends in 2 h", the hidden-results note and the score in the bubble's description; opening the photo, each link and "Add option" are its custom actions. Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): the kicker says "Multiple answers", as td_api.tl gives no "pick up to N"; the board's timer chip is the pill above; whether results are hidden is known only once voted (TDLib tells nothing before), so the note shows then; answers' photos, stickers and places (other `pollMedia` kinds) are not shown, only links |
| Checklists | `REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-C4 (the owner's board 1), padded in the bubble, `mediaMaxWidth` wide: a kicker row of the checklist symbol (`ic_checklist`, `inlineIconSmall`) and "CHECKLIST" in `labelMedium` (the secondary content color, in capitals), the title in `titleMediumEmphasized` (user content), then the tasks as rows of at least 48 dp (`minimumInteractiveComponentSize`, `small` ripple bounds): a round check of `sizes.avatarInline` (24 dp) before each task's text (`bodyMedium`, user content), an outline ring of `sizes.avatarRing` in the content color while not done (the secondary color where the user may not mark tasks), filled with the bubble's accent and a check in its on-accent color once done; where the checklist lets the user mark tasks (td_api.tl `checklist.can_mark_tasks_as_done`) a tap ticks or unticks it (`Role.Checkbox`, `ToggleOn` / `ToggleOff`), and while the change goes to Telegram the check shows a `LoadingIndicator` and takes no tap; a done task is struck through in the secondary content color, with the avatar of who ticked it (`avatarInline`, in their shape) at its end. Under the tasks a flat determinate `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` (amplitude 0: a level, not work under way) of the done share in the accent over the bubble's `track`, beside "2 of 5 done" in `labelMediumEmphasized` (tabular figures), one item for screen readers. Where the user may add tasks (`can_add_tasks`) "Add task" (a `FilledTonalButton` on the bubble's block color, the add symbol) opens an inline `OutlinedTextField` ("New task") in its place, a close `IconButton` before it and the check that adds it after it; the keyboard's Done adds too; a refused task is a snackbar and the field stays. Outgoing on the bubble's accent colors, incoming on the sender's hue (§13.6). Screen readers hear the bubble as one (§9): "Checklist", the title, "2 of 5 done", each task with "done by Ada" / "not done" / "saving"; ticking each task and "Add task" are its custom actions. The attach menu offers "Checklist" (the checklist symbol, after "Poll") to Telegram Premium subscribers only, in every chat but secret chats, never to anyone else (td_api.tl `premiumFeatureChecklists`); its form (`ChecklistFormSheet`, a `ModalBottomSheet` built as the poll form) has "New checklist" in `titleLargeEmphasized`, the title field, "Tasks" with 1–30 rounded fields and "Add a task", "Settings" with "Others can mark tasks as done" (on by default) and "Others can add tasks" as switch rows, and the send split button (silent, scheduled). The service messages ("Ada marked 2 tasks as done", "Ada added a task to the checklist") are pills. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-10): the round checks are drawn (a circle outlined or filled, `Role.Checkbox`), not Material's square `Checkbox`, as the board draws them; the progress bar is the wavy indicator held flat rather than a bar of the poll's kind; the kicker is in capitals, as the board sets it; the attach menu gates checklists by the signed-in user's `is_premium`, as td_api.tl names no option for it, and offers them in private chats too, where polls are not |
| New poll | The attach menu's "Poll" (`REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-C4, the owner's boards 5a, 5b): a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded, at most `contentMaxWidth`, its header pinned above what scrolls: "New poll" / "New quiz" in `titleLargeEmphasized` and the composer's round send button at the end (a long press for silent and scheduled), enabled once the question and two answers are there. Then the question and "Description (optional)" in the rounded tonal `TextField`s; "Add media" (a `FilledTonalButton` with the add-a-photo symbol, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`), which opens the system Photo Picker for one image (no permission; anything but a photo says "Polls take photos only"), prepared as picked photos are (`AttachmentRepository.prepare`), a `LoadingIndicator` while it is, then the photo (`sizes.contentIcon`, `medium` corners, from Coil at its size) with its file size, "Change media" (a `TextButton`) and "Remove photo". "Options" (`titleSmallEmphasized` `primary`) with "4 of 12" at its end (`labelLarge` `onSurfaceVariant`; TDLib's `poll_answer_count_max`, 10 until it says): each answer a rounded tonal field ("Option 1"), in a quiz a `Checkbox` before it for each correct answer (several allowed), a link `IconToggleButton` (`ic_link`, `toggleableShapes()`) that opens a field for the answer's link under it (left to right, the URI keyboard), a remove `IconButton` once there are more than two, and a drag handle (Pingogram's six-dot symbol, 48 dp): dragging lifts the row into its graphics layer while the others slide on `defaultSpatialSpec` with a `SegmentTick` each, screen readers "Move up" / "Move down"; "Add option" (`TextButton`, the add symbol) while fewer than the limit; a quiz then "Explanation (optional)" with "Shown once someone answers". "Settings" as switch rows (`CheckSwitch`, toggle haptics): Anonymous voting (disabled in channels, which are always anonymous); Multiple answers (polls); Quiz mode; Allow changing vote (polls); Hide results ("Voters see the results once the poll closes"); Shuffle options; Let members add options (public polls outside channels, disabled otherwise with why). "Closes": Never, 1 h, 1 day and Custom as connected `ToggleButton`s (`ButtonGroupDefaults` connected shapes, `SegmentTick`), Custom opening the composer's `DatePicker` + `TimePicker` in a `DatePickerDialog`, its choice below in `bodyMedium`; a choice longer than TDLib's `poll_open_period_max` is disabled, and the group is left out where TDLib gives none. The draft (texts, links, the photo's copy, the settings, the closing) is in the saved state. Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): Send pinned in the header rather than at the board's bottom, so it stays in view with the keyboard up; the photo's size shown as its file size; a quiz's explanation goes as text only (td_api.tl lets it carry media, which the form does not offer); a photo's copy left behind when the sheet is dismissed goes with the upload cache's cleaning |
| Poll voters | Who chose each answer of a public poll: a `ModalBottomSheet` as the conversation's other sheets: "Votes" in `titleLargeEmphasized` over the question (`bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`), then a section per answer someone chose, in the poll's order: its text in `titleSmallEmphasized` `primary` with its votes (`labelMedium`, `tnum`) as a heading, its voters as `ListItem`s inset like the chat list's rows (40 dp avatar, the name as user content, when they voted in the supporting text), loaded 20 at a time: a `LoadingIndicator` below while a page comes, the failure in `error` with a "Try again" `TextButton`, or a "Show more" `TextButton` (`shapes()`). One keyed `LazyColumn`. A tapped voter closes the sheet and opens their profile (a chat voting as itself: its chat) |
| Paid media | `REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-C4, §1.2 (the owner's board 2), edge to edge in the bubble like an album (`mediaMaxWidth`): **not paid for**, the previews' blurred minithumbnails (`BlurredMinithumbnail`, dimmed in dark theme as media are) in the album's mosaic (`albumLayout`) under a `scrim` at 32%, the price on a pill in `tertiaryContainer` / `onTertiaryContainer` (the lock symbol, the number in `titleSmallEmphasized` with tabular figures, the star symbol) over "3 photos" / "2 videos" / "5 photos and videos" on a `mediaOverlay` label, and below the mosaic, on the bubble's block color with `medium` corners, the info symbol and "Pingogram can't pay yet. Open this post in another Telegram app to unlock it." in `bodySmall`; nothing is tappable and no purchase is offered, and the bubble's time shows below the note, not over the media. **Paid for**: its photos and videos as the album (`MediaAlbum`), each opening in the media viewer and growing into it by an id of its own (`richMediaId`, as a rich message's media; the viewer pages the post's media only), with "Bought · 25 ★" on a `mediaOverlay` label over the top start corner. The caption is the bubble's. Screen readers hear the mosaic as one item ("Paid media, 3 photos, 25 Telegram Stars. Pingogram can't pay yet…"), the paid album's items as any album's. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-10): the note sits below the mosaic and above the caption rather than below the caption as the board draws it, so the media keeps square corners where the note joins it and the time shows below; the price pill is `tertiaryContainer` (the board's gold) rather than the bubble's accent; and nothing in a locked post takes a tap, as a tap could only repeat the note |
| Giveaways | `REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-C4, §1.2 (the owner's board 3), padded in the bubble, `mediaMaxWidth` wide. **A giveaway**: the prize card on `tertiaryContainer` with `medium` corners (the gift symbol on a `MaterialShapes.Sunny` badge in `tertiary` / `onTertiary` at `sizes.headlineBadge`; "GIVEAWAY PRIZE" in `labelMedium`, in capitals; "3 ×" in `experimentalTypography.displaySmallCondensed` beside "Telegram Premium" in `titleSmallEmphasized`, or the number of Telegram Stars beside "Telegram Stars"; "for 3 months" or "shared among 3 winners" in `bodyMedium`); "Participating channels" (`titleSmallEmphasized` in the bubble's accent) over the channels as `AssistChip`s on the bubble's block color (a 24 dp avatar in the channel's shape, the name as user content), each opening the channel (`ChatEvent.OpenChat`); "Winners are picked on" over the date and time (`titleSmall`, tabular figures, the language's medium date and short time) and who takes part ("Among all subscribers of these channels" / "Among new subscribers…", the countries by name, the channel's own description of the prize) in `bodySmall`; and "Learn more", a `FilledTonalButton` on the block color across the width. **The winners**: the trophy symbol on a `Cookie9Sided` badge in the bubble's accent, "Giveaway winners" in `titleMediumEmphasized` over "3 people won Telegram Premium for 3 months" in `bodyMedium`, the winners as tiles three to a row on the block color with `large` corners (48 dp avatar over the name in `labelLarge`), each opening their profile; "The winners were told privately." where Telegram names none, that the giveaway was refunded, what nobody claimed; and "Learn more". A channel or person TDLib has not announced is named "Channel" / "Winner" with a placeholder avatar. **Learn more** opens a `ModalBottomSheet` as the conversation's other sheets: "About this giveaway" in `titleLargeEmphasized` over the prize, then what TDLib knows for the user (`getGiveawayInfo`, asked only then): while it runs, when the winners are picked and whether the user takes part or why not (joined before it began, administers a channel, from a country left out); once over, when they were picked, whether the user won (a Telegram Premium gift code shown to activate in another Telegram app, never applied here; the Telegram Stars won) and how many winners took their prize; a `DelayedLoadingIndicator` while it loads, the failure in `error` with "Try again"; a `bodySmall` note that Pingogram enters and buys nothing; "Close". Screen readers hear the bubble as one (§9) with the prize, the channels, the date and who takes part, or the winners' names, and open each channel, each winner's profile and the details as its custom actions. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-10): the chips are Material's assist chips on the bubble's block color rather than the board's avatar pills; the board's "Learn more" opens the details without asking Telegram until then; and the sticker Telegram attaches to a giveaway is not drawn, the gift badge standing for it |
| Unsupported messages | A message of a kind this version cannot show (td_api.tl `messageUnsupported`, and content a newer TDLib added), padded in the bubble: "This version of Pingogram can't show this message yet." in `bodyMedium` and the bubble's secondary content color, then "Update Pingogram" as a `TextButton` in the bubble's accent, which opens the latest release's page on GitHub (`ChatEffect.OpenUrl`). Builds an app store installed (Google Play, Huawei AppGallery, Samsung Galaxy Store: `AppInfo.isFromAppStore`, as About leaves out "Check for updates") show the text alone, as the store updates them. The chat list keeps "Unsupported message" as the preview. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-10): a link to the releases page rather than Telegram's own update button, which points at the stores, and the text alone in store builds |
| Dates in messages | A date a sender marked (td_api.tl `textEntityTypeDateTime`) shows in each reader's time zone, language and clock, in place of the sender's words, in the mention style (`mentionColor` of the bubble), as a link: as much of the date and time as its format asks (the date short or long, the weekday, the time with or without seconds; the platform's best pattern for that skeleton), or relative to now ("in 3 days", worded by the platform and again each minute); a date without a format keeps the sender's words. A tap opens a `ModalBottomSheet` as the conversation's other sheets: the date in full in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading), "In your time zone: Central European Time" in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, then "Add to calendar" (`ic_calendar_month`, the calendar app's new-event screen at that moment through `ACTION_INSERT`, no permission; a snackbar where no calendar app is installed) and "Copy" (`ic_content_copy`) as list items inset like the chat list's rows. Screen readers hear the date as shown, and open it as a link of the text. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-10): the sender's words are replaced, as Telegram's apps do, so a quote or copy of the bubble shows the reader's wording while the message's own copy ("Copy text") keeps the sender's; scheduled messages show relative dates in full, and rich messages' dates keep the sender's words but open the same sheet |
| Rich message | The posts channels write with headings, sections, photos, lists and tables (td_api.tl `messageRichMessage`), in the padded bubble of its side, as wide as a bubble gets (`sizes.bubbleMaxWidthFraction`, at most `bubbleMaxWidth`), its blocks `spacing.small` apart in the post's own direction (`isRtl`), every text as user content with links, mentions and spoilers working as in a text bubble. **Texts**: title `titleLargeEmphasized`; subtitle `titleMedium` in the secondary content color; header `titleMediumEmphasized`, subheader and second-level headings `titleSmallEmphasized`, smaller headings `labelLargeEmphasized`; a kicker `labelLarge` in the bubble's accent; paragraphs and list items the message text style (`MaterialTheme.chatStyle.messageText`); a footer `bodySmall` secondary; preformatted text and formulas (as written, not typeset) `bodyMedium` in `FontFamily.Monospace` on the bubble's block color with `medium` corners; a divider `outlineVariant`. **Quotes** as reply quotes: a tonal card on the block color with `medium` corners and the quote mark (`ic_format_quote`, `inlineIconSmall`), the credit `labelMedium` secondary; a pull quote `titleMedium` italic, centred. **Lists**: the item's label ("1.", "•") in the secondary color, or for a checklist a `Checkbox` in the accent that is no control (the sender checked it). **Sections**: a card on the block color with `medium` corners, the header a 48 dp row (`titleSmallEmphasized`, `ic_keyboard_arrow_down` turning half a turn on `fastSpatialSpec` in its graphics layer) that toggles it; the content grows in and shrinks away (`expandVertically` / `shrinkVertically` on `defaultSpatialSpec`, fades on the effects springs); open at first where Telegram says so, the user's toggles kept across process death. **Media**: photos, videos and GIFs as wide as the bubble's content, as tall as their aspect ratio makes them at `mediaMaxWidth` (within `mediaMinSize` and `mediaMaxHeight`), cropped, `medium` corners, dimmed in dark theme (§10), photos following automatic download, videos and GIFs their still with the play or GIF badge, spoilers the blurred minithumbnail; the caption `bodyMedium` and credit `labelMedium` below, secondary. A tap opens the media viewer at it, growing into it with a container transform (§5) and paging through the post's media only (no Delete there: it would delete the whole post); a photo that links somewhere opens its link instead. Collages and slideshows show their media one after another. **Tables**: the caption, then a grid that scrolls sideways where wider than the bubble, columns as wide as their widest cell (at most `mediaMaxWidth`), header cells `titleSmallEmphasized` on the block color, others `bodyMedium`; bordered tables ruled in `outlineVariant` (`DividerDefaults.Thickness`) with `small` corners, striped ones every other row on the block color. **Buttons**: a row of `FilledTonalButton`s sharing its width (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()`), a link's ending in `open_in_new`; buttons of other kinds disabled. A block Pingogram cannot show: "This part can't be shown yet" in `bodyMedium` italic, secondary. **Long and partial posts**: taller than one and a half windows, the post is cut there with a "Show more" `TextButton` in the accent below it; where Telegram sent only the start, "Show full message" follows, disabled with a `LoadingIndicator` before it while it loads, its failure a snackbar. Screen readers hear the bubble as one (§9): every block's text, "Section What's new, Collapsed" with an open section's content, checklist items as "Done" or "Not done", "Table, 3 rows" before its cells, the kind of each photo, video or GIF; sections ("Expand What's new", "Collapse Sizes"), "Open Photo 2 of 3" and "Show full message" are its custom actions. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-06): as with polls, the brief asked for section headers as buttons and headings as semantic headings; the bubble is one item, so they are its description and custom actions. Not yet: custom emoji pictures in rich texts (their emoji shows), merging cells that span rows, translation. |
| Bot buttons | The inline keyboard a bot attaches to a message (td_api.tl `replyMarkupInlineKeyboard`), below its bubble (and its reactions), `spacing.extraSmall` under it, as wide as a bubble can be (`sizes.bubbleMaxWidthFraction`, at most `sizes.bubbleMaxWidth`), on the bubble's side, the comments link `spacing.small` below it. Each row a connected group: `FilledTonalButton`s (`secondaryContainer` / `onSecondaryContainer`) sharing the row's width equally, `ButtonGroupDefaults.ConnectedSpaceBetween` apart, with the connected leading / middle / trailing shapes and their press morph (a row of one keeps `ButtonDefaults.shapesFor`' pill); the small container height (`ButtonDefaults.MinHeight`, 40 dp) in 48 dp touch targets, rows `spacing.small` apart so the targets meet without overlapping; labels `labelLarge` (`textStyleFor`) as user content on one line, ellipsized. Not Material's `ButtonGroup` itself, whose overflow measuring would compose every row again in each bubble. A bot's style: primary `primary` / `onPrimary` (`buttonColors`), danger `errorContainer` / `onErrorContainer`, the others tonal; disabled buttons Material's disabled colors. Link and login buttons end in `open_in_new`, switch-inline ones in `alternate_email`, at `iconSizeFor`; a button waiting for Telegram (a callback, a login) shows a `LoadingIndicator` there instead. Screen readers hear "3 buttons" in the bubble's description and press each through the bubble's custom actions ("Results, opens a link"). After a tap: a callback's answer a snackbar, or an `AlertDialog` with "OK" where the bot asks for an alert; a link whose button shows other text, which is not a Telegram link, an `AlertDialog` "Open this link?" with the link (left to right, selectable), "Cancel" / "Open"; a login an `AlertDialog` "Open example.org?" with what it shares, "Log in as …" and "Allow @bot to send me messages" as checkbox rows, a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers; a 2-step password a `ModalBottomSheet` (`titleLargeEmphasized` title, why in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, a filled `SecureTextField` with its error below and a `Reject` haptic, `TextButton` "Cancel" at the start and a default `Button` "Confirm" at the end); an inline query for another chat the forward sheet's chat list in a `ModalBottomSheet` ("Choose a chat", the query in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`), one tap opening the chat with "@bot query" in its composer. These dialogs guard what would leave the app unasked, as destructive confirmations do; errors stay snackbars. Mini Apps, games and payments: a snackbar that Pingogram does not offer them yet |
| Bot keyboard | A bot's reply keyboard (td_api.tl `replyMarkupShowKeyboard`, the chat's `reply_markup_message_id`) in the system keyboard's place, as the emoji panel: the keyboard's last height (`sizes.inputPanelHeight` before it showed, at most half the window), or for a keyboard that may be resized only as tall as its rows need, up to that, never less than the system keyboard while it comes and goes; on the composer's `surface`, no wider than a bubble can be (`sizes.bubbleMaxWidth`) and centred, `spacing.small` around. Its rows are the bot buttons' rows (connected `FilledTonalButton`s at the small height in 48 dp targets, `spacing.small` apart, the bot's primary and danger tones, a waiting button's `LoadingIndicator`) in a keyed `LazyColumn` that scrolls beyond; screen readers hear a pane, "Bot keyboard", of plain buttons. In the pill, before the emoji button, a toggle `IconButton` (`IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`, `PlainTooltip`): the grid (`ic_grid_view`, "Show bot keyboard") brings it back, the keyboard (`ic_keyboard`, "Show keyboard") the system keyboard, both without moving the composer. It comes up whenever neither the system keyboard nor the emoji panel is (a new keyboard, the chat opening); leaving it (the toggle, the field, the emoji button, back) puts it away until the toggle or a new keyboard, except a keyboard the bot keeps on screen (`is_persistent`), which comes back whenever the system keyboard goes down and which back passes by; a one-time keyboard puts itself away after a tap. Its hint (`input_field_placeholder`) is the field's. Not while editing or recording, nor in topics and threads; while an inline query ("@gif cats") shows its results above the composer, it steps aside and comes back after. Text buttons send their text. Requests ask first: the phone number an `AlertDialog` "Share your phone number with @bot?" (the number formatted, left to right, on one line; "Cancel" / "Share", a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it goes); the location the attach menu's "Location" sheet with its permission step; a poll its form, the quiz switch locked with a note where the bot requires a quiz or a regular poll; users and chats a `ModalBottomSheet` built from the forward sheet's parts: "Choose up to 3 people" / "Choose a group" / "Choose a channel" (`titleLargeEmphasized`), what the bot gets and what Telegram checks or does on sharing (only chats where the user is an admin when rights are asked; the bot added or made an admin) in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, the search field, the candidates the button allows as toggleable rows (up to its number; a chat one at a time), then a default `Button` "Share" (`shapes()`, `Confirm`) growing in on `defaultSpatialSpec`, the failure in `error` as a polite live region. A bot's request for a reply (`replyMarkupForceReply`) starts a reply to its message once, with its hint. Mini App buttons: a snackbar that Pingogram can't open them yet |
| Folder tabs | `ButtonGroup` connected `ToggleButton`s with `animateWidth` press physics in a 48 dp row (40 dp buttons in their 48 dp touch targets), `spacing.medium` above the list's sheet (owner decision 2026-11-06), in a horizontal scroll that brings the selected button into view and fades at the edges, so the group never overflows into its menu; selected label `labelLargeEmphasized`; fallback `PrimaryScrollableTabRow` if > 6. A long press on a tab (or a right click; a `LongPress` haptic) opens an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` anchored below it (one `DropdownMenuGroup`, `MenuDefaults.groupShape` / `itemShape`, outlined symbols): "Edit folder" ("Edit folders" on "All chats") first where the app has a folder editor, then "Mark all as read" while any chat of the tab's list is unread, muted ones included (td_api.tl `readChatList`); a tab with neither has no menu, and none opens in selection mode. Screen readers get the same items as the tab's custom actions. The long press never selects the tab. Owner decision (2026-11-06): where the app has a folder editor, "Edit folders" ends the row, `spacing.small` after the tabs' fade, an `OutlinedIconButton` (40 dp in its 48 dp target, `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`, `PlainTooltip`) with the tune symbol, opening the chat folder settings as the tab menu's "Edit folders" does; disabled in selection mode with the tabs. Owner decision (2026-11-07): selecting a tab slides its folder's list in from the side of the tab (from the end for a tab after the selected one, mirrored right to left) as the shown list leaves toward the other, side by side on `defaultSpatialSpec` in their graphics layers, the new list where the user left it; with reduced motion they crossfade on the effects springs. Owner decision (2026-11-07): Appearance → Chat list → "Edit folders button" (a switch row, `SwitchItem`, on by default, "Shows a button at the end of the folder tabs", kept on the device) hides it; while off the row ends at the tabs, and the tabs' menus keep "Edit folders". Owner decision (2026-11-08): Appearance → Chat list → "Folder tabs" (a segment with "Names" / "Icons and names" / "Icons" as `ChoiceRow`'s connected buttons, kept on the device, names by default) sets what a tab shows: its label; the folder's symbol (`core:ui`'s `folderIconDrawable`: TDLib's icon name mapped to the Material Symbols the folder editor offers, the plain folder for the others, Telegram's "All" for "All chats") at `ButtonDefaults.iconSizeFor` before its label; or the symbol alone, the unread count in a `Badge` on it (`BadgedBox`), while the selected tab keeps its name, as a tab's long press opens its menu rather than a tooltip. Screen readers hear every tab's name and count whatever the style |
| Main navigation | **Compact width: floating navigation bar** (§6.1). Medium and expanded widths, and windows of compact height (phones in landscape): `NavigationSuiteScaffold` → `WideNavigationRail` collapsed, "New chat" (a round `FloatingActionButton`) in its header on the Chats tab. Large and extra-large widths (≥ 1200 dp): the rail expanded, "New chat" as an `ExtendedFloatingActionButton` in a full pill. Search opens from the Chats tab's header |
| New chat | Owner decisions (2026-11-06, 2026-11-07): on compact windows a round `FloatingActionButton` beside the floating navigation bar's pill, `spacing.small` after it, a full circle as tall as the pill (`sizes.floatingNavigationBar`, `FloatingToolbarDefaults.StandardFloatingActionButton`, `CircleShape`), the filled edit symbol in `primaryContainer` / `onPrimaryContainer`, "New chat" for screen readers (`BarNewChatButton`, `:app`). Only on the Chats tab: on the other tabs the pill sits alone and does not move as the button comes and goes (owner decision to confirm, 2026-11-06), as the pill stays centred in the window and the button sits at its end; only where the pill and the button's room do not fit side by side does the pill move toward the start, by what is missing, on every tab alike (owner decision to confirm, 2026-11-07: with the tabs' labels back, a 411 dp phone leaves the pill a little off centre on every tab rather than moving it as the tab changes). On rail windows the rail's header holds it, in search's old place: a round `FloatingActionButton` on the collapsed rail, an `ExtendedFloatingActionButton` "New chat" in a full pill on the expanded one, on the Chats tab only, its place kept on the others. It hides in selection mode with the bar, fades as the tab changes, and grows into the New chat screen (new group, new channel, new contact, then contacts under a `titleSmallEmphasized` header; a small top bar on `surfaceContainer` and the list on a `ContentSheet`, as the archive) with a container transform from the circle (`newChatButtonTransform`), away while New chat shows. Not a FAB menu: Material says not to pair one with a floating toolbar or a navigation rail |
| New group | Two steps pushed above New chat (shared axis X), each a small top bar on `surfaceContainer` and a `ContentSheet`. **People**: the search bar's `InputField` on its container; the people picked as `InputChip`s (`InputChipDefaults.shapes()`, avatar, close symbol) in a `LazyRow` that scrolls sideways, growing in (`expandVertically` on `defaultSpatialSpec`) with the first; the contacts as toggleable `ListItem`s inset like the forward sheet's rows (48 dp avatar with its online dot, the name in `titleMedium`, "@username"), a picked one `large` in `secondaryContainer` with its avatar morphed into a check (§4); a `SmallExtendedFloatingActionButton` "Next" with a forward arrow; at most 200 picks, then a snackbar. **Details**: a form at most `contentMaxWidth` wide: the photo as an `avatarLarge` circle in `primaryContainer` (add-a-photo symbol; the picked photo with a camera badge in `primary` and "Remove photo"), opening the system photo picker; the name in the rounded tonal `TextField` (required, its error below it) with how many people will be added; "Auto-delete messages" (`titleSmallEmphasized` in `primary`) as connected `ToggleButton`s (off, a day, a week, a month); the `FLOOD_WAIT` countdown or a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator`, then a default `Button` "Create" (`shapes()`). People Telegram did not add (privacy) are named in an `AlertDialog` with "Share invite link" (share sheet) and "OK", then the group opens in the flow's place |
| New channel | Three steps pushed above New chat, as New group. **Details**: the photo, the name and an optional description (multi-line) in rounded tonal fields, then "Next", which creates the channel. **Type** (no back button: system back opens the channel, which exists by then): Private / Public as connected `ToggleButton`s with what each means, crossfading (effects springs, `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`) between the invite link on a `surfaceContainerHigh` card (`large`) with "Copy" and "Share" `FilledTonalButton`s (a `DelayedLoadingIndicator` while it comes, its error with "Try again") and the username after "t.me/" in a rounded tonal field, left to right, checked for its form at once and with Telegram after a pause: "Checking…", available, or the refusal as the field's error; "Next" only once it is free. **Subscribers**: the New group people step without back, its button "Skip" or "Add"; back opens the channel |
| Jump to bottom | `FloatingActionButton` (56 dp) in `secondaryContainer`, unread count in a `Badge` on its top end corner (at most "999+", left to right), reaching no more than `spacing.extraSmall` past the button's end, so a longer count grows toward the start and stays on screen, bottom-end above the composer (Material no longer recommends the small FAB) |
| Selection mode | `HorizontalFloatingToolbar` (expressive, `FloatingToolbarDefaults.vibrantFloatingToolbarColors()`), each icon button with a `PlainTooltip` (`TooltipBox`); the top bar becomes a small `TopAppBar` with close and "N selected" (`titleLarge`). Chat list: in the floating navigation bar's place (§6.1). Conversation: in the composer's place, sliding up on `defaultSpatialSpec` as the composer fades; a tap or long press toggles a message (toggle haptics); back is predictive, the toolbar scaling to 90% (`rememberSelectionBack`, `core:ui`) |
| Report sheet | `ModalBottomSheet` as "About this ad"'s report step: `titleLargeEmphasized` title, Telegram's reasons as transparent `ListItem`s, a comment in the rounded tonal `TextField` with a default `Button` ("Send report", `shapes()` press morph), steps crossfading on effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`, a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it reports |
| Composer | On `surface` (the messages' sheet runs into it). **Fade** (owner request 2026-11-07): the messages fade into it as they scroll under it, a `spacing.extraLarge` gradient from transparent to `surface` just above it (`ComposerFade`), not at all while the list rests on its newest message and fully a fade's height away, read in the draw phase. **Field**: a 56 dp pill (`sizes.composerField`) 8 dp from the window's sides, `surfaceContainer` (the top bar's color), `BasicTextField(state)` with `TextFieldDefaults.decorator` (no label, `spacing.extraSmall` around everything and `spacing.medium` above and below the text) and `tonalColors()` on its `Container`, in `extraLarge` corners, which make one line a full pill (`TextFieldDefaults.roundedShape` is `medium`, 12 dp, too square), `bodyLarge` as user content (`userContent`, `core:ui`: `TextDirection.Content`), so right-to-left text lines up to the right as it is typed. Inside the pill, outside Material's icon slots, which centre them (they stay at the bottom, level with the last line, as the field grows): at the start the attach "+" (`IconButton`, `onSurfaceVariant`) where the chat takes attachments; at the end the emoji button and, while the field is empty and not editing, the gallery (`ic_image`, the system Photo Picker without the attach menu), which gives way as text comes. **Bots** (`REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-C5): where the chat's bots take commands, or a private chat's bot has a menu button, the pill's start holds the bot button instead of "+" (an `IconButton` with `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`, 48 dp target): Pingogram's own "/" symbol (`ic_bot_commands`, a slash in a rounded square: Material Symbols has none) for the commands, which opens their sheet; for a bot's Mini App menu button (td_api.tl `botMenuButton`, which takes the commands menu's place) an app's tiles (`ic_bot_app`), named "Open app, Mini App" by the bot's text, which says in a snackbar that Pingogram can't open Mini Apps yet, never a WebView. The "+" then takes the gallery's place at the end, while the field is empty, giving way as text comes as the gallery does (the gallery is the attach menu's first option): Material asks for few icons in the pill, and so the field keeps the widths it has in other chats on a phone (three icons while empty, two while typing), the commands being what a bot chat is used through and attachments to bots rare. Not while editing. Typing "/" at the start of the field shows the commands it starts above the composer (Bot command suggestions); a bot with a Mini App menu button keeps its commands reachable there. The send-as avatar, where there is one, sits before the pill. **Button**: one 56 dp round button 8 dp beside the pill, bottom-aligned (`IconButtonDefaults.mediumContainerSize()`, `mediumRoundShape` morphing to `mediumPressedShape`): the microphone in `tertiaryContainer` / `onTertiaryContainer` while the field is empty, send (`primary` / `onPrimary`) once there is text, a check while editing. A long press on send (`LongPress` haptic) opens its menu below it: "Send without sound" and "Schedule message", also the button's screen-reader custom actions. The mic morphs to a wide `primary` pill (`IconButtonWidthOption.Wide`) while it records, its colors cross-fading on `defaultEffectsSpec`: held, it holds a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` whose amplitude follows the voice; locked, it is the send button. **Recording** (`feature:chat` `voice/`): hold the mic to record, slide toward the start to cancel, slide up to lock, release to send; the pill gives way to a recording bar as tall, a tonal pill (`surfaceContainer`, `extraLarge`) with a dot in `error` that swells with the voice and the length (`bodyLarge`, `tnum`), then "Slide to cancel" and a lock while held, Delete (`error`), the live waveform (`primary`, drawn in the draw phase) and Pause / Resume while locked; paused, the dot and the waveform turn `onSurfaceVariant`. Screen readers and keyboards record locked at once. Icon buttons keep 48 dp touch targets. **Width**: the pill and its button, with the reply or edit bar above them, capped at the bubbles' max width (`sizes.bubbleMaxWidth`) and centred on wide windows; the bar's `surface` and the emoji panel stay full width. **Reply bar**: above the pill, the reply icon (`primary`; a quote mark, "Quote" for screen readers, for a quote reply), the sender in `labelLarge` `primary` over one line of the message in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` (the quoted part for a quote reply), and close; a tap shows the message in the list. A reply to a message of another chat says "Reply to Ada in Team chat" and does not open it. **Edit bar**: while editing a message whose photo, video, GIF, song or file TDLib lets be replaced (td_api.tl `messageProperties.can_edit_media`), an `IconButton` "Replace photo or video" / "Replace file" (`swap_horiz`, `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`, a `PlainTooltip`) before close opens the Photo Picker (one photo or video) or the file picker (one file); the pick opens the attach preview without a caption field ("Replace with this" in `titleLargeEmphasized`, "The caption stays as you edit it in the message." in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`), a `TextButton` "Choose another" at the start and a default `Button` "Replace" at the end (`Confirm` haptic), which sends the new media with the edit's text as its caption; a refusal brings the edit back with a snackbar. Formatting shows in the field as in bubbles (an output transformation), a spoiler on `tertiaryContainer`; scheduling = `DatePicker`, then `TimePicker`, in a `DatePickerDialog`. The attach preview's caption, the forward sheet's comment and the share sheet's comment use the same field on `surfaceContainerHighest`, their send button the same round one. **Round video messages** (`docs/adr/0032-round-video-messages.md`): where the device has a camera, a tap on the microphone switches it to a camera (`ic_videocam_filled`, the same `tertiaryContainer` button) and back, as Telegram's apps do, with a `SegmentTick` haptic and the button's `PlainTooltip` ("Record video message" / "Record voice message", shown after the switch only, as touch would show it on the long press that records); the choice is kept. A press becomes a hold after 150 ms; holding the camera records with the voice gestures (slide to cancel, slide up to lock, release to send, the button turning into the wide `primary` pill with the voice's level), screen readers and keyboards record locked at once, and switch through the button's custom action ("Switch to video message"). While it records, a round preview sits centred over the messages above the composer, on a `scrim` at 32%: the camera's picture (camera-compose's `CameraXViewfinder`) clipped to `MaterialShapes.Circle`, two thirds of the window's shorter side and no taller than the room above the composer, inside a determinate `CircularWavyProgressIndicator` (`primary` on a `surfaceContainerHighest` track) that fills toward the minute's limit in the draw phase; until the picture comes, the camera symbol on `surfaceContainerHighest`; it grows in on `defaultSpatialSpec` with an effects-spring fade; screen readers hear "Video message, 0:07 of 1:00", politely, once a second. The recording bar is the voice one's; locked, "Flip camera" (`ic_cameraswitch`) comes before Pause where there are two cameras, and the send button reads "Send video message". At a minute it sends by itself. Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-25): leaving the app (or rotating) ends a video recording, releasing the camera and the microphone, and the bar then shows "Recording stopped" with only Delete and send; and recording starts after 150 ms of holding wherever a tap switches. The first switch (or a press without the permissions) opens a `ModalBottomSheet` as the Share location sheet's permission step: "Record video messages" in `titleLargeEmphasized`, the camera on `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided` in `primaryContainer`, why and what is kept in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, `TextButton` "Not now" at the start and a default `Button` "Continue" (both permissions at once; "Open settings" once Android no longer asks) at the end. Without a camera the button stays a microphone and nothing switches |
| Secret chat (conversation) | `REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-E1. At the start of the history (and in place of the empty state): a service-style card centred like service messages, on `surfaceContainerHighest` with `extraLarge` corners, at most `sizes.bubbleMaxWidth`: the lock in `primary` and "End-to-end encrypted" in `titleSmallEmphasized`, then four short lines in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` (only the two can read it, kept on this device only and not forwardable, the self-destruct timer, screenshots blocked on this device); one item for screen readers. While the chat waits or after it ended, the composer's place says why in its label (`ComposerUiState.Unavailable`). A message sent with a self-destruct timer shows it before its time in the meta: the timer icon (`sizes.inlineIconSmall`) and "30s" in `labelSmall`, the meta's color; static, as TDLib sends no update while it runs down. The window is secure while a secret chat (or its media in the viewer) shows: no screenshots or recordings |
| Self-destruct timer sheet | `ModalBottomSheet` from the top bar's timer: `titleLargeEmphasized` "Self-destruct timer", what it does in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, then Off, 1, 2, 5, 10, 15 and 30 seconds, 1 minute, 1 hour, 1 day and 1 week (and a timer the other side set to another value, in its place) as selectable `ListItem`s inset like the chat list's rows, the chosen one `large` in `secondaryContainer`. A tapped timer shows chosen with a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` under the text until TDLib takes it, as send-as does; errors are snackbars |
| Encryption key sheet | `ModalBottomSheet` from the top bar's key, scrolling: `titleLargeEmphasized` "Encryption key"; the key's 12 × 12 image (td_api.tl `secretChat.key_hash`) inside the QR login's frame (`MaterialShapes.Cookie12Sided` in `primaryContainer`, 80% of the width up to `sizes.illustrationMaxSize`, the image 60% of it with `small` corners and an `outline` border of `sizes.keyImageOutline`), its four colors the *Fixed* tones `primaryFixed`, `tertiaryFixedDim`, `onPrimaryFixed`, `onTertiaryFixedVariant`: they keep their tone in both themes, so the image looks the same in light and dark and keeps the light-to-dark order of the official palette (white, light, dark, middle), which the other person compares it with; then the first 32 bytes as four lines of eight hexadecimal pairs in `bodyMedium` and `FontFamily.Monospace`, left to right, centred; what to compare in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` (before the key is agreed only that it shows then); and "End secret chat" as an `error` `TextButton`, behind an `AlertDialog` |
| Bot commands sheet | `ModalBottomSheet` from the composer's "/" button, as the conversation's other sheets: `titleLargeEmphasized` "Commands"; with more than eight commands the search bar's `InputField` ("Search commands", by name and description, "No commands found" in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`); then the commands as `ListItem`s inset like the chat list's rows: "/start" in `bodyLarge`, left to right (`numericContent`; "/start@helper_bot" in a group, so only that bot takes it), what it does in the supporting style (two lines), in a group the bot's 40 dp avatar at the start, and at the end an `IconButton` with Pingogram's fill-in arrow (`ic_insert_command`, pointing to the start and up like a search suggestion's fill-in arrow, mirrored in RTL). **A tap sends the command** at once, as Telegram's apps do, since most commands take no arguments, replying where the composer replies; the arrow puts it into the field with a space after it and focuses the field, for its arguments, before what the field holds. Screen readers hear a row as one ("/start, Start the bot", the bot's name in a group), "Insert /start" as its custom action |
| Bot command suggestions | While the field holds a command being typed ("/", then the letters, digits and "_" of a command, and "@bot" in a group, before any space or line), the commands it starts show above the composer and its reply bar, next to the field they complete: one group of `SegmentedListItem`s (`ListItemDefaults.segmentedShapes`, `SegmentedGap` apart) on `surfaceContainer`, the pill's color, inset `spacing.small` as the pill is, at most `sizes.inputPanelHeight` (about four rows, the emoji panel's height) and a third of the window tall, scrolling beyond; each row as in the sheet with one line of description, the typed part of the command in `primary` and the emphasized weight, as search highlights. It grows in (`expandVertically` from the bottom on `defaultSpatialSpec`, effects-spring fade) and shrinks away once a space starts the arguments. A tap sends the command (the field's "/sta" with it); the arrow inserts it. A hardware keyboard picks with the up and down arrows (the picked row selected: `secondaryContainer`, `large`), Enter sends it (the first without a pick), Tab inserts it, Escape hides them until the command typed changes; the on-screen keyboard's Enter keeps starting a line. Screen readers hear "3 commands" as the pane's title when they show, and each row as in the sheet. Not while editing or recording |
| Inline bot results | While the field holds an inline query ("@gif cats": "@", a username and a space at the field's start), and the username names an inline bot, its results show above the composer and its reply bar, where the "/" suggestions show: one `surfaceContainer` panel (the pill's color) with `large` corners, inset `spacing.small` as the pill is, at most 40% of the window tall, scrolling beyond, growing in (`expandVertically` from the bottom on `defaultSpatialSpec`, effects-spring fade) and shrinking away once the field holds no query. While nothing follows "@gif ", the bot's placeholder ("Search GIFs…", td_api.tl `inline_query_placeholder`) follows the username in the field in `bodyLarge` `onSurfaceVariant`, placed in the layout phase. Inside, one `LazyVerticalGrid`: photos, GIFs, stickers and videos as square cells (at least `sizes.sharedMediaCell`, three across a phone; `sizes.panelSticker` where all are stickers), `extraSmall` corners and `sizes.mediaAlbumGap` apart as the shared media, decoded at the cells' size, the blurred minithumbnail under them, dimmed in dark theme as the conversation's media (§10), GIFs with "GIF" and videos with their length on a `mediaOverlay` label (still pictures: nothing plays in the grid); articles, contacts, places, songs, files, voice messages and games as rows across it, Material's list item on the panel's color with the bot's thumbnail (`sizes.contentIcon`, `medium` corners) or the kind's icon in a `secondaryContainer` circle, the title in `bodyLarge` over the bot's line in `bodyMedium`, user content (a phone number left to right). The bot's button (td_api.tl `inlineQueryResultsButton`) heads them as a centred `FilledTonalButton` (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()`, the bot's text). A tap sends a result (`Confirm` haptic) and clears the field; a long press opens the send button's menu ("Send without sound", "Schedule message" where the composer schedules), also screen readers' custom actions. The next page loads 6 items from the end, a `LoadingIndicator` below them, its error as a segment. States: `DelayedLoadingIndicator` in a cell's height; "No results" in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`; errors as global search shows them (an `errorContainer` segment, `large`, the error icon, the text and "Try again" in `onErrorContainer`; a flood wait counts down with "Try again" disabled); while a newer query is on its way the results stay, a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` along the panel's top after 200 ms, taking no room. A hardware keyboard picks with the down and up arrows (the pick `secondaryContainer`, `large`), Enter sends the pick (without one Enter stays the field's), Escape hides the results until the query changes. Screen readers hear "@gif, 24 results" as the pane's title and each result as one button, its kind then its title ("GIF, Cat"). A game says Pingogram can't play games yet, a Mini App button that it can't open Mini Apps yet (snackbars). Not while editing or recording |
| Inline bot location | Before the first query to a bot that asks for the user's location (td_api.tl `need_location`): a `ModalBottomSheet` in the style of Share location's permission step: "Share your location with @places_bot?" in `titleLargeEmphasized`, the location badge on `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided` in `primaryContainer`, what goes where in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` (to this bot, with what is typed to it, to no one else; asked again after Pingogram restarts), `TextButton` "Not now" at the start and a default `Button` "Share location" at the end (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()`), which asks Android's location permission first where Pingogram has none. "Not now", the scrim, dragging and back query without the location. The answer holds per bot for the process |
| Bot chat | A private chat with a bot. While it has no messages: in the sheet's middle, centred and scrolling where short, a service-style card like the secret chat's (`surfaceContainerHighest`, `extraLarge`, at most `sizes.bubbleMaxWidth`): the bot's photo, or its animation's still (GIFs play nowhere inline yet), edge to edge in the card's top corners at its aspect ratio up to `sizes.mediaMaxHeight`, dimmed in dark theme as the conversation's media (§10); then "What can this bot do?" in `titleSmallEmphasized` and the bot's description (td_api.tl `botInfo.description`) in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` as user content; one item for screen readers. Without a description or picture, the empty state. A default `Button` "Start" (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()`, `labelLargeEmphasized`) takes the composer's place, centred in its bar as "Join" does, disabled while Telegram is asked; it shows too when a start link (`t.me/bot?start=…`) opened the chat, with messages or not, and only its tap starts the bot (`sendBotStartMessage`, with the link's parameter): never by itself, also for links that ask to start at once |
| Send-as | Where TDLib lets the user choose who they post as (`chat.message_sender_id`): the current sender's avatar (`sizes.avatarExtraSmall`) in a small `IconButton` (`IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`, 48 dp target) at the start of the composer, before the attach button; not while editing or recording. It opens a `ModalBottomSheet`: `titleLargeEmphasized` "Send messages as", the senders as selectable `ListItem`s inset like the chat list's rows (avatar, name, "Your account" / "Channel" / "Anonymous admin"), the chosen one `large` in `secondaryContainer`; one that needs Telegram Premium disabled with "Needs Telegram Premium", never an offer. A tapped sender shows chosen at once with a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` under the title until TDLib takes it; loading is a `DelayedLoadingIndicator`, errors are snackbars |
| Emoji and sticker panel | The field's emoji button (a smiley; a keyboard while the panel shows) puts the panel in the keyboard's place, at the keyboard's last height (`sizes.inputPanelHeight` above the navigation bar before it showed, at most half the window), on the composer's `surface`; switching to and from the keyboard moves nothing, and back closes it. Above: a `LazyRow` of `IconToggleButton`s (`toggleableShapes()`, checked `secondaryContainer` / `onSecondaryContainer`), one per section, as tabs that jump the grid and follow it: Material Symbols for emoji categories, recent and favorites, a set's first sticker for a set. The grid: `LazyVerticalGrid` with `titleSmallEmphasized` section headers in `onSurfaceVariant`; emoji in cells of at least `sizes.panelEmoji` (48 dp), about 28 dp tall whatever the font scale, tap with a `KeyboardTap` haptic; stickers in cells of at least `sizes.panelSticker` (72 dp) with a `large` ripple, tap sends with `Confirm`; a set not loaded yet shows tonal `large` placeholders in `surfaceContainerHigh`. Below: Emoji / Stickers / GIFs as connected `ToggleButton`s (`ButtonGroupDefaults.connected*ButtonShapes()`, unchecked `surfaceContainerHighest`, selected label `labelLargeEmphasized`) centred, the backspace `IconButton` at the end on the emoji tab; screen readers hear them, and the section buttons, as selected tabs. Stickers: `DelayedLoadingIndicator`, the empty state (`Cookie9Sided`) and the error with "Try again" (`Cookie4Sided`, `errorContainer`). GIFs: where Telegram names a GIF search bot, the search bar's `InputField` ("Search GIFs", on its container, inset `spacing.small`) over the saved GIFs, or while it holds a query what the bot found; while it has the focus the keyboard comes up under the panel, which grows to keep the field and a row of results above it. GIFs in square cells of at least `sizes.sharedMediaCell`, `extraSmall` corners, `sizes.mediaAlbumGap` apart, their still picture over the blurred minithumbnail, dimmed in dark theme as the conversation's media (§10), a "GIF" `mediaOverlay` label (stills: no GIF plays inline yet); a tap sends with `Confirm`, a long press on a saved one opens an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` with "Remove from saved GIFs" in `error`, also a custom action. The next page loads 6 from the end with a `LoadingIndicator` below. No saved GIFs: an `EmptyState` (`Cookie9Sided`); no results: `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`; errors `Cookie4Sided` on `errorContainer` with "Try again". While editing, emoji only |
| Attach menu | `ModalBottomSheet` + `ButtonGroup` of options; Gallery opens the system Photo Picker (no recent-photos carousel: it would need media permissions, `REQUIREMENTS.md` §7) |
| Attach preview | The picked items in a `ModalBottomSheet` (title "3 photos" in `titleLargeEmphasized`): photos and videos as a row of square thumbnails, files as rows; then the composer's field for a caption and its round send button. Under photos and videos from the gallery, a switch row (Material's `ListItem` on the sheet, `Role.Switch`, `Switch` with a check in its thumb, `ToggleOn` / `ToggleOff` haptics): "Send without compression" over what it does in the supporting style (as files, at their size, without location or camera details), on at first where Settings → Data and storage → "Send photos in original quality" says so. A photo longer than Telegram takes as a photo (more than 20 times as long as wide) goes as a file either way |
| Share location | `ModalBottomSheet` from the attach menu's "Location": `titleLargeEmphasized` title, steps crossfading on effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`. Before Android asks: a `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided` badge (`primaryContainer`, the location icon), why in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, `TextButton` "Not now" at the start and a default `Button` "Continue" ("Open settings" once Android no longer asks) at the end. Searching: `DelayedLoadingIndicator`. Found: the badge, the coordinates in `titleMedium` (`tnum`), "Accurate to 12 m" in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, one for screen readers; while it improves an indeterminate `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` with "Improving accuracy…"; an approximate location says so with a `TextButton` "Use precise location"; `TextButton` "Open in maps" (only where an app handles `geo:`) and the composer's round send button (a long press for silent and scheduled). Not found: a `Cookie4Sided` badge, `titleMedium` + `bodyMedium`, "Try again". No map (it would need a map service, `REQUIREMENTS.md` §6.2). The location off is a snackbar with "Settings", never a dialog |
| Message actions | Long-press lifts the message (a copy of its bubble, where it was) over a `Scrim`, with the conversation behind it blurred by `sizes.messageMenuBlur` (`RenderEffect`, API 31+; the scrim alone below 31), so the message stays in view as the actions open (`MessageActionsMenu`, `feature:chat`). The quick reactions sit above it in a pill of the menu's container color (`MenuDefaults.groupStandardContainerColor`, its shadow), a `ToggleButton` row with shape morph that scrolls sideways, ending in a medium `FilledIconButton` that opens the reactions picker in a `ModalBottomSheet` over the menu (leaving it returns to the menu). An expressive menu sits below it: `DropdownMenuGroup`s with `MenuDefaults.groupShape` / `itemShape`, the everyday actions, then reporting and deleting in `error` in a second group. The groups sit in the overlay itself, not in a `DropdownMenuPopup` window, so the message, its reactions and its menu move as one and predictive back reaches them. Reactions and menu align with the bubble's side, grow from it on `fastSpatialSpec` as the scrim and blur fade in on `defaultEffectsSpec`; if they do not fit around the message in the safe area, all of it moves into view on `defaultSpatialSpec` (the long press's `LongPress` haptic accompanies it); a menu too tall scrolls. A second long-press selects text in the lifted message (`SelectionContainer`, the platform's text toolbar copies); where the message can be replied to, the toolbar offers "Quote" after Copy (`appendTextContextMenuComponents`), which replies quoting the selected part of the message's text (td_api.tl `inputTextQuote`: with its bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, spoiler and custom emoji, at most 1,024 characters), closes the menu and focuses the composer. The actions add "Reply in another chat" (`chat_paste_go`, mirrored in RTL) after "Reply" where TDLib allows it, which opens a `ModalBottomSheet` built from the forward sheet's parts ("Reply in another chat" in `titleLargeEmphasized`, "Replying to: …" one line in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, the search field and the chats where the user can write, Saved Messages first, no secret chats); one tap opens that chat with the reply in its composer, nothing sent. "Copy link" (`ic_link`) follows "Copy" in supergroups and channels ("Copy link to this thread" in a topic's or thread's conversation), copying TDLib's link, an album's for an album's item; Android 13+ confirms the copy itself, earlier versions get "Link copied". Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24): "Copy link to this thread" is the owner's wording; it copies the message's link inside its topic or thread (td_api.tl `getMessageLink.in_message_thread`), not a link to the thread itself. Both are also the bubble's custom actions for screen readers, beside Reply and Copy. A tap on the scrim, a reaction or an action fades it out (the message returning to its place on `fastSpatialSpec`) before its event; back is predictive, scaling it to 90%. With TalkBack (touch exploration), or in windows shorter than `sizes.messageMenuMinWindowHeight` (480 dp, phones in landscape), the same items show in a `ModalBottomSheet` |
| Seen by and reactions | A group message's actions (the floating menu and its sheet) start with "Seen by" (Material Symbols `done_all`) where TDLib lists who saw it (td_api.tl `messageProperties.can_get_viewers`: Telegram keeps read dates for a while, in groups up to a size; Pingogram adds no rule of its own) and "Reactions" (`favorite`, outlined) where it lists who reacted (`messageReactions.can_get_added_reactions`) and the message has reactions other than the paid one, in the first group after the quick reactions, as Telegram's apps place them. Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24): the rows are "Seen by" and "Reactions" without counts, since a count would ask Telegram before the user opens the list; and the placement and icons above. Either row opens one `ModalBottomSheet` as the conversation's other sheets: the title "Seen by" on the viewers' tab, "Reactions" on the others, in `titleLargeEmphasized`; where there is more than one tab, the folder tabs' and shared media's connected `ToggleButton`s in a fading horizontal scroll (not a `PrimaryScrollableTabRow`, so the app keeps one tab style, however many kinds of reaction): "Seen 4", "All 6", then each reaction with its count (the emoji, or the custom emoji's picture, in the label's style, tabular figures), `Role.Tab` with "Seen by 4 people" / "❤, 4 reactions". Rows as `ListItem`s inset like the chat list's rows: the 40 dp avatar (`sizes.avatarSmall`), the name as user content, "Seen at 9:41 AM" / "Seen yesterday at 9:41 AM" / "Seen Mar 17 at 9:41 AM" (and "Reacted …") in the supporting style, worded as the conversation's times, the latest first; on "All" the reaction at the end in `titleLarge`; one item for screen readers ("Ada Lovelace, reacted ❤, Reacted at 9:41 AM"). A tap opens the profile (`onOpenProfile`), or the info page of a channel someone reacted as, and closes the sheet. Reactions page as the end comes near, a `LoadingIndicator` below the rows, a failed page its error with a "Try again" `TextButton`; the first page a `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; nobody to show a `Cookie9Sided` badge in `primaryContainer` (the tab's symbol), `titleMediumEmphasized` and `bodyMedium`; a failure `Cookie4Sided` in `errorContainer` with why ("Telegram no longer shows who saw this message") and a default `Button` "Try again"; crossfading on the effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`. Nothing is asked of Telegram before the sheet opens, and then only for the tabs the user selects. **Removing reactions** (`REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-C6), where TDLib lets the user (td_api.tl `messageProperties.can_delete_reactions`: a group's administrators): on the reactions' tabs, never "Seen", each row ends in a more-options `IconButton` (`IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`, "Options for Ada Lovelace") opening an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` of "Remove reaction" and "Remove all recent reactions", both in `error` with the delete symbol; each asks with an `AlertDialog` (destructive, the delete symbol): "Remove Ada Lovelace's reaction?" (it goes from this message for everyone) or "Remove all of Ada Lovelace's recent reactions?" (every reaction they added in this chat lately), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers, "Cancel" and "Remove" in `error`. Done, their rows leave the sheet and a snackbar in the sheet says so; a refusal is a snackbar there. The question survives process death. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-10): the options sit on each row rather than a long press, so screen readers and touch find them alike; "recent" is Telegram's own limit (td_api.tl `deleteAllRecentMessageReactionsFromSender`), which Pingogram does not widen |
| Swipe to reply | A bubble follows a horizontal swipe toward the end (mirrored in RTL) in the layout phase, revealing a reply icon in a `surfaceContainerHighest` circle (`onSurfaceVariant`, the small icon button's sizes) drawn behind it, growing with the swipe; past `sizes.swipeReplyDistance` (64 dp) a `TextHandleMove` haptic and the icon pops to full size on `fastSpatialSpec`, and releasing there replies (the actions' "Reply"); the bubble springs back on `defaultSpatialSpec`. Only where the user can reply to a sent message, not in selection mode; service messages and sponsored cards have none. It does not exclude the system back gesture's edges. Screen readers reply through the bubble's "Reply" custom action |
| Share button | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24, `docs/audit/feature-gaps-octogram.md` P2): beside a channel's posts, and beside posts forwarded from a channel in any chat, where the chat lets messages be forwarded (no protected content, not a secret chat), once sent: a small `FilledIconButton` (40 dp, `IconButtonDefaults.smallContainerSize()`, in its 48 dp touch target, `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()` press morph) in `surfaceContainerHighest` / `onSurfaceVariant`, as the swipe-to-reply circle, with the forward symbol (`ic_forward`), on the bubble's far side (after an incoming post, before an own one; mirrored in RTL) `spacing.small` from it, its circle level with the bubble's bottom, above the reactions. The bubble keeps its width and place: the button takes the room a bubble leaves (at most 80% of the row), and the lifted copy of the message shows no button. A tap opens the forward sheet for the post (an album's every item), as the actions' "Forward". No tooltip: one per post would cost every channel bubble a popup's state. Screen readers hear the bubble as one and forward through its "Forward" custom action; the keyboard reaches the button after the bubble. Not in selection mode |
| Next unread channel | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24, `docs/audit/feature-gaps-octogram.md` P2): in a channel with no unread posts, once the list shows its newest post, dragging further up pulls the list up by half the finger's movement (in its graphics layer) and reveals, below it in the composer's place, the next channel of the main chat list with unread posts, in the list's order: a pill on `surfaceContainerHigh` (full shape, at most `sizes.bubbleMaxWidth`) with the channel's avatar (`avatarExtraSmall`) inside a determinate `CircularWavyProgressIndicator` (`avatarRow`) that fills as the pull nears `sizes.nextChatPullDistance` (96 dp), its name in `titleSmallEmphasized` (user content, one line) over "Pull up to open" in `bodySmall` `onSurfaceVariant`, which turns "Release to open" past it, with a `TextHandleMove` haptic. The pill rises with the pull and fades in with its progress, both in its graphics layer. Released past the threshold, the channel opens in this one's place (back returns to the chat list, or the detail pane shows it); released before, the list settles back on `defaultSpatialSpec`. Dragging down again shrinks the pull first. Not in topics, threads, selection mode or search, nor while older or newer pages are missing below. Screen readers hear nothing of the pill and open the channel through the message list's custom action "Open the next unread channel, Android Developers" |
| Forward sheet | `ModalBottomSheet`, from the conversation and from the media viewer's Forward (over the viewer, whose snackbar then shows the result): title, the search bar's `InputField` on its container, a keyed list of chats (Saved Messages first) as toggleable `ListItem`s inset like chat list rows, a picked one `large` in `secondaryContainer` with its avatar morphed into a check (§4); once a chat is picked, `Switch` rows (hide sender's name, hide captions), the composer's field for a comment and a default-size `Button` with the `shapes()` press morph ("Send", `labelLargeEmphasized`) grow in on the default spatial spring |
| Chats shown first | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-25, `docs/audit/feature-gaps-octogram.md` P2): Settings → Appearance → Chats → "Chats shown first" (where they show as the summary), a settings page: "Add a chat" (`ic_add` in the group's tonal circle) while fewer than 5 are chosen, then each chat (40 dp avatar, the title as user content, two lines at most) with a "Remove" `TextButton` named with it, in the order the sheets show them; screen readers move a chat with "Move up" / "Move down" custom actions (no drag: five rows rarely need it); a footer says where they show and that they stay on this device. Changes save at once, a refusal a snackbar. "Add a chat" opens the privacy exceptions' picker sheet (`PickerParts.kt`): "Choose up to 2 chats" (the places left), the search field, the main list's chats not chosen yet as toggleable rows, "Done" adding them at the end, as dismissing does. In the forward and share sheets they follow Saved Messages, then the chat list as before |
| Share sheet | `REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-N2 (direct share): what another app shared, or a `t.me/share` link. A `ModalBottomSheet` built from the forward sheet's parts: `titleLargeEmphasized` "Send to", below it what is shared (the start of the text on one line as user content, "3 photos and videos" / "2 files", "· 2 chats selected") in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`; the search bar's `InputField`; the chats as the forward sheet's toggleable rows, Saved Messages first. Once a chat is picked the bottom grows in on `defaultSpatialSpec`: one chat, why in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` and a default `Button` "Open chat" (`labelLargeEmphasized`, `shapes()`), which opens the chat with the share in its composer; several, the composer's field for a comment and a default `Button` "Send" (a `Confirm` haptic), the two crossfading on the effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`. Results of sending to several chats are snackbars of the app shell. Nothing is sent without that tap |
| Sponsored message | Channels and bots (`REQUIREMENTS.md` §2): a filled `Card` (default `medium` shape and colors) among the messages, start-aligned and as wide as a bubble can be, never hidden or restyled: the sponsor's circular photo (where TDLib gives one), the label, the sponsor's name and an info `IconButton` (`IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`) on top, the text with its entities, the media (`small` corners inside the card), and the advertiser's `FilledTonalButton` (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()` press morph) at full card width. Below the newest message (`messagesBetween` 0: below every message; otherwise after that many new messages), only where the history reaches the newest message. Viewed once wholly on screen (read from the list's layout); its button, a link in its text or its media open it. The info button opens "About this ad", a `ModalBottomSheet`: what sponsored messages are, the advertiser and more about the ad (selectable `ListItem`s), "Report ad" in `error` with TDLib's reasons as a second page (crossfade on effects springs, `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`, a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it reports), and an informational note on Telegram Premium, never an offer or a link. Screen readers hear label, sponsor and text as one, "About this ad" as a custom action |
| Reactions picker | In a `ModalBottomSheet` (with TalkBack: in the actions sheet, in place of its actions; content crossfades on effects springs, the sheet resizes on `defaultSpatialSpec`): the message's own reactions as a `HorizontalUncontainedCarousel` of reaction chips (emoji over count; the user's own on `secondaryContainer` with a `primary` outline along the carousel's mask, `maskBorder`, as the badges' ring), then every reaction the user can add (TDLib's top, recent, popular) as a lazy grid of the same `ToggleButton`s under `titleSmallEmphasized` section headers; a bubble's "+N" opens the sheet there. Custom emoji show their sticker's still picture, or a placeholder |
| Chat list row | `ListItem` (leading 48 dp circular avatar, title, one line of preview with the sender inline, trailing time + badge): Material's two-line item, 72 dp, every row the same height. From 150% font scale the time starts the preview's line ("9:41 AM · …") and the trailing column keeps only the message state and the badges, so the title keeps most of its line. The preview stays one text: the list item reads its baselines while measuring to tell one line from several. Inset in its sheet, `large` when selected (§4). The pinned chats (owner decision 2026-11-06) come first as one group under "Pinned" (`titleSmallEmphasized` in `primary`, lined up with the rows' content, a heading): segmented rows (`ListItemDefaults.segmentedShapes`: `large` corners at the group's ends, `extraSmall` between, `SegmentedGap` apart) on `surfaceContainer`, two tones above the sheet's `surface` (owner decision 2026-11-07: one tone was barely visible in dark theme and vanished with AMOLED, which moves the ladder down a tone, §2.1), inset `spacing.small` as every row; swipes, selection and the long press work as on any row, and a selected or open one takes the selected colors and `large` shape. The group is the list's own rows, no subcomposition. Swipe `SwipeToDismissBox`: by default read/unread at the start, archive at the end (a full swipe triggers it); Appearance's Swipe actions picks each side's action (read, archive, pin, mute, delete, nothing), the row turning it into unarchive, unpin or unmute where those apply and not swiping a side without an action; behind the row archive on `tertiaryContainer`, delete on `errorContainer` (it asks first), the others on `secondaryContainer`. Every action stays in the selection toolbar and as screen-reader actions. With "Swipe to change folders" rows do not swipe: the whole list does (§6 "Swipe actions") |
| Story rings | Material has no stories component; built from its parts as the floating navigation bar is (§6.1). The chat list's first row on the Chats tab (`StoryRingsRow`, `feature:chats`), above the archive row, scrolling away with the chats (one lazy item, so the list's scroll cost stays), only while the main stories list (td_api.tl `storyListMain`) has chats and Appearance's "Show stories" is on (owner decision to confirm, 2026-10-24: a "Chat list" group of its own above "Chats", a switch row (`SwitchItem`) whose summary says nothing loads while it is off, beside Swipe actions and "Edit folders button"): a keyed `LazyRow` (`animateItem` on the motion scheme's springs, `spacing.extraSmall` apart, `spacing.small` padding), each item `sizes.avatarMedium` + `spacing.large` wide with `large` ripple bounds: the chat's 48 dp circular avatar (`sizes.avatarRow`) in a concentric circle of `sizes.avatarRing`, a ring's width away, `primary` while a story is unseen and `outlineVariant` once all are seen (a circle, as avatars are, rather than a `MaterialShapes` ring, which read as noise around round avatars), drawn in the draw phase; the name (the user's own "Your story") under it in `labelSmall` `onSurface`, one line (two from 150% font, as search's top people), ellipsized, as user content. One button for screen readers: "Ada, 3 stories, not seen". No ring on the chat rows' avatars: that would cost the perf-critical row a lookup and a draw for every chat. **Posting** (board 10c, §13.11): while the user's stories are being posted, their own ring is a determinate `CircularWavyProgressIndicator` (`primary` on `surfaceContainerHighest`, indeterminate while TDLib does not know the sizes) around their avatar at `sizes.avatarSmall`, a cancel badge (`ic_close` on `surfaceContainerHighest`, ringed in `surface`), and "Posting 62%" in `labelSmallEmphasized` `primary` under it; once TDLib gave up, a `sizes.avatarRing` ring in `error`, an error badge on `errorContainer` and "Not posted" in `error`; screen readers hear "Your story, Posting 62%". A tap opens a `ModalBottomSheet`: "Posting 2 stories" (or "Your story wasn't posted") in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading), a determinate `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while sending, "62% sent" (or "Waiting to send") and what stopping does in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, why TDLib gave up or why stopping failed in `error` (a polite live region), then "Stop posting" (or "Remove") as an `error` `TextButton` at the start and, where the user has stories already, "View your story" as a `FilledTonalButton` at the end, both with `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`; the sheet closes by itself once nothing is posting |
| Story viewer | `StoryViewerRoute`, full screen above everything (no navigation), as the media viewer: the theme's `scrim`, dark theme in either mode, light system bar icons. A `HorizontalPager` of the stories list's chats (plain slide), each page one column centred in the safe area: the story in a 9:16 frame with `large` corners on `surfaceContainer`, as large as fits above the bottom bar, and the bar below it, as wide as the frame.
