Inside the frame, the picture fills it (Coil at the frame's size over the blurred minithumbnail; a video on Media3's `PlayerSurface` with its thumbnail until the first frame), a `DelayedLoadingIndicator` until the file is on the device. At the top on a `mediaOverlay` gradient: one segment per story (`sizes.pinnedIndicator` tall, `sizes.mediaAlbumGap` apart, round ends, `onMediaOverlay` on its 38% track, the current one filling in the draw phase: 5 s for a photo, the video's length), then the chat's avatar (`avatarSmall`), its name in `titleSmallEmphasized` over the story's age ("3h", the language's narrow units) in `bodySmall`, one heading, and the mute (videos with sound) and close `IconButton`s (`IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`) in `onMediaOverlay`. At the bottom the caption with its formatting (`bodyMedium`, three lines until tapped) on a `mediaOverlay` gradient. Taps: the start third (mirrored right to left) the previous story, elsewhere the next; holding pauses; a volume key gives a muted video its sound back (and changes the volume); a swipe down or predictive back (scaling to 90%) closes; the last story of the last chat closes. Content it cannot show (a live stream, unsupported content, a deleted story) an `EmptyState` on `Cookie9Sided` for a photo's time; a failure `Cookie4Sided` with "Try again". The bar (`sizes.composerField` + `spacing.small` above and below, also on pages beside the current one so they line up): for a user's story that may be replied to, the rounded tonal field ("Reply privately…", `extraLarge`, `tonalColors()`, user content) with a send `FilledIconButton` once it holds text, which pauses the story while it has the keyboard; and the reaction `IconButton` (heart outline, or the chosen emoji in `titleLarge`) opening a pill of `IconToggleButton`s (`toggleableShapes()`, checked `secondaryContainer`) on `surfaceContainerHigh` over the frame's bottom, growing in on `defaultSpatialSpec` with effects-spring fades, toggle haptics, back closing it first. Screen readers: the frame is one polite live region, "Story 2 of 5 by Ada", its click the next story, "Previous story" and "Pause" / "Resume" its custom actions |
| Story albums | Boards 11a and 11b (td_api.tl `storyAlbum`). **On a profile**: a section of its own, `StoryAlbumsSection` of `feature:media`, which shows nothing while the albums load, when the chat has none or when they fail: "Albums · 4" in `titleSmallEmphasized` `primary` (a heading) over the covers two to a row, each a 6:5 picture with `largeIncreased` corners (the album's photo, or its video's still, over the blurred minithumbnail, dimmed in dark theme as the conversation's media, §10; `surfaceContainerHighest` without one) over its name in `titleSmall` (user content, two lines at most), the tile one button for screen readers ("Hampi trip, story album", "Open album"). **Opened** (`StoryAlbumRoute`, pushed above the profile, shared axis X): a small `TopAppBar` with back and the album's name in `titleLargeEmphasized` (one line, a heading) on `surfaceContainer`, then on a `ContentSheet` "Neha Kulkarni · 9 stories" in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` over a keyed `LazyVerticalGrid` of 9:16 tiles (`GridCells.Adaptive(sizes.sharedMediaCell)`, three across a phone, `spacing.small` apart, `large` corners), each the story's picture with its day ("Oct 3", the year before this one) in `labelSmallEmphasized` `onMediaOverlay` on a `mediaOverlay` pill at its bottom start; one button for screen readers ("Story from Oct 3", "Video story from Oct 3", "Play story"). A tap plays the album in the stories viewer from that story, the album alone (no other chats). The next 30 load 6 tiles before the end, a `LoadingIndicator` below them, a failed page its error in `error` with a "Try again" `TextButton`. Loading a `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; an empty album an `EmptyState` (`Cookie9Sided`, the stories symbol); a failure `Cookie4Sided` in `errorContainer` with "Try again". Albums are only read: Pingogram creates and edits none yet |
| Live story | Board 12 (td_api.tl `storyContentLive`). **In the stories viewer**: the frame shows an `EmptyState` on `Cookie9Sided` in `secondaryContainer` with the live symbol, "Live now" over that it is watched on the voice chat screen and that others may see the user joined, and a default `Button` "Watch live" (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()`), which takes its own taps and opens the voice chat screen on the poster's call (`VoiceChats.watchLiveStory`); the story moves on after a photo's time as others do. **Watching** (`GroupCallActivity`, `joinLiveStory`, muted): the stream full width on `scrim` (an RTMP live story's stream, else the poster's camera, the first video Telegram sends) under a header on a `mediaOverlay` gradient: the poster's avatar (`sizes.avatarSmall`), their name in `titleSmallEmphasized` over where the stream is ("1,204 watching · 4:05", `bodySmall`, a polite live region), all in `onMediaOverlay` and one heading, a "LIVE" pill in `labelMediumEmphasized` `onError` on `error`, and close ("Leave live story"), which leaves. Below it, where Telegram offers comments, the voice chat's comments pane (§6 "Voice chat comments", paid comments shown with their Stars, the field saying where comments cost Stars that Pingogram can't pay) in the lower share of the height; without comments the stream takes it all. Picture-in-picture shows the stream as for live streams. Nothing is paid: no reactions with Stars, no "Donate" |
| Profile music | Boards 13a and 13b (td_api.tl `getUserProfileAudios`). **On a profile** (`ProfileMusicSection` of `feature:media`, through a slot), below the header and above the actions, only for a user with songs (and on the user's own profile): a card on the rows' `surface` with `extraLarge` corners, `spacing.medium` inside: the shown song's album cover (`sizes.avatarRow`, `medium` corners, its picture as story album covers draw theirs, or the music symbol on `tertiaryContainer` without one), a button that opens the playlist ("Open playlist"); its title in `titleSmallEmphasized` (user content, one line, a heading) over "Lena Fischer · 1 of 3" in `bodySmall` `onSurfaceVariant`; play or pause as a `FilledIconButton` and, with more than one song, "Next song" as a `FilledTonalIconButton` (Pingogram's skip-next symbol), both `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`; then the position and the length in `labelSmall` with tabular figures either side of an expressive `Slider` ("Playback position") whose state follows the song while untouched, a release seeking. Songs stream from TDLib, one after another, only while the profile is on screen. A song that cannot be played says so in `bodySmall` `error`. The user's own profile without songs: "Profile music" in `titleSmallEmphasized` `primary` (a heading) over what it is, and "Add music" (a `FilledTonalButton` with the add symbol, a `LoadingIndicator` in its place while a song is added). **Playlist** (board 13b): a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded: "Profile music" in `titleLargeEmphasized` over "4 songs" ("4 songs · anyone who sees your profile can play them" on the user's own) in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, one heading, and on the user's own "Add music" at the end; why the last change failed in `error`, a polite live region; then the songs in a keyed `LazyColumn`, each a row with `large` corners: the cover (`sizes.avatarSmall`), the title in `titleSmall` (user content; the shown one in `primary`, after the equalizer symbol while it plays, "Playing" for screen readers) over "artist · 3:40" in `bodySmall` `onSurfaceVariant`; a tap plays it ("Play Nachtfahrt", selected while shown). On the user's own profile each ends in an options `IconButton` ("Options for Nachtfahrt") opening an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup`: "Move up" and "Move down" where they apply, then "Remove" in `error` in a second group, also the row's custom actions; a change shows at once and comes back when Telegram refuses it. "Add music" opens the system file picker for audio (no permission); a song's length, title and artist come from its file, read on the device |
| New story | `docs/adr/0036-posting-stories.md`, board 10a. `NewStoryRoute`, full screen (no navigation), on `surface`, at most `contentMaxWidth` wide and centred. Android's photo picker (photos and videos, at most 10 in all) opens by itself as the screen first shows; cancelling it with nothing picked closes the screen. A small `TopAppBar`: close at the start, "New story" in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading), and "4 selected" in `labelLargeEmphasized` with tabular figures on a `secondaryContainer` pill at the end. Then the shown item as large as the room allows, cropped to fill, with `extraLarge` corners on `surfaceContainerHighest` (a `LoadingIndicator` while the first is still being added): its number in `labelLargeEmphasized` `onPrimary` on a `primary` circle (`sizes.avatarExtraSmall`) at its top start, a video's length on a `mediaOverlay` pill at its bottom start, and "Remove from story" (`FilledTonalIconButton`, the delete symbol, `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`) at its top end. "In this story" (`titleSmallEmphasized` `primary`, a heading) with "Drag to reorder" (`bodySmall` `onSurfaceVariant`) at the end while there are two or more; then the items as a keyed `LazyRow` of 3:4 tiles `sizes.panelSticker` wide with `large` corners, each its number on a `sizes.avatarBadge` `primary` circle and a video's length, the shown one ringed in `primary` (`sizes.avatarRing`); a tap shows a tile; a long press (`LongPress` haptic) lifts it to drag into its place, following the finger in its graphics layer while the others slide on `defaultSpatialSpec`, a `SegmentTick` at each place it passes; items being added show a `LoadingIndicator` tile; "Add more" (the add symbol over `labelMedium`, an `outline` border, `large` corners) while the post takes more. Under them the lock symbol and "Picked with Android's photo picker. Pingogram can only see these 4 items." in `bodySmall` `onSurfaceVariant`. At the bottom, inside the navigation bar's inset: "Camera" (`FilledTonalButton` with the camera symbol, the camera app, where the device has a camera) and "Next" (a filled `Button` taking the rest of the row), both at Material's medium height with `ButtonDefaults.shapesFor`, "Next" enabled once every item is added. From 150% font the tiles show neither a video's length nor "Add more"'s label (named for screen readers), and the badges grow with their numbers. Screen readers hear each tile as one tab ("Video 3 of 4, 0:14", selected or not) with "Move earlier", "Move later" and "Remove from story" as its custom actions. Errors (a file that cannot be read or is too large, one that is no photo or video, no picker or camera app) are snackbars. Nothing needs a permission |
| New story: composing | Board 10b, the second step of `NewStoryRoute` after "Next", which back leaves for picking again with the picks kept. On the theme's `scrim`: the picked items full bleed in a `HorizontalPager`, cropped to fill (a video shows its still and length), and over their top inside the status bar's inset a segment per item (`sizes.pinnedIndicator`, round ends, `sizes.mediaAlbumGap` apart, `onMediaOverlay`, the others at 38%, decorative) and "Back to picking", a `FilledIconButton` in the media overlay's colors with `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`. Below, on a `surface` sheet with `extraLarge` top corners, at most `contentMaxWidth`, above the keyboard: the caption's formatting bar, Bold, Italic, Link and Spoiler as connected `ToggleButton`s with their symbols, checked where the selection has them, `ToggleOn` / `ToggleOff`, "Link" asking for its address in an `AlertDialog` ("Apply", "Remove" on a link, "Cancel"); the caption in the rounded tonal `TextField` (`large` corners, "Caption" its label, `bodyLarge` as user content), its formatting drawn as messages show it, a spoiler on `tertiaryContainer`; who the story is for, an `AssistChip` on `secondaryContainer` with the audience's symbol and a down arrow ("Who can see it: Close friends" for screen readers) that opens an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` of Everyone (`ic_public`), Contacts, Close friends (`ic_favorite_filled`) and Selected people (`ic_group`, the chip then "3 people"), the chosen one with a check and selected for screen readers, Selected people opening its picker (§6 "New story: who sees it"); "Stays for 24 h", a label with the schedule symbol on a `surfaceContainerHigh` pill; then what the post reaches, "Shown to 14 close friends · 1 of 4 items" in `bodySmall` `onSurfaceVariant` (a polite live region), or why Telegram does not let the user post now in `error` ("You reached this week's limit of stories. | Telegram links | No screen of their own (`docs/adr/0013-deep-links.md`): a link opens its target's screen with that screen's transition. Then "Double tap": "On others' messages" and "On your messages" as switch rows, a footer saying a second double tap takes the reaction back and a single tap still acts at once. In the conversation a double tap on a bubble, where nothing inside takes the taps, adds the quick reaction to its message or takes it back, as tapping its badge does (`ToggleOn` / `ToggleOff` haptics, the badge's crossfade). The first tap acts at once (showing or hiding a grouped bubble's time), never waiting for a second; a double tap then toggles the time back, so only the reaction stays. Not on messages still sending or that failed, not in selection mode; never the paid reaction. Screen readers react through the message's actions, as before |
| Swipe actions | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24): Settings → Appearance → Chat list → "Swipe actions" (`docs/audit/feature-gaps-octogram.md`, P2), a row with the two actions as its summary ("Right: Read · Left: Archive", the sides swapped in right-to-left layouts), opening a settings page without row icons: "Swipe right" and "Swipe left" (the swipe toward the end first, so "Swipe left" first right to left), each a radio group (`RadioItem` in a `selectableGroup`, `SegmentTick` per pick) of Mark as read or unread, Archive or unarchive, Pin or unpin, Mute or unmute, Delete, Nothing, saved at once, a refusal a snackbar; the footer says screen readers offer every action whatever the swipes do and that deleting asks first. Above them a "Folders" group: "Swipe to change folders" as a switch row (`SwitchItem`), off by default, its footer saying which way shows which tab; while it is on the two radio groups are disabled (kept for when it is off) and Appearance's summary reads "Change folders". In the chat list with it on, rows do not swipe; a horizontal drag anywhere on the list (Chats tab, with folder tabs, not in selection mode or the archive) moves it like a pager (owner decision 2026-11-07): the list follows the finger and the folder's list beside it comes in with it, side by side, both in their graphics layers, its chats already there (the first page of the tabs beside the selected one is loaded ahead, only those), a `TextHandleMove` haptic at a third of the width; released past it (or flung so it would land there) both settle on `defaultSpatialSpec` (owner decision 2026-11-11, §5.1), the neighbour in the list's place, and its tab is selected (swiping toward the end shows the previous tab, mirrored right to left) without a frame between; otherwise both spring back on `defaultSpatialSpec`. Toward a side without a tab it follows a fifth of the finger. Screen readers and keyboards change folders with the tabs |
| Chat appearance | Settings → Appearance → Chat appearance (`REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-S1), a settings page (`SettingsGroup`s, no row icons): first a segment with a live preview of a conversation in the theme's chat style (`MaterialTheme.chatStyle`: two incoming messages of one sender joined as a group and an outgoing reply, on the wallpaper; one description for screen readers); then "Messages": "Message text size" over its percentage and "Bubble corners" over how round they are, each a `SettingsSegment` with a nested list item and an expressive `Slider` through its steps (`SegmentTick` per step): text 85–130% in 5% steps, corners the shape scale's `medium`, `large`, `largeIncreased`, `extraLarge` (the default) and `extraLargeIncreased`; then "Wallpaper": the wallpapers as chips like the Theme page's (`sizes.themeChip` circles filled with the wallpaper, "No wallpaper" on `surfaceContainerHighest` with a close symbol, the selected one morphing to `Cookie9Sided` with a check), one radio group for screen readers. Wallpapers come from the current theme's roles only (`ChatWallpaper.brush`, `core:ui`): `surfaceContainerLow`, a 16% tint of `primary`, `secondary` or `tertiary` over `surface`, or a top-to-bottom gradient between two of those; never an image. Appearance shows a "Chat appearance" row under "Chats" with the three in its summary |
| Chips (filters, mentions) | `FilterChip`, `AssistChip`, `SuggestionChip` |
| Archive row | The main list's first row after the story rings (one lazy item), as a chat row: the archive on the neutral avatar placeholder (`surfaceContainerHighest`), "Archived chats" (`titleMediumEmphasized` while unread) over the names of the first archived chats, the unread chats in a muted `Badge`. Three styles, kept on the device (`ArchiveRowStyle`): **full**; **compact**, Material's one-line `ListItem` (56 dp), the archive symbol in `onSurfaceVariant` centred where the avatar was (so the title lines up with the chats'), the title and the badge; **hidden**, the top bar's "Archived chats" button in its place. A long press (the list item's `LongPress` haptic) opens an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` ("Make compact" / "Show chat names" with `unfold_less` / `unfold_more`, "Hide from chat list" with `visibility_off`), the row `large` in `secondaryContainer` while it is open; a swipe either way past the threshold hides it, revealing "Hide" on `secondaryContainer` with `large` corners as the chats' swipes do (`TextHandleMove` at the threshold, `Confirm` as it hides). Hiding shows a snackbar "Archived chats hidden. Open them from the top bar." with "Undo". Screen readers get the styles as custom actions. Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24): a hidden row comes back from the archive's settings or "Undo", and the archive stays reachable from the top bar's button; Telegram's apps instead reveal it by pulling the list down past its top, which Material's stretch overscroll does not offer |
| Archive settings | `ArchiveSettingsRoute`, a settings-style page pushed above the archive from its top bar (shared axis X): the medium flexible top bar "Archive settings" (`TwoRowsTopAppBar`, as `SettingsTopBar`), the page on `surfaceContainerHigh` (true black with AMOLED), segmented groups under `titleSmallEmphasized` `primary` headers with `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` footers. "New chats": "Archive and mute new chats" (from non-contacts) as a switch row (`Role.Switch`, `CheckSwitch`), disabled where Telegram does not offer it (TDLib's option `can_archive_and_mute_new_chats_from_unknown_users`; a switch that is on can still be turned off), the footer then saying so. "When a new message arrives": "Keep unmuted chats archived" and "Keep chats from folders archived", the latter disabled and shown on while the first is on, which keeps them already. Each switch is sent at once (`ToggleOn` / `ToggleOff`) and flips back with a snackbar when Telegram refuses it. "In the chat list": the archive row's style as radio rows (Full, Compact, Hidden; `SegmentTick`), kept on the device. Telegram's groups load with a `DelayedLoadingIndicator` in a segment, or say why they failed with a "Try again" `TextButton`; the row's style shows in every state |
| Settings rows | Groups of `SegmentedListItem`s (`SettingsGroup`, `feature:settings`) with `ListItemDefaults.segmentedShapes` / `segmentedColors` / `SegmentedGap` (§2.2, §4), outlined leading icons, the label in the list item's `bodyLarge`; a `titleSmallEmphasized` header in `primary` above each group on settings pages, none on Settings home (its single-row groups would only repeat their row), groups `spacing.medium` apart there and `spacing.extraLarge` with headers. Clickable rows use the clickable list item. Switches use the clickable one with `Role.Switch` semantics and a trailing `Switch` showing a check icon in its thumb when on (`thumbContent`). Single choices use the selectable one with a leading `RadioButton`, or, where every label fits, a connected button group in a row of its own: `ToggleButton`s with `ButtonGroupDefaults.connectedLeading/Middle/TrailingButtonShapes()` (segmented buttons are deprecated in Material 3 Expressive). Buttons and progress sit in a segment of the same container and shape (`SettingsSegment`); text fields sit directly on the page (rounded tonal style). Leading icons sit in a tonal circle, one `*Container` role per group. Every row of a page has an icon, or none does. A row that acts on its item ends in a `TextButton` naming the action ("Unblock" in Blocked users, its name in the screen reader's label), not an icon. User content in rows (names, usernames, chat titles, device and app names) takes its own text direction, phone numbers left to right (§9). **Settings home** has the tabs' large header (`TabHeader`, as Chats and Contacts; owner decision 2026-11-07), "Settings" in `displaySmallEmphasized` collapsing to the dense bar, also as the list pane of the list-detail on expanded windows. **Pages**: the medium flexible top bar (`SettingsTopBar`: `TwoRowsTopAppBar` with `MediumFlexibleTopAppBar`'s heights, whose title slot says which row it fills, so the title takes §3.3's styles without reading the bar's text styles), collapsing with `exitUntilCollapsedScrollBehavior`, its width capped with the content; the content inside the window margin (`windowMargin()`, §7). **Expanded windows**: list-detail (§7), with Settings home as the list, its page rows selectable and the open page's selected (the list item's selected colors and shape); the page in the detail pane without a back button (system back closes it), and the pages it opens (Exceptions, Blocked users, 2-step verification, licenses…) in the detail pane's own back stack, on the shared axis X; with no page open, an `EmptyState` (`Cookie9Sided`, settings icon) on the page's color |
| Chat folders | Settings → Chat folders (`REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-L3), from Settings home's General group, a settings page (segmented groups in a keyed `LazyColumn`): "New folder" (add symbol) as a row of its own; "Your folders" with each folder's icon (its Material Symbol in the group's tonal circle; Telegram's icons without one show the folder symbol), name (user content) over what it includes ("Groups, 3 chats", "Shared" first, joined by the language's list format) and a drag handle (Pingogram's six-dot symbol, 48 dp) at the end. Dragging the handle, or a long press anywhere on the row, lifts it into the list item's dragged shape (`LongPress` haptic), the row following the finger in its graphics layer and the rows it passes sliding into its place on `defaultSpatialSpec` with a `SegmentTick` each; the order goes to Telegram as the finger lifts. Screen readers move a folder with "Move up" and "Move down" custom actions. A tap opens the editor. Then "Suggested folders": Telegram's suggestions with their description and an "Add" `TextButton` (a `LoadingIndicator` while it is created). The editor, pushed above it: the name in the rounded tonal `TextField` with "4 / 12" below it (or "Give the folder a name." in `error`); "Icon": "Automatic" as a radio row, then the symbols as `IconToggleButton`s (`toggleableShapes()`, checked `secondaryContainer` / `onSecondaryContainer`) wrapping in a segment, radio buttons for screen readers; "Included chats" with "Add chats" and "Excluded chats" with "Exclude chats" (visibility-off symbol), each followed by its kinds of chats and its chats (40 dp avatars) with a remove `IconButton` naming them, a footer saying what the side does ("Add at least one chat or kind of chat." in `error` after a refused "Save"); a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers, then "Delete folder" (an `error` `TextButton`, behind an `AlertDialog`) at the start and a default `Button` "Save" / "Create" at the end, `Confirm` and `Reject` haptics. "Add chats" and "Exclude chats" open a `ModalBottomSheet`: the title in `titleLargeEmphasized`, the search bar's `InputField`, "Kinds of chats" as checkbox rows, "Chats" as toggleable rows inset like the chat list's, a picked one `large` in `secondaryContainer` with its avatar turned into a check; a chat picked for one side leaves the other; "Done" |
| Shared folder sheet | A shared folder's link (`t.me/addlist/…`, `ChatFolderInviteRoute`, a dialog destination like the join sheet): a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded and scrolls, at most `contentMaxWidth`: the folder's icon on a `Cookie9Sided` badge in `primaryContainer`, "Add the folder “Work”?" ("New chats in “Work”", "You have “Work” already") in `titleLargeEmphasized`, what adding does in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, centred; "2 chats to join" (`titleSmallEmphasized` `primary`) over the chats the user is not in as toggleable rows inset like the chat list's, all picked at first, a picked one `large` in `secondaryContainer` with its avatar turned into a check (§4), "Group" / "Channel" as supporting text; "Chats you are in" as plain rows; why adding failed in `error` (a polite live region), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it adds; `TextButton` "Cancel" at the start and a default `Button` "Add folder" / "Join chats" at the end (disabled with nothing picked; "Close" alone when nothing is new). Steps crossfade on the effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`: loading a `DelayedLoadingIndicator`, an invalid link `Cookie4Sided` on `errorContainer` with "Close", and "Try again" where trying again may help. Nothing is joined before that tap |
| Stickers | Settings → Stickers (`REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-C5), from Settings home's General group (`ic_mood`), a settings page (segmented groups in a keyed `LazyColumn`): first, owner decision (2026-11-08), a search field (`PickerSearchField`, the search bar's `InputField` on its container, at the rows' width; "Search sticker sets" / "Search emoji sets"), which while it holds words replaces the page's rows with what it found: "Your sets" (the user's own whose title or name has the words, matched on the device) and "On Telegram" (Telegram's catalog, `searchStickerSets`, asked 300 ms after the last key, the newest words cancelling the older search; sets already among "Your sets" left out), each as the trending page's rows (a tap opens the set's sheet; "Add", a `LoadingIndicator` while it adds, then a `primary` check), a `DelayedLoadingIndicator` while Telegram is asked, its failure in `error` with a "Try again" `TextButton`, or "No sets found" (`bodyLarge` `onSurfaceVariant`, a polite live region), and a footer that the words are sent to Telegram; then "Trending stickers" (Pingogram's rising-line symbol, "2 new sets" or what it holds), "Archived stickers" (`ic_archive`, "3 sets") and "Emoji" (`ic_mood`, the user's custom emoji sets) as page rows; then "Your sticker sets": each set's cover (its first sticker, still, `sizes.leadingIconContainer`, the emoji until the picture shows; no circle: stickers are artwork on their own), its title (user content) over "24 stickers", and the folders' drag handle at the end, with the same drag (handle or long press, the dragged shape, `SegmentTick`s, "Move up" / "Move down" for screen readers), the order going to Telegram as the finger lifts; a footer says the panel shows them in this order on every device. A tap opens an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup`: "Show stickers" (the set's sheet), "Share link" (Android's share sheet, `t.me/addstickers/…`), "Archive"; "Remove" in `error` in a second group, behind an `AlertDialog`; the same as custom actions. Archived or removed, a set leaves the list at once with a snackbar and "Undo". Trending stickers and Archived stickers: settings pages of the same rows, a tap opening the set's sheet, with an "Add" `TextButton` at the end (named with the set), a `LoadingIndicator` while it is added and a `primary` check once the user has it; a footer says what the page holds; empty an `EmptyState` (`Cookie9Sided`). Loading `DelayedLoadingIndicator`, errors `SettingsError` with "Try again". **Emoji** (owner decision to confirm, 2026-10-25): the same page for the user's custom emoji sets (td_api.tl `stickerTypeCustomEmoji`, which Telegram lists apart), titled "Emoji": "Trending emoji" and "Archived emoji", then "Your emoji sets" with the same rows ("40 emoji"), drag, menu ("Show emoji", Share link `t.me/addemoji/…`, Archive, Remove) and "Undo"; its footer says the order follows the account and that Pingogram shows custom emoji in messages but offers none to send yet, information rather than an offer. Its Trending and Archived pages and a set's sheet ("Add emoji") are the stickers'. **Settings** (owner decision 2026-11-08, the stickers' page only, after its page rows, under "Settings"): "Suggest stickers by emoji" in a `SettingsSegment` over what the choice does, All sets / My sets (the default) / Off as `ChoiceRow`'s connected buttons; and "Loop animated stickers" as a switch row (`SwitchItem`, on by default; off, an animated sticker plays once where it shows, then rests on its first frame). While the composer's field holds one emoji alone (not while editing or recording), its stickers show above the composer where the "/" suggestions show (`StickerSuggestionsArea`): a row of the panel's sticker cells (`sizes.panelSticker`, `large` ripple) on `surfaceContainer` with `large` corners, inset `spacing.small`, scrolling sideways, growing in on `defaultSpatialSpec` and shrinking away on `fastSpatialSpec`, a pane ("Sticker suggestions") for screen readers; a tap sends the sticker (`Confirm` haptic) in the emoji's place. "My sets" suggests the installed sets' stickers (td_api.tl `getStickers`, its favorite, recent and trending ones left out), "All sets" adds public sets' (`searchStickers`) |
| New sticker set | The owner's boards 21a–c (td_api.tl `createNewStickerSet`). Settings → Stickers ends its page rows with "New sticker set" (`ic_add`, "Make a set of your own from your photos"), which pushes `NewStickerSetRoute`, a settings page whose steps crossfade on the effects springs (`SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`), back returning a step. **Name** (21a): "Step 1 of 2" and what the set needs in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`; the title ("Name", user content, at most 64 characters) and the link ("Link", after the prefix "t.me/addstickers/", left to right, ASCII keyboard) in rounded tonal `TextField`s on the page; the link follows Telegram's suggestion for the title (`getSuggestedStickerSetName`, 500 ms after the last key) until the user types one, and is checked on the device (a letter, then letters, digits and single underscores, 5 to 64 characters) then by Telegram (`checkStickerSetName`, 400 ms after the last key): "Checking…" in `onSurfaceVariant`, "officecats_kl is available" after a check in `primary`, "Another set has this link" or "Telegram doesn't take this link" after the error symbol in `error`, in `labelLargeEmphasized`, a polite live region, over the rules in `bodySmall`; a default `Button` "Next" at the end, enabled once the link is free. **Stickers** (21b): the title as the top bar's, "Step 2 of 2 · 5 stickers" and how to change them; three tiles to a row, square, with `extraLarge` corners on the rows' container: each sticker fitted with its emoji on a `surfaceContainerHighest` badge (`sizes.avatarExtraSmall`) at its bottom end, a `LoadingIndicator` for a photo being made into a sticker, and "Add sticker" (the add symbol over `labelMediumEmphasized` in an `outline` border) while fewer than 120; a tap on a sticker opens its emoji sheet, a long press (`LongPress`) an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` of "Move earlier", "Move later" and "Remove" in `error` in a group of its own, the same as screen readers' custom actions on "Sticker 3, emoji 😂"; a footer that a photo becomes a sticker as it is; why Telegram refused the set in `error`, a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it publishes, and "Publish set" at the end. "Add sticker" opens Android's photo picker (images only, no permission); the photo is written on the device as a WebP whose longer side is 512 px, transparency kept, and the emoji sheet opens for it (a smiling face until chosen, as Telegram requires one). The emoji sheet is a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded: "Emoji for sticker 3" in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading), then Unicode's emoji under their groups' names (`titleSmallEmphasized` `onSurfaceVariant`, headings) in 48 dp cells (`sizes.panelEmoji`, `headlineSmall`), a tap choosing. **Published** (21c): a check on a `MaterialShapes.Sunny` badge in `primaryContainer` (`sizes.avatarLarge`), "Office cats is live" in `headlineSmallEmphasized` (a heading), "5 stickers · t.me/addstickers/officecats_kl" in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, the first five stickers on a pill of the rows' container, then "Share link" (a default `Button` with the share symbol, Android's share sheet), "Open set" (a `FilledTonalButton`, the set's sheet) and "Done" (a `TextButton`), with the `Confirm` haptic as it publishes. The step, the texts and the stickers survive process death. Owner decisions (2026-11-11, the recommended choices): see §13.12 |
| Make a sticker | The owner's boards 14a and 14b (td_api.tl `getOwnedStickerSets`, `addStickerToSet`). The media viewer's More menu offers "Make a sticker" (`ic_mood`) after "Copy" for a photo whose chat lets its content leave it; it opens `MakeStickerRoute` in the viewer's place, full screen (no navigation), closing back to the conversation. **The editor** (14a): a small `TopAppBar` with close and "Make a sticker" in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading), "Next" a default `Button` (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()`, `labelLargeEmphasized`, a `LoadingIndicator` in its label's place while the sticker is written) at its end, enabled once the photo is on the device. Then, at most `contentMaxWidth` inside the window margin: the sticker's square with `extraLarge` corners over a checkered ground of `surfaceContainerLow` and `surfaceContainerHighest` (`spacing.large` squares), which stands for transparency; in it the photo fills the square, clipped to corners of 12% of its side, dragged to move and pinched to zoom (1 to 5 times, never past its edges), held in the draw phase while the fingers are down; the white outline (3% of the side) around it while on; the text layers in `displaySmallEmphasized` white over a near-black edge (`StickerInk`, the same in every theme: a sticker goes to others) and the emoji layers in `displayLarge`, each dragged to its place, the one last touched framed in `primary` (`avatarRing`, `small` corners), a frame the sticker leaves out. Until the photo is there, its download's determinate `CircularWavyProgressIndicator` (a delayed indicator before TDLib knows the size), or why it failed with "Try again". Under it the tools as a connected group sharing the width (`ButtonGroupDefaults` connected shapes, `ConnectedSpaceBetween` apart): "Add text" (the "Aa" glyph) and "Add emoji" (`ic_mood`) `FilledTonalButton`s, disabled at 10 layers, and "Outline" (`ic_edit`) a `ToggleButton`, checked while on; from 150% font the labels take the icons' room and two lines where they need them, the three sharing one height. Under the tools, centred in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, how to crop and place, or for the layer touched, that it drags and "Remove" (an `error` `TextButton` naming it). "Add text" asks in a `BasicAlertDialog` (the alert dialog's shape and colors, "Add text" in `titleLargeEmphasized`, a filled one-line `TextField` of at most 40 characters, "Cancel" / "Add"); "Add emoji" opens a `ModalBottomSheet` of Unicode's emoji under their groups' names in 48 dp cells, as Settings' sticker sets' emoji sheet. Screen readers hear the photo with "Zoom in" and "Zoom out" as custom actions, and each layer ("Text: Monday mood") with "Remove". "Next" writes the drawing as a 512 px WebP on the device and opens **the sheet** (14b): a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded and scrolls, at most `contentMaxWidth`, its steps crossfading on the effects springs (`SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`). Choosing: the sticker (`avatarLarge`, `largeIncreased` corners on `surfaceContainerHigh`) beside "Add to sticker set" in `titleLargeEmphasized` over "Pick an emoji so it shows up in suggestions" in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` (one heading); the emoji as one radio group of medium `IconToggleButton`s (`toggleableShapes()`, round, morphing to a square when chosen, chosen in `primary` / `onPrimary`), five quick ones and "More emoji" (`ic_add`), which opens the emoji sheet; the user's own sets as a radio group of segmented rows (`ListItemDefaults.segmentedShapes` on `surfaceContainerHigh`): the set's cover (`StickerCover`, `avatarSmall`), its title (user content) over "12 stickers", a `RadioButton` at the end, then "New sticker set" (the add symbol in a `surfaceContainerHighest` circle) over "Name it on the next step"; the first set chosen, or the new set where there is none; a delayed indicator while they load, a failure with "Try again"; then "Add to Office cats" (or "Next"), a default `Button` across the sheet at Material's medium height with `shapesFor`, why Telegram refused it in `error` above it (a polite live region) and a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it answers. A new set: "New sticker set" in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading), that the set is public at its link, its name and link in filled `TextField`s (the link after "t.me/addstickers/", left to right), the link Telegram's suggestion for the name until typed, checked 500 ms after the last change ("Checking…" in `onSurfaceVariant`, "Available" in `primary`, taken or malformed in `error`, `labelLargeEmphasized`, a polite live region), "Create set" and a "Back" `TextButton`. Done (`Confirm` haptic): a check on a `MaterialShapes.Sunny` badge (`headlineBadge`) in `primaryContainer`, "Added to Office cats" in `headlineSmallEmphasized` (a heading) over the set's link, then "Share link" (`FilledTonalButton`, Android's share sheet) and "Done" (a default `Button`) sharing the row. The scrim, dragging and back close the sheet and show the editor again, except while Telegram answers. Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): see §13.11 |
| Sticker set sheet | A sticker set (`StickerSetRoute`): what a `t.me/addstickers/…` link and a set's row in Settings → Stickers open (an installed set's "Show stickers", a trending or archived set's tap), a dialog destination like the join sheet. A `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded, at most `contentMaxWidth`: the title (`titleLargeEmphasized`, user content) over "24 stickers" (`bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`), one heading, centred; the stickers in a `LazyVerticalGrid` of `sizes.panelSticker` cells (the sticker panel's), animated ones playing within the app's limit, each an image for screen readers ("🐝 Sticker"), not tappable; then why a change failed in `error` (a polite live region), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers, "Share" (`TextButton`, the share sheet) at the start and a default `Button` "Add stickers" or an `error` `TextButton` "Remove" at the end, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`. Steps crossfade on the effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`: loading a `DelayedLoadingIndicator`, a set that does not exist or failed a `Cookie4Sided` badge on `errorContainer` with "Close" (and "Try again" where it may help). Nothing changes before a tap |
| Two-step verification | A settings page (`SettingsGroup`s, no row icons): "Password" (Change password, "Turn off" in `error` behind an `AlertDialog`) or "Set password", the recovery email (or the address waiting for its code: "Enter the code", "Don't use this address"), and a requested reset ("Cancel reset", "Reset password now" in `error` once Telegram's waiting period is over). Each flow is a settings page of its own: what the step asks in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, the rounded tonal fields on the page (passwords in `SecureTextField`), the field's error below it with a `Reject` haptic, a `FLOOD_WAIT` countdown in `error` beside a determinate `CircularWavyProgressIndicator`, a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it asks Telegram, then a `TextButton` (Forgot password?, Skip, Resend code) at the start and a default `Button` at the end, both with `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`. Steps crossfade on the effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`; back returns a step. Other errors are snackbars; what a flow did is a snackbar on the page |
| Change number | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-23): from Edit profile, whose last group "Phone number" is one row with the number (`bodyLarge`, left to right, formatted) and a "Change" `TextButton` at its end, named with the number for screen readers. `ChangePhoneNumberRoute`, a settings page as 2-step verification's flows, one step at a time (crossfading on the effects springs, `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`; back returns a step): the current number and what changes (account, chats and contacts stay; a code goes to the new number) in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, then a default `Button` "Change number"; the new number with sign-in's country field (opening its picker) and calling code and number fields (live formatting, left to right) in the rounded tonal style, the field's error below it ("Another Telegram account already uses this number"); the code as sign-in shows it: where Telegram sent it, a code field in the verification code style (`headlineSmall`, `tnum`, the SMS code autofill hint) submitting itself once complete, the resend countdown with its `CountdownIndicator` and then the next way's `TextButton`, "Wrong number?" at the start and "Next" at the end; a flood wait's countdown and a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers, `Reject` haptics for a refused number or code. Done (`Confirm`): a `Cookie9Sided` badge with a check, "Number changed" in `titleLargeEmphasized`, the new number, and "Done" |
| Privacy exceptions | A privacy setting's page shows "Exceptions" for its choice: "Always allowed" unless the choice is Everybody and "Never allowed" unless it is Nobody, each over its count ("2 users and members of 1 group") or "Add users or groups", opening a settings page titled by the side: the setting's name as the group's header, "Add users or groups" first (`ic_add` in the group's tonal circle), then each person or group (its 40 dp avatar, the name as user content, "Group" below a group) with a "Remove" `TextButton` named with it, and what the side means as the footer. Changes are saved at once, the rows waiting meanwhile; a refusal is a snackbar that brings them back. "Add users or groups" opens a `ModalBottomSheet` built from the chat folder picker's parts: `titleLargeEmphasized` title, the search bar's `InputField` ("Search chats"), the people and groups of the chat list as toggleable rows inset like the chat list's (a picked one `large` in `secondaryContainer`, its avatar turned into a check), and "Done", which saves, as dismissing does; one picked here leaves the other side |
| Who can message me | Privacy and security's privacy group ends with "Messages" (its choice as the summary), which opens a settings page: "Who can message me" over "Everybody" and "My contacts and Telegram Premium users" as radio rows (`RadioItem` in a `selectableGroup`), saved at once, the rows waiting while Telegram answers, a refusal a snackbar that brings the previous choice back. Where Telegram does not let the account choose the second (td_api.tl option `can_set_new_chat_privacy_settings`), it is disabled with "Telegram lets only Telegram Premium subscribers choose this" as its summary: information, never an offer or a link. The group's footer says what it means, and a price for non-contacts' messages another app set ("Non-contacts pay 25 Telegram Stars…"), which Pingogram neither offers nor changes. The Last seen page adds "Read time": "Show my read time" as a switch row (`SwitchItem`), saved at once, its footer saying who always sees it and that hiding it hides other people's too without Telegram Premium |
| Birthday (Edit profile) | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24): a "Birthday" group after the bio, as the phone number's: the date in `bodyLarge` ("December 10, 1815", or "December 10" without its year, the language's pattern; "No birthday" in `onSurfaceVariant`) with an "Add" / "Change" `TextButton` at its end, named with the date for screen readers; then, with a birthday, "Who can see it" over the privacy setting's choice, which opens that setting's page. Saved with the page's "Save", as the other fields. The dialog: a `DatePicker` in a `DatePickerDialog`, no day after today, its title row "Birthday" with "Show the year" and a `CheckSwitch` at the end (one toggleable, `ToggleOn` / `ToggleOff`; below the picker nothing fits), the headline without the year while it is off, and "Remove" (with a birthday), "Cancel" and "Done" `TextButton`s |
| Login email and auto-delete | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-23): rows of Privacy and security's last group, "Account", before "Delete account": "Login email" (`ic_alternate_email`, Telegram's pattern of the address in its summary; only where the account has one, as Telegram lets change it only then) and "Auto-delete messages" (`ic_timer`, the timer in its summary). Login email: a settings page as Change number's, one step at a time: the address and what it is for in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` with a default `Button` "Change email"; the new address in the rounded tonal `TextField` (the email autofill hint, left to right), its error below it with a `Reject` haptic; the code Telegram sent in the verification code style (`headlineSmall`, `tnum`, the email one-time code autofill hint), submitting itself once complete, "Send the code again" `TextButton` at the start and "Next" at the end; a flood wait's countdown and a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers. Done (`Confirm`): a `Cookie9Sided` badge with a check, "Login email changed", the new pattern, "Done". Auto-delete: a settings page with "Delete messages in new chats after" as radio rows (`RadioItem` in a `selectableGroup`: Off, 1 day, 1 week, 1 month, and a timer another app set), saved at once (the rows waiting while Telegram answers), a refusal a snackbar that brings the previous timer back; what the timer does as the group's footer. Loading `DelayedLoadingIndicator`, errors `SettingsError` with "Try again" |
| Delete account | Settings → Privacy and security → "Delete account" (an "Account" group last, the period in its summary), a settings page: "Delete my account if away for" as radio rows (`RadioItem` in a `selectableGroup`: 1, 3, 6, 12, 18 and 24 months, and a period another app set, in days), saved at once, a refusal a snackbar, with what it means as the group's footer; then "Delete now" (`titleSmallEmphasized` `primary`): what is lost, what stays and that the number can sign up again, in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` on the page; an optional reason in the rounded tonal `TextField` (user content, up to four lines); with 2-step verification on, why, the password's hint and a `SecureTextField` (its error below it, a polite live region, with a `Reject` haptic); the `FLOOD_WAIT` countdown and a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it deletes, as 2-step verification's flows; then "Delete account" as a default `Button` (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()`, `labelLargeEmphasized`) at the end in `error` / `onError`, as "Hang up" is, disabled until the password is typed. It asks with an `AlertDialog` (destructive, the delete symbol); once deleted (`Confirm` haptic) the logout returns the app to Welcome |
| Color themes | Settings → Appearance → Theme (`docs/adr/0016-color-themes.md`), wallpaper colors and basic colors side by side; Appearance's "Theme" row opens it, the theme's name as its summary and its chip (`leadingIconContainer`, 40 dp) at its end. A settings page: first a live preview in a `SettingsSegment` (the theme's name in `titleSmallEmphasized` `primary`, a chat list row with its avatar, name, preview, time and unread `Badge`, an incoming bubble on `surfaceContainerHigh` and an outgoing one on `primaryContainer` in `extraLarge` corners, a default `Button`; samples as user content, one description for screen readers); then "Wallpaper colors" (Android 12+) and "Basic colors" (Pingogram first) as chips in segments, wrapping to the width (`FlowRow`, `spacing.medium` apart): `sizes.themeChip` (56 dp) circles split into the scheme's `primary` (top half), `secondary` (bottom start) and `tertiary` (bottom end), in the light or dark scheme the app shows, drawn in the draw phase. The chosen chip morphs into `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided` on `defaultSpatialSpec` (snapping with reduced motion) with a check on its `primaryContainer`. Each group is a radio group for screen readers, every chip named ("Teal", "Wallpaper colors"); a pick ticks (`SegmentTick`) and the whole app, this page included, cross-fades to it at once. On expanded windows the page sits in the settings list-detail's detail pane (§7) |
| Settings account card | A single `SegmentedListItem` (`extraLarge` at rest): avatar in a `primary` ring with an edit badge, name, phone, username, a "Premium" status label (`tertiaryContainer`, Telegram Premium only, never an offer), and a `FilledTonalIconButton` chevron; the whole card opens Edit profile and is one item for screen readers |
| Frozen account | While Telegram keeps the account frozen (td_api.tl `updateFreezeState`): above Settings home's account card, the settings banner card (`SettingsBannerCard`, as Notifications' "Notifications are off"): `errorContainer` / `onErrorContainer` with `extraLarge` corners, the lock on a `MaterialShapes.Flower` badge in `onErrorContainer`, "Your account is frozen" in `titleMediumEmphasized` over what the account still can do and when Telegram deletes it (the date in the language's long form; left out where Telegram gives none) in `bodyMedium`, and "Appeal" as a filled `Button` in the inverse colors, which opens Telegram's appeal link as any link in the app; a polite live region. It leaves as TDLib lifts the freeze. Owner decision (2026-11-11, the recommended choice): the notice is Settings home's only, not a bar over every screen, since the account still reads its chats; the composer's refusals stay TDLib's own errors |
| Demo | The demo for the app stores' reviewers (`docs/adr/0034-demo-mode-for-store-review.md`, owner decisions 2026-11-01). **Entry**, hidden from everyday users: on Welcome, pressing and holding the logo's shape for about a second (1 s, held without lifting; a `LongPress` haptic) opens an `AlertDialog`: "Open the demo?", "It shows sample chats stored on this phone, without a Telegram account.", `TextButton`s "Cancel" and "Open" (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()`). Nothing on screen points to it, and screen readers get no action for it: it is for reviewers, whose store instructions name the steps; the logo stays decorative to them. **While it shows**: a banner under the status bar, above every screen of the app (owner, 2026-11-01: one banner, not a label per top bar), "Demo · Sample chats on this device. Not connected to Telegram." in `labelLarge` with "Demo" in `labelLargeEmphasized`'s weight, `onTertiaryContainer` on `tertiaryContainer`, `spacing.medium` from the sides and `spacing.small` above and below, two lines at most, a heading for screen readers; the screens lay out below it (it takes the status bar's inset). Settings' account card shows a "Demo" label (`secondaryContainer`, the account label's style) in the phone number's place; the other accounts, "Add account", the Settings tab's account menu and "Keep connected" are left out. **Calls** are scripted (owner, 2026-11-01): the call buttons and voice chats work as in a signed-in session, with the real call screens, and connect to nothing; the call button always opens its menu (`CallButtonMenu`), which adds "Receive a demo call" (Material Symbols `call_received`) after "Voice call" and "Video call", the sample person calling a moment later; screen readers get it as a custom action too. **Leaving**: "Leave demo" in "Log out"'s place, a destructive row with the same icon, "Deletes what you did in it and goes back to the start", behind a destructive `AlertDialog` ("Leave the demo?", "Leave"); the app opens again at Welcome. What only Telegram's servers can answer, and the demo has no sample for, says "This needs a Telegram account. The demo can't do it." where the screen shows errors (the places are listed in the ADR). Permanent: never removed, the logo never moved and the gesture never changed without the owner's decision and the store instructions updated in the same pull request |
| Account switcher | `docs/adr/0031-multiple-accounts.md` (owner decision 2026-10-25: as Telegram's apps), shown only once the app offers several accounts (`DeviceAccountsRepository.isAvailable`). **Settings home**: below the account card, a group of segmented rows (`OtherAccountsGroup`, `feature:settings`): each other account its 40 dp avatar where the rows' icons sit, its name as user content (two lines at most) over its phone number (`numericContent`, masked with "Hide phone numbers"), its unread chats in a `primary` / `onPrimary` `Badge` at the end; one item for screen readers that "Switch to Ada Lovelace"; then "Add account" (`ic_person_add` in the group's tonal circle) while fewer than the limit are signed in (3, a fourth with Telegram Premium; never an offer). A tap switches: the app opens again on that account, in a new task. **The Settings tab**: a long press (`LongPress` haptic) on the floating navigation bar's Settings tab or the rail's Settings item opens an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` anchored to it (`AccountMenuAnchor`, `:app`): the accounts in one `DropdownMenuGroup` (`MenuDefaults.groupShape` / `itemShape`), each its `avatarExtraSmall` avatar, its name (user content, one line) and at the end a check (`ic_check`, "Current account") on the shown one or the others' unread chats in the same `Badge`; "Add account" in a second group. The tab's tap selects it as before; screen readers get "Switch to …" and "Add account" as the tab's custom actions. **Adding an account** opens the sign-in at phone number entry, without the welcome screen; back there, the system's or the top bar's, leaves it for the account shown before |
| Buttons | M3 Expressive sizes: `ButtonDefaults.ExtraSmall…ExtraLarge` container heights. The default (40 dp) for actions in forms and flows: auth puts its primary action at the bottom end and the secondary as a `TextButton` at the bottom start, within thumb's reach. Medium (56 dp) and up only for hero moments (Welcome's "Start messaging"), never full width on wide windows. `FilledTonalButton` for secondary, `OutlinedButton` rare, `TextButton` in dialogs; every button with `ButtonDefaults.shapes()` press morph |
| Text fields | Forms (auth): filled `TextField`s, passwords `SecureTextField`, the country or region a read-only field ("Country/region", never "country" alone: the list holds regions, Huawei AppGallery review guideline 4.8; "calling code", not "country code", for the "+44") with `ExposedDropdownMenuDefaults.TrailingIcon` that opens the picker ("Choose a country or region", its search ("Search countries and regions") a `SearchBarDefaults.InputField`, the current country or region selected: the list item's selected container, `large`, rows inset like the chat list's, scrolled into view); codes autofill as SMS or email one-time codes. Auth banners (offline, flood wait) lead with an icon, the flood wait's a clock in its countdown's `CircularWavyProgressIndicator`. Composer and Edit profile: the rounded tonal field (`TextFieldDefaults.roundedShape` + `tonalColors()`) on the page, no enclosing segment. One style per region |
| Icon buttons | `IconButton`/`FilledIconButton` with Expressive shapes (`IconButtonDefaults.*Shape`) and sizes; toggleable ones use `IconToggleButton` with shape morph |
| Progress | `LoadingIndicator`, `ContainedLoadingIndicator`, `LinearWavyProgressIndicator`, `CircularWavyProgressIndicator` |
| Dialogs | `AlertDialog` for destructive confirmations only; `BasicAlertDialog` for custom |
| Snackbars | `SnackbarHost` with M3 `Snackbar`; errors are never dialogs |
| Notification permission request (chat list, Android 13+) | Filled `Card` on `surfaceContainerHigh` at the top of the list's sheet, inset `spacing.small` like the rows: `ic_notifications` in `primary`, `titleMedium` title, `bodyMedium` text in `onSurfaceVariant`, `TextButton` "Not now" and `Button` "Allow" / "Open settings" (both with `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`); grows in and shrinks away (`expandVertically` on `defaultSpatialSpec`, fades on the effects springs). Never a dialog |
| Notification settings | A settings page (`SettingsGroup`s, no row icons): a warning row in `errorContainer` / `onErrorContainer` first while Android blocks Pingogram's notifications; then a group per kind of chat with switches (notifications, previews, mentions for groups, pinned messages for groups and channels; owner decision to confirm (2026-10-25): in private chats, after the previews, "New stories" in a `SettingsSegment` over what the choice does, then Automatic (Telegram's default: the 5 people the user writes to most), All and None through `ChoiceRow`, and while any notify "Name who posted" as a switch row), "Sound and vibration" (Android's channel settings; "Turned off…" in `error` when the channel is blocked) and "Exceptions"; "Reactions", a row of its own opening a settings page (no row icons): "Reactions to my messages", "Reactions to my stories" and "Votes in my polls" as radio groups (Everyone, My contacts, Nobody), then "Show the reaction" as a switch row, with a footer that the settings follow the account to its other apps and that Pingogram does not show reaction notifications yet (TDLib does not pass them on); "Keep connected" (foss) with its battery cost in the summary; "Reset all notification settings" in the error role, behind an `AlertDialog`. "Reactions" (a page row) and "Contact joined Telegram" (a switch row, the account's `disable_contact_registered_notifications` option) share a group after the kinds of chats. With more than one account on the device, an "Accounts" group before them holds "Notifications from all accounts" as a switch row (`SwitchItem`, on by default), its summary saying that off only the shown account notifies and calls still ring (`docs/adr/0031-multiple-accounts.md`); "Sound and vibration" opens the shown account's channels. Exceptions: a list like Blocked users (segmented rows, avatar, state, a remove `IconButton`), a row opens a `ModalBottomSheet` of mute options as `ListItem`s with outlined icons |
| Automatic download (Data and storage) | A group with a `PageItem` per network (Mobile data, Wi-Fi, Roaming; outlined icons) whose summary lists what downloads by itself, a Data Saver note as its footer. Each opens a settings page (no row icons): switches for photos, videos and GIFs, files, voice and video messages; videos and files each followed by a `SettingsSegment` with its limit ("Up to 10 MB") in a nested list item and an expressive `Slider` through fixed steps (`SegmentTick` haptic per step), disabled while its switch is off; a Data Saver row on top while it holds the network back. In conversations, a photo left for a tap shows an `IconButton` (download icon in `onMediaOverlay` on a `mediaOverlay` circle, like the upload ring) over its blurred minithumbnail, an indeterminate `CircularWavyProgressIndicator` around a cancel button while it downloads. While the settings differ from the defaults, "Reset automatic download" (`ic_refresh`, what it does as its summary) closes the group and puts every network's defaults back at once, with a snackbar |
| Use less data for calls | Data and storage, a "Calls" group after "Network" (`docs/audit/feature-gaps-octogram.md` P3): one segment, "Use less data for calls" (`ic_call` in the group's tonal circle) over what it does (the video sent at most 360p; voice unchanged) as a nested list item, then Never / Mobile data / Always through `ChoiceRow` (connected `ToggleButton`s where the labels fit, radio rows otherwise; `SegmentTick`), saved at once, a refusal a snackbar. NTgCalls offers no audio setting, so it changes only the video a call sends |
| Power saving | Settings → Power saving (Settings home's General group, after Data and storage, `ic_energy_savings_leaf`), a settings page (no row icons): "Turn on" as radio rows in a `selectableGroup` (Off, With Battery Saver, its summary saying whether Battery Saver is on now, Always; the default With Battery Saver), with what it is for as the group's footer; then "While on" as switch rows (`SwitchItem`), one per part, all on by default: "Stop animated stickers and emoji" (their first frame), "Stop autoplay" (videos and GIFs in the media viewer wait for a play button: a `FilledIconButton` at `largeContainerSize` on a `mediaOverlay` circle, `onMediaOverlay` play symbol, centred over the thumbnail), "Stop call screen animations" (the ringing avatar's turn, the speaking rings' fades and a voice chat's floating reactions); a footer says Android's "Remove animations" works on its own too. Changes save at once, a refusal a snackbar. Reduced motion stays the system's (§5): power saving adds to it, never replaces it |
| Proxies | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24, `docs/adr/0027-proxies.md`): Data and storage's "Network" group holds "Proxy" (`ic_vpn_lock`, the proxy in use or "Off · Connect directly" as its summary). The Proxy page, a settings page without row icons: a first group of "Use proxy" (a switch row, while there are proxies), "Add proxy" and "Check again"; without proxies its footer says what they are for. Then "Your proxies" as radio rows in a `selectableGroup` (the one in use selected: `secondaryContainer`, `large`): the name (user content) or the address (`server:port`, left to right) over the protocol, the address when named, and how it answered ("Available · 84 ms", "Unavailable", "Checking…"), with a more-options `IconButton` ("Options for Home") opening an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup`: Edit; Delete in `error` in a second group, behind an `AlertDialog`; a footer says what a proxy's owner can see. The form (New proxy, Edit proxy): "Protocol" as connected `ToggleButton`s (SOCKS5, HTTP, MTProto; `SegmentTick`), then the rounded tonal fields on the page: Server and Port (left to right), Username and Password (`SecureTextField`) or the Secret (`SecureTextField`), and an optional Name, each wrong field's error below it as a polite live region; HTTP's "HTTP requests only" as a switch row; how "Check" went in `bodyMedium` (`error` when unavailable), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it saves, `TextButton` "Check" at the start and a default `Button` "Save and connect" / "Save" at the end (`Confirm` once saved, `Reject` on a refusal) |
| Network usage | Data and storage's "Network" group, below "Proxy": "Network usage" (`ic_data_usage`). A settings page without row icons: the networks (Mobile data, Wi-Fi, Roaming) as connected `ToggleButton`s in a segment (`SegmentTick`), the one the device uses first; then "Since Oct 1, 2026" (the date TDLib counts from) over "Total" and each kind of data the network carried, as rows with "Sent 1.2 MB · Received 34 MB" as supporting text (kinds with nothing left out), a footer saying it is counted on this device; last "Reset statistics" in `error`, behind an `AlertDialog`, a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it resets and a snackbar once done. Numbers only, no chart |
| Suggest frequent contacts | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-25, `docs/audit/feature-gaps-octogram.md` P3): Privacy and security's "Contacts" group ends with "Suggest frequent contacts" (TDLib's option `disable_top_chats`, inverted) as a switch row (`SwitchItem`), on by default, shown once read, its summary saying what search shows and that turning it off clears them; switched at once, a refusal a snackbar. The group's rows have no icon, as "Sync contacts" |
| Prefer IPv6 and cloud drafts | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-25, `docs/audit/feature-gaps-octogram.md` P3): Data and storage's "Network" group ends with "Prefer IPv6" (`ic_language`, TDLib's option `prefer_ipv6`) as a switch row (`Role.Switch`, `CheckSwitch`, toggle haptics), shown once read, switched at once and flipped back with a snackbar when TDLib refuses it. The "Cache" group adds "Delete cloud drafts" after "Clear cache", a destructive row (`error`, the edit symbol on `errorContainer`; a `LoadingIndicator` while it runs), behind a destructive `AlertDialog` that says secret chats' drafts stay; done, a snackbar |
| Proxy link sheet | A proxy link (`tg://proxy`, `t.me/socks`, `ProxyLinkRoute`, a dialog destination like the join sheet): a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded and scrolls, at most `contentMaxWidth`: the `ic_vpn_lock` badge on `Cookie9Sided` in `primaryContainer`, "Connect through this proxy?" in `titleLargeEmphasized`, what a proxy's owner can see in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, centred; the proxy as two list items on the sheet's color: the address (left to right) over the protocol (never the credentials or the secret), and how it answered (a polite live region) with "Check again"; "You already use this proxy." where it is in use; why connecting failed in `error`, a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it connects; `TextButton` "Cancel" at the start and a default `Button` "Connect" at the end. Connected (`Confirm` haptic): the badge with a check, "Proxy turned on", where to turn it off, and "Done"; the two crossfade on the effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`. Nothing changes before "Connect" |
| Notifications | `MessagingStyle` conversations; small icon `ic_notification`, accent the brand seed (`notification_accent`): a notification has no theme. Placeholder avatars: initials on the §2.2 avatar pairs of the wallpaper's (or brand) light scheme, in Google Sans Flex. Background transfers: one silent ongoing notification in the "Transfers" channel, "Sending 2 files" / "Downloading 1 file", "1.2 MB of 4.5 MB" and the progress (`NotificationCompat.ProgressStyle` on Android 16, asking for Live Update promotion, the percentage as its status bar text; the standard progress bar before), with "Cancel" |
| Media viewer | A live photo (td_api.tl `messagePhoto.video`: iOS Live Photos, Android Motion Photos) shows "LIVE" in `labelSmall` on a `mediaOverlay` pill at its bubble's top start (screen readers hear "Live photo"); in the viewer its motion downloads after the still and plays over it, muted and looping as a GIF does, the still showing until the first frame. A video its sender marked a start time in (td_api.tl `messageVideo.start_timestamp`) starts there the first time it plays in the viewer; a message link with a time (`?t=`, `messageLinkInfo.media_timestamp`) opens the chat at the video and the viewer above it, starting at the link's time instead; picture-in-picture's own position wins over both. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-10): the motion loops by itself rather than playing on a press-and-hold as Telegram's apps do, since a hold already means something in the viewer and loops cost nothing more than a GIF; albums show no "LIVE" label. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-11): a link's time applies to videos only (td_api.tl gives one for voice messages and songs too, which open no viewer). A photo sent as a file (owner request 2026-11-07: an image Android decodes, with a thumbnail, `Document.isViewableImage`) opens here once downloaded rather than in another app, alone (no paging to the chat's photos), and its file row shows its thumbnail in the icon's place (`sizes.avatarMedium`, `medium` corners, over its blurred minithumbnail, dimmed in dark theme). Full-bleed `Scaffold`, `HorizontalPager`, container-transform entry and exit, above the screen it opened from, which stays as it is below it (an overlay destination, `OverlayNavigator`), so the media shrinks back into an unchanged conversation. Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-25, `docs/audit/feature-gaps-octogram.md` P3): a tap in the outer 15% of either side turns to the previous or next item (mirrored in RTL, on the pager's `defaultSpatialSpec` scroll), instead of showing or hiding the chrome as a tap elsewhere does; not while a photo is zoomed in, not on a hidden spoiler; screen readers keep the page's custom actions. Actions in a `HorizontalFloatingToolbar` (standard colors): Forward, Share, Save, Delete (where allowed), each with a tooltip, then More (`DropdownMenuPopup`: show in chat, copy, …). Caption and controls capped at `contentMaxWidth`. A video's seek bar is the expressive `Slider` with its inactive track showing in `outline` how far the player has the video, drawn in the draw phase: a video that streams (`docs/adr/0028-streaming-media.md`) plays as it downloads, with no download ring once its first frame shows. **Picture-in-picture**: on devices that offer it, and never for a secret chat's media, a playing video has a picture-in-picture `IconButton` (`picture_in_picture_alt`, `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`, a `PlainTooltip`) at the end of its controls' row, and a swipe up takes it there too, where a swipe down closes the viewer; the viewer closes and the video plays on in a window of its own, only the video on `scrim`, its aspect ratio (kept within 1 : 2.39 and 2.39 : 1), with play or pause as the window's action. Expanding the window opens the viewer again at that video, where it left off; closing it stops the video. The window cannot open by itself when the user leaves the app, as the call screen's does: it is an activity of its own, so the app's never resizes |
| Profile header | Where the user set a profile color (td_api.tl `profile_accent_color_id`), the bar takes its hue's tinted fill with `extraLarge` bottom corners, and an emoji status (`emoji_status`) shows its custom emoji after the name (`sizes.avatarExtraSmall` expanded, an inline icon collapsed), decorative (§13.12). Material's large flexible top bar built from its parts (`TwoRowsTopAppBar` with `LargeAppBarCollapsedHeight`, as `SettingsTopBar` builds the medium one), `exitUntilCollapsedScrollBehavior`, transparent on the page: expanded, centered, the avatar (`sizes.avatarProfile`, 120 dp) above the name (`displaySmallEmphasized`, two lines at most, smaller rather than cut) and the status with "Premium" / "Scam" / "Fake" labels, the bar as tall as that at the current font scale; collapsed, the name in `titleLargeEmphasized` beside back. The avatar is a circle while expanded (a forum's info page a rounded square, §4) and morphs into `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided` past half the collapse, on `defaultSpatialSpec` (`rememberMorphShape`, snapping with reduced motion). Below it the actions as pills, sharing the row: "Message" (Saved Messages on one's own profile) a filled `Button`, Mute / Unmute (`ToggleOn` / `ToggleOff` haptics) and "Secret chat" `FilledTonalButton`s, each its icon over its label, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`. Then "Photos" with more than one profile photo: a `HorizontalUncontainedCarousel` of square photos (`sizes.profilePhotoItem`), masked to `extraLarge`, paged as it nears the last one |
| Profile info and more | Segmented list items (`ListItemDefaults.segmentedShapes`, `SegmentedGap`) under `titleSmallEmphasized` headers, inset by the window margin and capped at `contentMaxWidth`, no row icons: "Info" — the bio (a bot's "About") with links and mentions, selectable, "Copy" as a custom action; each username (tap copies, a share `IconButton` shares `t.me/…`); the phone number, formatted, left to right (tap opens the dialer with `ACTION_DIAL`, long press or a copy `IconButton` copies); the birthday. "More" — "Add to contacts" / "Edit contact" (where the app has a contact editor), "Block user" in `error` behind an `AlertDialog` / "Unblock user", "Report" in `error`, which opens the report sheet (§6 "Report sheet", with the reasons Telegram gives for the account). "Secret chat" asks first with an `AlertDialog` that says the chat lives on this device and the other person's only. Where the private chat has "Disable sharing" on (td_api.tl `chat.has_protected_content`, set by either person with Telegram Premium), "Info" ends, before the note, with "Messages in this chat can't be forwarded, copied or saved" over "Sharing", as information: Pingogram offers no switch for it. Owner decision (2026-11-11, the recommended choice): the state only, not the setting, as TDLib does not say who turned it on Rows that do not apply are left out: nothing to block, report or start a secret chat with on one's own profile or a deleted account, no secret chat or contact with a bot |
| Contact note | `REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-P2 (td_api.tl `userFullInfo.note`, `setUserNote`; the owner's boards 15a and 15b). On a contact's profile (not one's own, a bot or a deleted account; once the full info is known), the last row of "Info": the note in `bodyLarge` (user content), or "Add a note" in `onSurfaceVariant` without one, over "Note" in `primary` and the emphasized weight, "·", a lock (`inlineIconSmall`) and "Only you see this" in the supporting style; at its end the edit symbol in a `secondaryContainer` circle (`leadingIconContainer`), decorative, as the whole row edits ("Edit note" for screen readers). Its container takes an 8% tint of `primary` over the row's color (`surface`, `surfaceContainer` with AMOLED), so the private note stands apart from what the contact shares. A tap opens a `BasicAlertDialog` in the alert dialog's shape and colors, scrolling where it does not fit: a `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided` badge (`avatarRow`) in `primaryContainer` with the note symbol, "Note about Arjun" in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading), "Only you see this note. It stays with your contacts on all your devices." in `bodyMedium`, the note in a filled multi-line `TextField` labelled "Note" (3 to 6 lines, sentences capitalized) with why a save failed (a polite live region) at the start below it and "66 / 140" at the end (`bodySmall`, tabular figures, "66 of 140 characters" for screen readers), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers, then `TextButton` "Cancel" and a default `Button` "Save" (`labelLargeEmphasized`, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`), disabled until the note changed. Saved, the dialog closes and the row shows the note TDLib sends back; a failure stays in the dialog with the text. The dialog and what was typed survive process death. Edit contact has the same note as a multi-line rounded tonal field after the names (2 to 6 lines), "Only you see this note. It stays with your contacts on all your devices." and the counter below it, saved with the names only where it changed. Notes are plain text: one written with formatting in another app keeps it until edited here |
| Birthday suggestions | `REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-P2 (td_api.tl `suggestUserBirthdate`, `messageSuggestBirthdate`; the owner's boards 16a and 16b). **Offering** (16a): on a contact's profile without a birthday (not one's own, a bot or a deleted account; once the full info is known), a segment of "Info" before the note on `tertiaryContainer` / `onTertiaryContainer` (the board's gold), in the group's segmented shape: the gift (`ic_redeem`) on a `MaterialShapes.Sunny` badge (`avatarRow`) in `tertiary` / `onTertiary`, "No birthday on Lena's profile" in `titleSmallEmphasized` over "Suggest one. Lena decides whether to keep it and who sees it." in `bodyMedium`, one item for screen readers; then "Suggest a birthday", a default `Button` across the segment in `tertiary` / `onTertiary` with the cake symbol (`ic_cake_add`, decorative) and `labelLargeEmphasized`, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`, a `LoadingIndicator` in its label's place while Telegram answers. It opens `core:ui`'s birthday picker (the `DatePicker` of Edit profile's birthday, "Show the year" in its title row) titled "Suggest a birthday" with "Cancel" and "Suggest", whose pick goes to Telegram at once; "Birthday suggested to Lena" or the error is a snackbar, and the offer stays until Lena keeps a birthday. **The message** (16b): `core:ui`'s `BirthdaySuggestionCard`, for `feature:chat` to centre among the messages as a service message: a card on `surfaceContainerHigh` with `extraLarge` corners, at most `mediaMaxWidth`; its header on `tertiaryContainer` with the gift on a `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided` badge (`headlineBadge`) in `tertiary`, "Ada suggested your birthday" ("You suggested Lena's birthday" for one sent) in `titleSmallEmphasized` and the date ("Dec 10", the language's short form) in `experimentalTypography.displaySmallCondensed`, one heading; then what accepting does and which privacy setting applies ("If you accept, it goes on your profile. Who sees it follows your Birthday privacy setting (My contacts)."), or for one sent that the other person decides, in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, centred; then "Decline" (`FilledTonalButton`) and "Accept" (a default `Button`, `labelLargeEmphasized`, a `LoadingIndicator` while Telegram answers) sharing the row with the press morph, collapsing once answered into a full pill, "Added to your profile" with a check on `secondaryContainer` or "Declined" on `surfaceContainerHighest`, crossfading on the effects springs (`SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`), a polite live region. A suggestion sent shows no buttons. Chat list previews say "Ada suggested your birthday" / "You suggested a birthday" |
| Shared media | The last section of the profile on compact and medium windows, the supporting pane (`SupportingPaneScaffold`, a small top bar "Shared media") on expanded ones: tabs as a connected `ButtonGroup` of `ToggleButton`s in a fading horizontal scroll, as the folder tabs (Photos and videos, Files, Links, Voice, Music, GIFs: six, which a connected group holds, and one tab style app-wide), then the tab's items in the same `LazyVerticalGrid` as the page: photos, videos and GIFs in square cells of at least `sizes.sharedMediaCell` (three across a phone), `extraSmall` corners, `mediaAlbumGap` apart, the smallest size that fills a cell, a video's length or "GIF" on a `mediaOverlay` label; files, songs, voice messages and links as segmented rows with their icon in a `secondaryContainer` circle. Photos, videos and GIFs open in the media viewer, files in the chat at their message, links in the browser. The next page loads 20 items from the end; loading is a `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; an empty tab and a failed one a `MaterialShapes` badge (`Cookie9Sided` in `primaryContainer`, `Cookie4Sided` in `errorContainer` with "Try again"), `titleMediumEmphasized` and `bodyMedium`, as `EmptyState` without filling the screen |
| Group and channel info | `ChatInfoRoute`, pushed above the conversation (shared axis X), the title's avatar growing into it (§5), or in the Chats tab's third pane on extra-large windows (§7): the profile's page (`profilePageColor()`, rows on `surface`, `surfaceContainer` with AMOLED) and its large flexible top bar (§6 "Profile header"): the chat's photo over its title and "Public group · 1,024 members" ("subscribers" in a channel), with the "Scam" / "Fake" labels and the verified badge. The pills: Mute / Unmute (`ToggleOn` / `ToggleOff`) and "Leave" ("Leave channel"), `FilledTonalButton`s with icon over label.
