Then segmented groups under `titleSmallEmphasized` headers, no row icons, as "Profile info and more": the description (links and mentions, selectable, "Copy" as a custom action) and the link (`t.me/…`, left to right; administrators of a private group see the primary invite link instead): tap copies, a share `IconButton` shares, "QR code" shows it. "Members" ("Subscribers"): the first five as member rows, then "See all 1,024 members"; where the group hides its members, a row saying so. "Administration", each row where the user's rights allow it, with counts as supporting text: Photo, name and description, Permissions (groups), Administrators, Invite links, Restricted users, Removed users, and Boosts (supergroups and channels). Last "Clear history" (groups) and, for the owner, "Delete group" / "Delete channel" in `error`. The delete row shows only where Telegram lets the user delete the chat for everyone. Leaving, clearing and deleting each ask with an `AlertDialog`; errors are snackbars. The shared media follow as on a profile; expanded windows put them in the supporting pane. Loading `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; a chat that cannot be read an `EmptyState` on `Cookie4Sided` with "Try again". |
| Leaving as the owner | The owner's "Leave" on a group's or channel's info page (td_api.tl `getChatOwnerAfterLeaving`, `transferChatOwnership`; others keep the `AlertDialog`): a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded and scrolls, its steps crossfading on the effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`. **Choosing**: "Leave Rust Berlin?" in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading) over that the user owns it and may choose who owns it next, in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`; "Next owner" in `titleSmallEmphasized` `primary` (a heading) over one radio group of `ListItem`s with `large` corners, the chosen one in `secondaryContainer`, a `RadioButton` at the end: "Let Telegram choose" (the group symbol in a 40 dp `secondaryContainer` circle, `primary` / `onPrimary` while chosen) over who Telegram makes the owner and when ("Ada Lovelace becomes the owner in 7 days, unless you come back"; in a basic group "as soon as you leave"; without a name, that Telegram picks a member), chosen at first; then the other administrators (40 dp avatar, the name as user content, "Admin"), a `DelayedLoadingIndicator` while they load; a note in `bodySmall` `onSurfaceVariant` that only administrators can be chosen ("No other administrators" without any); then `TextButton` "Cancel" at the start and a default `Button` in `error` / `onError` at the end, "Leave" or, with someone chosen, "Transfer and leave" (`labelLargeEmphasized`, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`). **The password** (someone chosen, once Telegram allows a transfer now; its refusals are the member rights page's snackbars): "Make Alan Turing the owner?", what changes, the 2-step verification password in a filled `SecureTextField` that takes the focus, why it was refused below it (a polite live region, a `Reject` haptic), then "Back" and "Transfer and leave". A `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers, the buttons and the sheet's dismissal waiting. Done, the page closes as after any leave. The sheet and the choice survive process death, the password never. Owner decisions (2026-11-11, the recommended choices): only administrators are offered, as Telegram's apps do; leaving after a transfer happens at once, in one tap; a leave that fails after the transfer leaves the user an administrator, with a snackbar |
| Group and channel settings | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24): "Group settings" / "Channel settings", a row of the info page's "Administration" (administrators who may change the info, and the owner). `ChatSettingsRoute`, a settings-style page (medium flexible top bar, segmented groups, no row icons), each group where the user's role allows it: "Reactions" with All reactions / Some reactions / No reactions as radio rows (`SegmentTick`), and while some, a `large` segment saying "Members can react with the reactions you pick." over Telegram's emoji as `IconToggleButton`s (`toggleableShapes()`, `titleLarge`, checked `secondaryContainer`), wrapping; "Content" with "Restrict saving content" (owner); "Posts" (channels) with "Sign messages" and, while signed, "Show authors' profiles"; "Topics" (a supergroup's owner) with "Topics" and, while on, "Show topics as tabs"; "Discussion" with the discussion group (a channel's, opening a `ModalBottomSheet` of the groups that may be linked: `titleLargeEmphasized` title, what linking does, the groups as rows, a delayed indicator, the error or "no group" note) or the linked channel (a group's, read only), and "Unlink" in `error` behind an `AlertDialog`; "Group type" with "Upgrade to supergroup" (a basic group's owner) behind an `AlertDialog`, which opens the new supergroup. Each change goes to Telegram at once, a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` at the top while it answers; refusals are snackbars |
| Join requests | "Join requests" in the info page's "Administration", with the count as supporting text, while people wait and the user may invite. `JoinRequestsRoute`, a settings-style page: "Dismiss all" (`TextButton` in `error`, behind an `AlertDialog`) at the start and "Add all" at the end while several wait, a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while they are processed; the requests as one segmented group, each the 40 dp avatar, the name as user content over the bio (two lines) or "Requested Oct 22", an "Add" `FilledTonalButton` and a dismiss `IconButton` (`ic_close`) at the end, both named with the person for screen readers, disabled while that request is answered; a tap opens the profile. "Load more" (`TextButton`, with its error in `error` and "Try again") below the last while more wait; a footer says where requests come from; none: a note in `bodyLarge` `onSurfaceVariant` |
| Recent actions | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24): "Recent actions" in the info page's "Administration" of a supergroup or channel, for every administrator, after "Administrators". `RecentActionsRoute`, a settings-style page (medium flexible top bar "Recent actions"): the kinds of action as `FilterChip`s (`FilterChipDefaults.shapes()`, several at once, none for every kind) in a row that scrolls sideways (Messages, Members, Admins, Restrictions, Info and settings, Invite links, Voice chats), what the page holds in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` (the last 48 hours, only administrators see it), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while the chips' actions load, the actions shown until then staying; then the actions, newest first, as one segmented group in a keyed `LazyColumn`, at most `contentMaxWidth`: the 40 dp avatar of who did it, their name (`bodyLarge`, user content) over what they did ("Made Alan Turing an admin", the names isolated, §9) and, for a message's edit, deletion or pin, the message in one line in `onSurfaceVariant`, the day and time at the end in `labelSmall`; a person's row opens their profile. Older actions load 5 rows from the end (a `LoadingIndicator`, a failure in `error` with "Try again"); none a line of `bodyLarge` `onSurfaceVariant`; loading `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; a page that cannot be read `Cookie4Sided` with "Try again". Telegram's other kinds of action read "Did something Pingogram can't show yet" |
| Statistics | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24): "Statistics" in the info page's "Administration", after "Recent actions", where Telegram gives the user the chat's statistics (td_api.tl `supergroupFullInfo.can_get_statistics`: administrators of larger supergroups and channels). `ChatStatisticsRoute`, a settings-style page (medium flexible top bar "Statistics") with a keyed `LazyColumn` at most `contentMaxWidth` wide: the period ("Oct 1 – Oct 22, as Telegram counts it") in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`; "Overview" as segmented rows, the value in `titleMedium` with tabular figures over its label, the change against the period before at the end in `labelLarge` (`primary` up, `error` down); then each chart in a segment of its own (`large` corners, the rows' container) under its title in `titleSmallEmphasized`: no chart library, a canvas drawn in the draw phase whose paths are built once per size (`drawWithCache`), `sizes.chartHeight` tall: lines `sizes.chartLine` wide, stacked bars, or stacked areas of shares of the whole, over hairline grid lines in `outlineVariant` at quarters of the highest value, which is labelled above the plot, the x axis's first, middle and last days (or hours) below it, both in `labelSmall` `onSurfaceVariant`; the series take `primary`, `tertiary`, `secondary`, `error` and `onSurfaceVariant` in turn (Telegram's own chart colors are not used), named in a legend of dots and `labelMedium` names where there are several. Screen readers hear each chart as its series' first, last and highest values. A chart Telegram is still making shows a `LoadingIndicator` in its place, one without data a line of `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`. In supergroups "Top members" follows: avatar, name, "312 messages", a tap opening the profile. Loading `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; an error `Cookie4Sided` with "Try again" |
| Group members | A settings-style page above the info page: the medium flexible top bar (`TwoRowsTopAppBar`, as `SettingsTopBar`) titled by the filter ("Members", "Administrators", "Restricted users", "Removed users"; "Subscribers" in channels), the search bar's `InputField` on the rows' container (`surface`; none on the administrators' filter), then the filters as `FilterChip`s (`FilterChipDefaults.shapes()`) in a row that scrolls sideways, only those the user's rights allow (restricted and removed users for admins who may restrict). The members as one segmented group under their count: 40 dp circular avatar with its online dot, the name as user content, the status ("online" in `primary`), after the name the member's tag as a pill (§6 "Member tags"), the role at the end in `labelMedium` ("Owner", "Admin", "Restricted", "Removed"). A tap opens the member's profile (a channel's chat); a long press an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` of what the user may do (Set tag, Promote to admin / Edit admin rights, Dismiss admin, Restrict / Lift restrictions, Allow back; Remove in `error` in a second group; on the user's own row only "Set tag", where they may set theirs), the row in its selected colors while open, the same as screen-reader actions. Removing asks with an `AlertDialog`. The next page loads 5 rows from the end (a `LoadingIndicator` below the rows; a failed page its error in `error` with a "Try again" `TextButton`); nobody found an `EmptyState` on `Cookie9Sided`, a failure on `Cookie4Sided` |
| Member tags | `REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-P3 (td_api.tl `chatMember.tag`, `setChatMemberTag`; the owner's boards 18a and 18b). In groups (not channels), a member's tag follows their name in their row on the members and info pages: `labelMediumEmphasized` on a full pill, an administrator's or the owner's in the accent color (`primaryContainer` / `onPrimaryContainer`), a member's tonal (`surfaceContainerHighest` / `onSurfaceVariant`), one line, user content; screen readers hear "tag Beta tester" after the name. "Set tag" in a member's menu (administrators with "Edit member tags", `canManageTags`, on members and the administrators they may edit; and on the user's own row where the group's permissions let members set theirs, the permission "Set their own tag", `canEditTag`, or the user manages tags) opens a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded and scrolls: the member's avatar (`avatarRow`) beside "Set tag" ("Your tag" for one's own) in `titleLargeEmphasized` over their name and role ("Sahil Rao · Admin") in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, one heading; the tag in a filled `TextField` labelled "Tag" (one line, user content, at most 16 characters, `MemberTagLimits`), with "Shown next to their name in this group" or why a change failed (a polite live region) at the start below it and "11 / 16" (`bodySmall`, tabular figures, "11 of 16 characters" for screen readers) at the end, the keyboard's Done saving; a preview on `surface` with `large` corners, decorative: "Preview" in `labelMediumEmphasized` `onSurfaceVariant`, a sample incoming bubble (`surfaceContainerHigh`, `extraLarge`) with the member's avatar (`avatarExtraSmall`), their name in `primary` `labelLargeEmphasized` and the tag as its pill, over "Hello, everyone!", then which tags show in the accent color in `bodySmall`; a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers; "Remove tag" (an `error` `TextButton`, while they have one) at the start and a default `Button` "Save" (`labelLargeEmphasized`, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`) at the end, disabled until the tag changed. Saved or removed, the sheet closes, the row shows the tag and a snackbar says "Tag saved for Owen Park" / "Tag removed from Owen Park"; a refusal stays in the sheet with what was typed. The sheet and what is typed survive process death. Recent actions word a tag given or taken ("Gave Owen Park the tag “Beta tester”", "Removed the tag of Owen Park"), under the "Admins" chip. Owner decision (2026-11-11, the recommended choice): see §13.12 |
| Community entry | The owner's board 19b (td_api.tl `community.are_chats_collapsed`). In the main list (not in folders or the archive), the chats of each community the user joined that shows as one entry leave their places for one entry, where the newest of them would be; pinned chats stay pinned. The entry is the first segment of a group on `surfaceContainer`, as the pinned chats' (`ListItemDefaults.segmentedShapes`, `spacing.small` above and below the group): Material's two-line list item at 72 dp, the community's photo on `MaterialShapes.Sunny` (`AvatarShape.Community`, `avatarRow`, initials on its placeholder colors without one), its name (user content, `titleMediumEmphasized` while any of its chats is unread, `titleMedium` otherwise) over "Community · 3 chats you joined" ("Community · 4 chats · muted" while its notifications are muted) in the supporting style, then at its end the unread messages of its chats in a `Badge` (`primary` / `onPrimary`, `surfaceVariant` / `onSurfaceVariant` while muted, "999+" at most) and a chevron (`ic_keyboard_arrow_down`, `sizes.inlineIcon`, `onSecondaryContainer`) in a `secondaryContainer` circle of `sizes.avatarExtraSmall`, turning half a turn as it expands on `fastSpatialSpec` in its graphics layer. A tap shows its chats below it or hides them (kept on the device, across process death): each the chat list's own row (swipes, selection, the long press and the open state as everywhere), a segment of the group inset `spacing.large` at the start. A long press (the list item's `LongPress` haptic, the entry in its selected colors and `large` shape while open) opens an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` with "Show chats separately" (`ic_unfold_more`, `toggleCommunityAreChatsCollapsed`), which puts the chats back in their places; a refusal is a snackbar. Disabled in selection mode, whose toolbar acts on the expanded chats as on any other. Screen readers hear one button ("Kyra Labs Community, Community · 3 chats you joined, 5 unread messages", "Expanded" / "Collapsed") with "Show chats separately" as its custom action. Owner decision (2026-11-11, the recommended choice): see §13.12 |
| Community page | The owner's board 19a (td_api.tl `community`). A group's or channel's info page that belongs to a community (`supergroupFullInfo.community_id`) ends its "Info" group with the community's name over "Community" and a `chevron_right` in `onSurfaceVariant`, which opens `CommunityRoute`, pushed above it (shared axis X; in the extra pane where the page sits there): a settings-style page (the medium flexible top bar "Community", the profile page's color) with, for its owner and administrators who may change its info, "Community options" (a `FilledTonalIconButton` with the settings symbol, `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`) opening an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup`: for its members "Show as one entry in the chat list" / "Show its chats separately" (td_api.tl `toggleCommunityAreChatsCollapsed`, a snackbar once Telegram took it), "Rename" for the owner and administrators who may change its info, and, for the owner, "Delete community" in `error` in a group of its own. Then, centred, the community's photo on `MaterialShapes.Sunny` (`AvatarShape.Community`, `avatarLarge`, initials on its placeholder colors without one), its name in `headlineSmallEmphasized` (user content, two lines) over "Community · you own it" / "Community · you're an admin" / "Community" in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, one heading. For its members, under it, two connected buttons sharing the column's width (`ButtonGroupDefaults` connected shapes and their press morph, "Add chats" taking 1.3 shares): "Add chats" (a default `Button` with the add symbol, `labelLargeEmphasized`; "Suggest a chat" where the user may not change the list, td_api.tl `can_edit_chat_list`) and "Mute" / "Unmute" (a `FilledTonalButton`, `setCommunityNotificationSettings`, the community's other settings sent back as Telegram has them; "Mute the community's chats" for screen readers). For those who may change the list, while requests wait (`communityFullInfo.add_chat_request_count`), "2 requests to add a chat" (`titleSmallEmphasized` `primary`) over the requests as one segmented group (`getCommunityAddChatRequests`, read again whenever the count changes): each chat's avatar in its kind's shape, its title (user content) over "Suggested by Riya Sharma" and, for a hidden one, "Hidden · admins only", two lines at most, and at its end "Add" (a `FilledTonalButton`) and "Decline" (an `IconButton` with the close symbol), both named with the chat for screen readers, a `LoadingIndicator` in their place while Telegram answers; a tap opens the chat; the answer is a snackbar. "5 chats in this community" (`titleSmallEmphasized` `primary`) over its chats as one segmented group (`ListItemDefaults.segmentedShapes`, the rows' container): each chat's avatar (`avatarRow`, in its kind's shape, §13.2), its title in `bodyLarge` (user content, one line) over what it is ("Group · 1,120 members", "Channel · 3,904 subscribers", "Bot") after, for a hidden one, "Hidden · admins only" on a `surfaceContainerHighest` pill with the visibility-off symbol (`labelMediumEmphasized`), the two wrapping; at its end "Joined" (a `secondaryContainer` pill with a check, `labelLarge`, not a control) or "Join" (a default `Button`, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`, `labelLargeEmphasized`, a `LoadingIndicator` in its label's place while Telegram answers), nothing for a bot. A tap opens the chat; screen readers hear a row as one ("Pingogram Beta, Group · 1,120 members, Joined"), "Join Spenzia Waitlist" as a button of its own. Where the user may change the list, a long press on a chat ("Remove from community" for screen readers, also a custom action) asks with an `AlertDialog` ("Remove Pingogram Beta from the community?", that the chat stays and its members leave the community unless they are in another of its chats, "Remove") before `removeCommunityChat`. A footer in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` says hidden chats show only to administrators. A request an administrator must accept, a Mini App check and a refusal are snackbars. "Rename" opens a `BasicAlertDialog` (the alert dialog's shape and colors): "Rename community" in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading), the name in a filled one-line `TextField`, a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers, `TextButton` "Cancel" and a default `Button` "Save", disabled while blank; the name typed survives process death. "Delete community" asks with an `AlertDialog` (destructive: the community goes for everyone, its chats stay), then the page closes. "Add chats" opens a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded and scrolls: "Add chats" ("Suggest a chat") in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading) over what adding does (or that the administrators decide) in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`; the user's groups and channels that may be added (`getSuitableCommunityChats`, read each time the sheet opens, those in the community left out) as one segmented group of rows that pick a chat each (`SegmentedListItem` with `selected`, the avatar in its kind's shape turned into a check once picked, the selected colors and shape), or a `DelayedLoadingIndicator`, why they could not be read with "Try again", or that there are none; "Visible to admins only" as a switch row (`CheckSwitch`, the row toggling it, `Role.Switch`) over what it means; a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while the chats go one after another; `TextButton` "Cancel" and a default `Button` "Add" ("Suggest"), disabled until a chat is picked. Telegram turns each chat into a request where the user may not change the list (`COMMUNITY_REQUEST_CREATED`); "2 chats added to the community" or "Request sent to the community's admins" is a snackbar, and the first refusal stops it with the chats not yet sent still picked. Whether the sheet shows, the chats picked and the switch survive process death. Loading `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; a failure `EmptyState` on `Cookie4Sided` with "Try again". Owner decision (2026-11-11, the recommended choice): see §13.12 |
| Welcome message setup | The owner's board 20 (td_api.tl `welcomeMessage`). A supergroup's info page lists "Welcome message" in its "Administration" group (after "Invite links") for its owner and administrators with the right to manage welcome messages (`can_send_welcome_messages`, "Manage welcome messages" on the rights page). `WelcomeMessagesRoute`, pushed above it (shared axis X; in the extra pane where the page sits there): a settings-style page (the medium flexible top bar "Welcome message", the profile page's color). First a card on `primaryContainer` with `extraLarge` corners: "Welcome new members" in `titleMediumEmphasized` over "Shown once to each person who joins" in `bodyMedium`, a `CheckSwitch` at its end (a `LoadingIndicator` in its place while turning off goes to Telegram); the whole card is one switch for screen readers. On while the group has a welcome message: turning it on opens the editor for a first one, turning it off asks with an `AlertDialog` (destructive, "Stop welcoming new members?") and deletes them all. While on: "Messages" (`titleSmallEmphasized` `primary`, a heading) with "2 of 5" at its end (`labelLarge` `onSurfaceVariant`, TDLib's option `welcome_message_count_max`, 5 until TDLib says), then a card per message as one segmented group (`ListItemDefaults.segmentedShapes`, the rows' container): its photo (`sizes.avatarMedium`, `large` corners, dimmed in dark theme as media) beside its text in `bodyLarge` with its formatting (user content, six lines), a message of a kind Pingogram cannot show saying so in `onSurfaceVariant`; below, its kind on a `surfaceContainerHighest` pill (`labelMediumEmphasized`: "Text", "Photo", "Photo and caption", "Can't be shown"), then "Edit message 1" (`ic_edit`) and "Delete message 1" (`ic_delete` in `error`, a `LoadingIndicator` while it goes) `IconButton`s with `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`; screen readers hear the card as one item ("Message 1, Text, Welcome to…"). Deleting asks with an `AlertDialog`. "Add message", an `OutlinedButton` across the column at Material's medium height with the add symbol, disabled at the limit; then a footer in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` (who sees them, or the limit once reached); then "What new members see" over a `surfaceContainer` panel with `extraLarge` corners: "Welcome message · only they see this" in `labelMediumEmphasized` `primary` over the messages as incoming bubbles (`surfaceContainerHigh`, `extraLarge`, at most `sizes.bubbleMaxWidth`, a photo at `sizes.avatarSmall`, three lines of text), one item for screen readers. **The editor**: a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded and scrolls: "New welcome message" / "Edit welcome message" in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading); the photo (`sizes.mediaMinSize`, `large` corners; a `LoadingIndicator` while the picked photo is copied) with "Change photo" (`FilledTonalButton`, `ic_add_a_photo`) and "Remove photo" (an `error` `TextButton`), or "Add photo", which opens Android's photo picker (images only, no permission); the text in a filled multi-line `TextField` (3 to 8 lines) labelled "Message" ("Caption" under a photo), user content, with why Telegram refused it below (a polite live region), or, for a message another app formatted, "Formatting isn't kept when you change the text."; a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers; `TextButton` "Cancel" and a default `Button` "Save", enabled once there is a text or a photo. Saved, the sheet closes with a snackbar and the `Confirm` haptic; what is typed survives process death. Loading `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; a failure an `EmptyState` on `Cookie4Sided` with "Try again". Owner decisions (2026-11-11, the recommended choices): see §13.12 |
| Member rights and group permissions | Settings-style pages (medium flexible top bar, segmented groups, `SwitchItem`-like switch rows with `Role.Switch` and a check in the thumb). A member's rights: the member's avatar and name in a segment, then the administrator rights (or, restricting, the permissions) as switch rows, those the user may not grant (rights they lack themselves, permissions the chat's defaults deny) disabled; a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers; "Dismiss admin" / "Lift restrictions" a `TextButton` in `error` at the start, a default `Button` ("Promote", "Restrict", "Save") at the end, both `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`. Restrictions last until lifted (no end date yet). Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24): for the owner of a supergroup or channel editing a person's administrator rights, "Transfer ownership" in `error` over what it does ("Ada becomes the owner. You stay an administrator.") follows the rights in a segment of its own, a `LoadingIndicator` at its end while Telegram is asked whether it may be done now (td_api.tl `canTransferOwnership`); a password that is missing or too new, or a device logged in too recently, is a snackbar that says how long to wait. Otherwise an `AlertDialog` (destructive, the key symbol): "Transfer ownership to Ada?", what changes and that only Ada can give it back, then the 2-step verification password in a filled `SecureTextField` with its error below it (a polite live region, a `Reject` haptic), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers, "Cancel" and "Transfer" in `error`; done, the page closes. Permissions: what members may do as switch rows, each sent at once and taken back on a refusal; then "Slow mode" in a `SettingsSegment`: the delay ("Every 30 seconds", "Off") in a nested list item above an expressive `Slider` through Telegram's delays (`SegmentTick` per step), sent when released. Editing the photo, name and description: first the photo in a segment of its own on the rows' container, `core:ui`'s `PhotoEditor` as on Edit profile (the photo large and round, initials on the chat's placeholder colors without one; "Set new photo" a `FilledTonalButton` with the add-a-photo icon, which opens the system Photo Picker, images only, no permission; "Remove photo" an `error` `TextButton` behind an `AlertDialog`; both with `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`; while preparing or removing a `ContainedLoadingIndicator` on it, while uploading a determinate `CircularWavyProgressIndicator` around it, the buttons waiting), which changes the photo at once, apart from "Save"; then rounded tonal `TextField`s on the page, a field's error below it, "Save" at the end |
| Invite links | A settings-style page: "Primary link" — a segment with the link (left to right) and how many joined, Copy, Share and QR code as `FilledTonalButton`s sharing its row, "Revoke link" in `error`; "Your links" — "Create a new link", then each link (its name or address, "3 joined · Expires Oct 30"), a tap opening a `DropdownMenuPopup` (Copy, Share, QR code, Edit; Revoke in `error` in a second group); "Revoked links" each with a "Delete" `TextButton`, then "Delete all revoked links" in `error`. Revoking and deleting ask with an `AlertDialog`. A new or edited link: the name in a rounded tonal `TextField` with its count, "Expires" and "Limit members" as radio-row groups (`SegmentTick`), "Request admin approval" as a switch row (which turns the member limit off), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers, a default `Button` "Create link" / "Save" at the end. QR code: a `ModalBottomSheet`, `titleLargeEmphasized` title, the code made on the device (`core:ui`'s `QrCode`: its modules one path, built once per size) on a `large`-rounded square (at most `sizes.illustrationMaxSize`), dark modules on a light ground in every theme (`onSurface` on `surfaceContainerLowest`, the inverse roles in dark) as scanners expect, the link below it (selectable, left to right) and a default `Button` "Share link" |
| Join sheet | An invite link (`ChatInviteRoute`, a dialog destination like the forward sheet): a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded and scrolls, at most `contentMaxWidth`: the chat's avatar (`avatarLarge`), its name in `titleLargeEmphasized` (user content, three lines at most) with the verified badge or the "Scam" / "Fake" label, "Private group · 1,284 members" in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, up to five members' avatars (`avatarExtraSmall`, one item for screen readers: "Members include …"), the description (user content); what a request does, or that a paid chat needs a subscription Pingogram does not offer (no "Join" then), in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`; why joining failed in `error` (a polite live region), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while it joins; `TextButton` "Cancel" at the start and a default `Button` "Join group" / "Join channel" / "Request to join" at the end, both `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`. Steps crossfade on effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`: "Request sent" (`Cookie9Sided` badge, `headlineSmallEmphasized`, "Done"), an invalid link (`Cookie4Sided` on `errorContainer`, "Close", and "Try again" where trying again may help); loading a `DelayedLoadingIndicator`. A chat the user is in opens instead of the sheet. In the conversation of a public group or channel the user is not in, a default `Button` "Join group" / "Join channel" / "Request to join" takes the composer's place, centred in its bar |
| Message channel | The owner's boards 7a and 7b (td_api.tl `supergroup.has_direct_messages_group`, the channel's `supergroupFullInfo.direct_messages_chat_id`). In a channel the user reads without posting, whose subscribers can write to its administrators, the composer's place holds "Message channel": a Material medium-height (56 dp) `Button` across the bar, at most `sizes.bubbleMaxWidth` wide and centred, `ButtonDefaults.shapesFor` press morph and text style, the chat symbol (`ic_chat`) before its label, in `primaryContainer` / `onPrimaryContainer`. Where each message costs Telegram Stars (td_api.tl `paid_message_star_count` of the direct messages chat's supergroup), the label is "Message · 10 ★" in `tertiaryContainer` / `onTertiaryContainer` (the board's gold in the Coral theme), under a note on `surfaceContainerHigh` with `large` corners, the info symbol in `onSurfaceVariant` before "This channel charges 10 ★ per message. Pingogram can't pay yet." in `bodyMedium`: information, never an offer or a payment step. Screen readers hear "Message channel, 10 Telegram Stars per message". A tap opens the user's thread with the channel, pushed as any chat; its composer is the usual one where messages are free, and where they cost Stars the same sentence takes its place (`ComposerUiState.Unavailable`); the channel's administrators never pay. The direct messages chat is looked up (`getSupergroupFullInfo`, `getChat`) once the channel shows: a read, nothing is sent or joined. Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): the board's round mute button beside "Message channel" is left out, as the channel's top bar holds its notifications toggle (§13.5); a paid channel's button still opens the thread, which reads the earlier messages and says the price where the composer would be; the gold is `tertiaryContainer`, which is gold only in the Coral theme; the admins' page is §6 "Channel direct messages" |
| Channel direct messages | The owner's board 7b (td_api.tl `directMessagesChatTopic`): a channel's direct messages chat, for its administrators, opens the list of who wrote instead of one conversation (`DirectMessagesRoute`). The conversation's top bar with the channel (its avatar in its shape, §13.2) over "12 people wrote · 4 unread" on `surfaceContainer`, then on a `ContentSheet` the search bar's field ("Search names", the senders' names matched on the device), All / Unread / Paid as `FilterChip`s (`FilterChipDefaults.shapes()`, "All · 12" and "Unread · 4", one radio group for screen readers), and one row per person in TDLib's order: Material's two-line `ListItem` (72 dp) inset like the chat list's rows, the sender's 48 dp avatar, the name in `titleMedium` (`titleMediumEmphasized` while unread) with a star (`ic_star_filled`, `inlineIconSmall`, `tertiary`) where they pay Telegram Stars per message (td_api.tl `can_send_unpaid_messages` false), one line of the last message, and at the end its time (`labelSmall`, `primary` while unread) over the unread count in a `primary` `Badge` (a dot where only marked unread). A tap opens the person's conversation, pushed as a topic (its top bar their name over the channel's); a long press (the list item's `LongPress` haptic, the row in its selected colors and shape) opens an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` with "Mark as read" / "Mark as unread" (`setDirectMessagesChatTopicIsMarkedAsUnread`), also the row's custom action. More load near the end with a `LoadingIndicator` below; `DelayedLoadingIndicator`, an `EmptyState` (`Cookie9Sided`; nobody yet, or nobody under the filter) and a failure (`Cookie4Sided`, "Try again"). Screen readers hear a row as one: name, "Pays per message", the message, the time, the unread messages. Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): the board's "archive" in the long press is left out, as td_api.tl has no archive for these conversations; the Paid filter shows those whose senders pay rather than paid messages; the page opens wherever Telegram lists the direct messages chat, and an entry from the channel's own info page is left to that page |
| Boosts | `ChatBoostRoute`, a settings-style page above the info page (medium flexible top bar "Boosts"): "Level" as one segment, "Level 2" in `titleMediumEmphasized` and "14 boosts" in `titleMedium` on a row, a determinate `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` toward the next level (its wave still, `waveSpeed` 0: a level, not work under way), "6 more boosts for level 3" (or "The highest level is reached") in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, one item for screen readers with the progress as its range; "Overview" segmented rows (values over labels: boosts, members or subscribers with Telegram Premium "312 · 2.5%", "You boost this channel"); then a note in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` on what boosts are, that Pingogram shows them but cannot give them, and how many boosts the user has unused: never an offer or a link |
| QR code scanner | `QrScannerRoute`, full screen above the screen it opens from (no navigation), on `scrim`: the camera's picture (`CameraXViewfinder`) under a `mediaOverlay` dim with a `MaterialShapes.Cookie12Sided` window (72% of the shorter side, at most `sizes.illustrationMaxSize`) outlined in `primaryFixed` (`sizes.keyImageOutline`), drawn in the draw phase; screen readers hear what to point the camera at, and the screen's name as its pane title. At the top, inside the safe area: close (`FilledTonalIconButton`) at the start, the torch (`FilledTonalIconToggleButton`, `toggleableShapes()`, flashlight on/off symbols) at the end where the camera has one, each with a `PlainTooltip`. At the bottom: what to point it at in `bodyMedium` `onMediaOverlay` on a `mediaOverlay` pill (the computer's login code when linking a device), and "Scan from image" (`FilledTonalButton` with the photo library symbol, the Photo Picker). A code shows its result instead, on a `surfaceContainerHigh` card with `extraLarge` corners (a polite live region): `titleMedium` title, what it means in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` (the code's own text, user content, three lines at most, when it is no Telegram link), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram is asked, then `TextButton` "Copy" at the start (that text only) and a default `Button` "Scan again" at the end; a `Confirm` haptic for every code read. Without the camera, a `surfaceContainerHigh` card in the middle: a `Cookie9Sided` badge (`primaryContainer`, camera symbol), `titleLargeEmphasized` title, why the camera is used and that nothing it sees leaves the phone, and "Continue" (Android's request) or "Open settings" once Android no longer asks; without a camera, that there is none. Steps crossfade on effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec` |
| Personal channel | Edit profile's "Personal channel" (a page row of its own, before the phone number) opens a settings page without row icons: "Show on your profile", "None" and the user's public channels (td_api.tl `getSuitablePersonalChats`) as radio rows in one `selectableGroup` (titles as user content), a `SegmentTick` per pick, the choice shown at once with a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` below until Telegram takes it, a refusal a snackbar; a footer on who sees it, or, without channels, which can be shown. Other users' profiles show it in "Info" as a row: the title (user content) over "Channel", a tap opening its chat |
| Your color | The owner's board 22b (td_api.tl `accentColor`, `setAccentColor`). Edit profile's personal channel group ends with "Your color" ("The color of your name in chats"), which pushes `ProfileColorRoute`, a settings page with "Save" in its top bar (a default `Button`, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`, enabled once another color is picked). First a preview on the chosen hue's tinted fill with `extraLarge` corners: the user's avatar (`sizes.avatarMedium`) and name in `titleLargeEmphasized`, over a sample incoming bubble on `surface` with the name in the hue's ink (`labelLargeEmphasized`) and "This is how your name looks in groups"; one description for screen readers. Then "Name color": Telegram's seven built-in colors as `sizes.themeChip` circles in the vivid fill of the sender hue each is drawn in (§13.6), the chosen one morphing into `Cookie9Sided` with a check (`rememberMorphShape`), one radio group, a `SegmentTick` per pick, named ("Blue"); a footer on where the color shows. Then, where Telegram offers more, "Premium colors": the others as dimmed circles (38%) of the hue they are drawn like, each with a lock badge on `surfaceContainerHighest`, "Premium color" for screen readers, not choosable, and a footer that they come with Telegram Premium: information, never an offer. Saved, the page closes with the `Confirm` haptic; Telegram's refusal is a snackbar. The color picked survives process death |
| Link previews in secret chats | Privacy and security, a "Secret chats" group after "Contacts": "Link previews in secret chats" as a switch row (`SwitchItem`), off by default, its summary saying that Telegram's servers make the previews and so see the links; a change that cannot be saved is a snackbar |
| Hide phone numbers | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-24): Privacy and security, an "On screen" group after "Secret chats": "Hide phone numbers" as a switch row (`SwitchItem`), off by default, its summary saying what it is for. While on, every number on screen (the account card, Edit profile and Change number, profiles, blocked users, Contacts and its invitations, a shared contact's card, a bot's request for the number) shows its calling code and its groups with the other digits as "•" ("+44 •••• ••••••", `phoneNumberToShow`, `core:ui`), in the same style and direction (`numericContent`), so nothing moves; screen readers read it as shown. Copying, dialling and sharing use the whole number; fields where a number is typed (sign-in, Add contact, the new number) are not masked |
| Show IDs | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-25, `docs/audit/feature-gaps-octogram.md` P3): Privacy and security's "On screen" group adds "Show IDs" after "Hide phone numbers", a switch row (`SwitchItem`), off by default, its summary saying where they show. While on, a profile's and an info page's "Info" group ends with the ID (`bodyLarge`, left to right, `numericContent`) over "ID", a tap copying it ("Copy" for screen readers; "ID copied" before Android 13). The data center Octogram shows beside it is left out: td_api.tl gives none for a user or chat, and reading it from a file's remote ID would rely on TDLib's internal format |
| Chats in the share sheet | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-25, `docs/audit/feature-gaps-octogram.md` P3): Privacy and security's "On screen" group ends with "Chats in the share sheet" (`REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-N2, on by default), a switch row (`SwitchItem`) whose summary says that Android's share sheet shows the recent chats with their names and photos, and that turned off it shows only Pingogram, whose own chat picker (§6 "Share sheet") then takes the share. Switched at once, a refusal a snackbar. In "On screen" because the share sheet shows the chats on screen, outside the app |
| Websites | Privacy and security → "Websites" (after Active sessions, `ic_public`), a settings page: "Log out of all websites" as a row in the error role (`ic_logout` in an `errorContainer` circle, a `LoadingIndicator` while it runs), then "Logged in with Telegram": each website the globe in the group's tonal circle, its domain (user content) over the bot that logged the user in with the browser and system ("Example Bot · Firefox, Linux") and the address, place and time it was last active, and a "Log out" `TextButton` named with the domain (a `LoadingIndicator` while it logs out); a footer says what logging out does. Both log-outs ask with an `AlertDialog` (destructive, the globe); results are snackbars. None: a note in `bodyLarge` `onSurfaceVariant`. Loading `DelayedLoadingIndicator`, errors `SettingsError` with "Try again" |
| Log in with Telegram | A website or app the user logs into with the account (td_api.tl `oauthLinkInfo`): a link that opened Pingogram from another app (`internalLinkTypeOauth`, resolved by `LinkRepository`), or a request Telegram announces (`updateNewOauthRequest`), opens `OauthLoginRoute`, a dialog destination like the QR login and proxy link sheets; such a link tapped inside the app offers the browser instead, as TDLib asks. Built from "Link a device" and "Proxy link sheet": a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded and scrolls, at most `contentMaxWidth`, its steps crossfading on the effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`. **The code**, where Telegram wants one (`match_code_first`): the globe on a `Cookie9Sided` badge in `primaryContainer`, "Which code does example.org show?" in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading) over that a wrong one cancels the login in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, the codes as `FilledTonalButton`s (`titleLarge`) in a centred `FlowRow`, and "Decline" (`TextButton`); picking one only keeps it. **The question**: the badge; "Log in to Example News with Telegram?" (the verified name, or "Unverified app") in `titleLargeEmphasized`; then list items on the sheet's color: the domain (`bodyLarge`, left to right) over "Website" or "App"; "Example News gets" over "Your name, username and photo" and "From your account, Ada Lovelace"; the browser and system, and the place the request's address points to (user content); "Share my phone number" as a switch row (`CheckSwitch`, `Role.Switch`, toggle haptics), off at first, where the website asks for it; where it asks to message the user, "Let @examplenews_bot message me", off at first too; where it asks for a TON wallet, that Pingogram doesn't share it, in `bodySmall`; an error in `error` (a polite live region); a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers; `TextButton` "Decline" at the start and a default `Button` "Allow" at the end (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()`). **Done** (`Confirm` haptic): a check on the badge, "You're logged in to example.org", that Pingogram opened it in the browser to go on and that it is listed in Privacy and security → Websites, and "Close". A request that cannot be read, or a wrong code: `Cookie4Sided` in `errorContainer`, why, and "Close" ("Try again" where nothing was read). Nothing is sent before "Allow", which checks the code (`checkOauthRequestMatchCode`), then accepts (`acceptOauthRequest`); "Decline", the scrim, dragging and back decline the request (`declineOauthRequest`, in the app's scope); a request nobody answers expires as TDLib says, and "Allow" then shows Telegram's refusal in the sheet. The code and the switches survive process death. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-11): no board; the bot's messages are an opt-in switch beside the phone number's where the website asks for them (td_api.tl `request_write_access`), as leaving them out would refuse what the website asks without saying so; a TON wallet is never shared (TON Connect is left out); a request Telegram announces opens the sheet over whatever shows; the codes Telegram wants only after confirming are not asked for |
| Passkeys | `docs/adr/0037-passkeys.md`, board 17a. Privacy and security → "Passkeys" (after Websites, `ic_key`, "Sign in with your fingerprint, face or screen lock"), a settings page: a status card (`SettingsStatusCard` on `primaryContainer`, `extraLarge`; the key on a `Cookie9Sided` badge): "Passkeys" over "Sign in without SMS codes", "Use your fingerprint, face or screen lock on a device you trust." and "On · 2 passkeys" or "Off"; then "Your passkeys": each the key in the group's tonal circle, the password manager that keeps it (Telegram's name for it, user content) over "Added Oct 14 · used 9:41 AM" ("never used"), and a "Remove" `TextButton` named with it (a `LoadingIndicator` while it goes), behind a destructive `AlertDialog` ("Remove this passkey?", that the password manager keeps it until deleted there); "You have no passkeys yet." as the group's first segment without one; "Add passkey" (`ic_add`, a `LoadingIndicator` at its end while Telegram and Android's sheet answer) on Android 14 and later only; the footer that password managers, not Pingogram, keep them (and before Android 14 that adding one needs it). Then "Also protecting this account": Two-step verification ("On" / "Off") and Active sessions ("20 sessions, including this device"), each opening its page. Adding opens Android's own passkey sheet; results are snackbars ("Passkey added to …", "Removed …", "Passkeys aren't available to Pingogram yet" where no password manager may make Telegram's passkeys for it, "Couldn't add a passkey. Try again."); a closed sheet says nothing. Loading `DelayedLoadingIndicator`, errors `SettingsError` with "Try again". Owner decisions (2026-11-11, the recommended choices): the page on every Android version, adding from Android 14; the board's per-passkey options button is a "Remove" `TextButton`, as Websites and Blocked users act on a row, since removing is the only option; the board's device line is left out, as td_api.tl gives a passkey no device |
| Sign in with a passkey | `docs/adr/0037-passkeys.md`, board 17b. On the phone number step, while TDLib waits for a phone number (not while the number is being changed after a code was sent) and only on Android 14 and later: below the phone fields, "or" (`labelMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`) between two `outlineVariant` dividers, decorative; then "Sign in with a passkey", a `FilledTonalButton` across the form at Material's medium height (`ButtonDefaults.shapesFor`, its text style), the key on a `Cookie9Sided` badge (`sizes.leadingIconContainer`) in `tertiary` / `onTertiary` before the label, a `LoadingIndicator` in the badge while Telegram and Android's sheet answer (the button named for screen readers meanwhile); and "Uses a passkey you added in Privacy and security. No code needed." in `bodySmall` `onSurfaceVariant`, centred. Next and "Log in with QR code" stay at the bottom as before. A tap asks Telegram for its request and opens Android's own passkey sheet; with the password manager's answer TDLib moves on as after a code (to the 2-step verification password, or signed in), the `Confirm` haptic once Telegram took it, `Reject` where it refused it. A closed sheet says nothing; no passkey found, no password manager allowed to use Telegram's passkeys for Pingogram, and other failures are snackbars, Telegram's refusals as the step's other errors (a flood wait its banner). Owner decisions (2026-11-11, the recommended choices): the option sits under the fields, above the bottom bar's Next, where the board puts Next first; Material's medium height (56 dp) rather than the board's 64 px; the badge in `tertiary` rather than the board's gold container, which vanished on the tonal button in the brand theme; and the board's footer ("an unofficial Telegram client", the privacy policy), which this step does not show today, is left as it is |
| Link a device | `QrLoginRoute`, a settings page: a `Cookie9Sided` badge (`primaryContainer`, computer symbol), "Log in on another device?" in `titleLargeEmphasized`, that only codes Telegram shows on the user's own devices may be scanned in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, why the last try failed in `error` (a polite live region, a `Reject` haptic), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` while Telegram answers, `TextButton` "Cancel" at the start and a default `Button` "Log in" at the end. Done (`Confirm` haptic): the badge with a check, "Device linked", the new session as Active sessions shows it under "New session", a footer that it can be logged out there, and "Done". Active sessions starts with "Link desktop device", a row of its own with the scanner symbol in the group's tonal circle |
| Your QR code | `MyQrCodeRoute`, a settings page: the avatar (`avatarLarge`), the name in `titleLargeEmphasized`, the code of the profile link (`core:ui`'s `QrCode` on a `large`-rounded square, 60% of a `MaterialShapes.Cookie12Sided` frame in `primaryContainer`, as QR login's), the link below it (selectable, left to right); a temporary link (no username) says for how long it works, with a "Set username" `TextButton` (Edit profile); then "Scan QR code" (`FilledTonalButton`) at the start and "Share" (default `Button`, the share sheet) at the end. The Contacts tab's header holds its two `FilledTonalIconButton`s with tooltips: "Your QR code" and "Scan QR code" |
| Home screen widget | Recent chats (`docs/adr/0017-home-screen-widget.md`), built from Glance's components, since a widget is `RemoteViews`, not Compose: Glance's `Scaffold` on `widgetBackground` with the launcher's corner radius; its `TitleBar` with the Pingogram mark and "Chats", and a "New chat" `CircleIconButton` (edit symbol, `primaryContainer` / `onPrimaryContainer`) kept `spacing.small` from the rounded corner; then a `LazyColumn` of rows as the chat list's: a 40 dp round avatar, the title (16 sp, `titleMedium`'s size, bold while unread) over one line of the last message (14 sp, `onSurfaceVariant`), and a pill badge with the count or "@" (`primary` / `onPrimary`, muted `surfaceVariant` / `onSurfaceVariant`), drawn from shape drawables so launchers before Android 12 round them too. Signed out, locked or without chats: one centred `bodyMedium`-sized line in `onSurfaceVariant`. Colors from the wallpaper or the color theme, light or dark with the system; the launcher's font, as RemoteViews cannot load Google Sans Flex; sizes in `WidgetTokens` (`:app`), Material's tokens in RemoteViews' units. Each row is one item for screen readers ("Alice, 3 unread messages, Hi") |
| Chat export | `REQUIREMENTS.md` §5.9, `docs/adr/0021-chat-export.md`. "Export chat" is a row without icon in the profile's "More" group (Saved Messages: on the user's own profile, which its title opens) and in the info page's last group, only where the chat lets its content be saved; not in the conversation's top bar (its two places hold search and a call or the timer) nor in the chat list's selection toolbar (its five buttons fill the narrowest phones). `ChatExportRoute`, a settings page pushed above it (shared axis X; in the extra pane where the page sits there): the medium flexible top bar "Export chat", then segmented groups on the page's color: the chat's name (user content) over "The export goes only into the folder you choose. Nothing is uploaded."; "Format" with HTML / JSON as connected `ToggleButton`s (`SegmentTick`, `labelLargeEmphasized` checked) and what the chosen one is for in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`; "Media" with switch rows (Photos, Videos, Voice messages, Files) and "Largest file" over an expressive `Slider` (1 MB to any size, `SegmentTick` per step, disabled without a kind) as Automatic download's, a footer that media left out is named; "Messages" as radio rows (All messages, From a date, which opens a `DatePickerDialog` up to today); "Folder" with its name (user content) or "No folder chosen" and a "Choose" / "Change" `TextButton` (the Storage Access Framework's picker); then a default `Button` "Export" at the end, enabled once a folder is chosen. Running: a segment "Exporting Ada" (`titleMedium`), a `LinearWavyProgressIndicator` (determinate where the size is known), "1,234 messages so far" or "Telegram asked to wait 12 s" as a polite live region, a footer that it goes on in the background, and a `TextButton` "Cancel export" at the end. Done: an `EmptyState` (`Cookie9Sided`, check) "Chat exported" with the counts, line by line, `TextButton` "Export again" and `Button` "Open folder" (a snackbar where no app opens folders). Failed: `Cookie4Sided` in `errorContainer` with why (folder gone, storage full, Telegram's error) and "Try again". A secret chat or one with protected content: `Cookie9Sided` with the lock and why, no action. Steps crossfade on the effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`; loading a `DelayedLoadingIndicator`. The notification: "Exporting Ada" with the progress and "Cancel" in the "Transfers" channel |
| Send feedback | `REQUIREMENTS.md` §6.2, `docs/TESTING_GUIDE.md`: pushed above Settings → About (shared axis X), whose "Send feedback or report a bug" row (`ic_bug_report`) opens it. A small `TopAppBar` with back on `surfaceContainer`, the page on a `ContentSheet`, at most `contentMaxWidth` inside the window margin: what it does in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`; "What happened?" in the rounded tonal `TextField` (three lines at rest, user content); "In the report" (`titleSmallEmphasized` `primary`) over a `surfaceContainerHigh` card with `large` corners: app, device, last crash, connection log, each a `labelMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` label over its `bodyMedium` value, one item each for screen readers; what is taken out in `bodySmall` `onSurfaceVariant`; `TextButton`s "Show report" / "Hide report", "Copy" and, with a crash kept, "Delete crash report" in `error`; the whole report selectable in `bodySmall` `FontFamily.Monospace` on `surfaceContainerHighest` (`medium`), growing in on `defaultSpatialSpec`; then "Open GitHub issue" (`FilledTonalButton`) and "Share report" (default `Button`) at the end, all with `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`. Loading `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; results are snackbars |
| Tooltips / onboarding | `TooltipBox` with `PlainTooltip` on icon-only buttons in toolbars, `RichTooltip` for onboarding |
| Date pickers (schedule) | `DatePicker` + `TimePicker` in `DatePickerDialog`. For a Telegram Premium subscriber scheduling from the composer's send button (td_api.tl `messageSchedulingStateSendAtDate.repeat_period`, which Telegram takes from them only and for one message at a time), the time step ends in "Repeat": a row of at least 48 dp with the repeat symbol in `onSurfaceVariant`, "Repeat" in `bodyLarge` and the chosen period ("Never", "Daily", "Weekly", "Every 2 weeks", "Monthly", "Every 3 months", "Every 6 months", "Yearly") in `labelLarge` `primary` with a drop-down arrow, opening an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` (one `DropdownMenuGroup`, `MenuDefaults.groupShape` / `itemShape`) of those, the chosen one with a check; one drop-down list for screen readers, the period as its state, the chosen item selected. The chosen period is kept across rotation and process death, and goes with the message; Scheduled messages shows it ("Daily"). Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-10): the repeat is offered only from the composer's own send (one message), not from the attach preview, polls, checklists, a location or inline results, and only to Telegram Premium subscribers, never as an offer; the periods are those Telegram's apps offer |
| Navigation drawer | not used (folders live in tabs) |
| Call screen | `REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-V1, `CallActivity` (`feature:calls`), over the lock screen, laid out as Android's own Phone app: full window on `surfaceContainer`, with the chip, the key's pill, the slider's handle and the call's buttons on `surfaceBright` above it. At the top inside the safe area: "Back to the app" (`IconButton`, keyboard arrow down) at the start; then, centred, the stage in `bodyLarge` `onSurfaceVariant` with tabular figures ("Calling…", "Ringing…", "Incoming voice call", "Connecting…", "Reconnecting…", "On hold", why it ended), or once connected the timer after the signal symbol (one bar in `error` while reconnecting), a polite live region; the name in `displaySmall` (user content, two lines at most); and once the key is agreed its four emojis in a pill (`surfaceBright`, full shape) with the encrypted symbol in `primary`, `titleLarge`, growing in on `defaultSpatialSpec`, a tap on the pill says what the emojis are for in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`; screen readers hear "Encryption key: …" as one item. In the middle the avatar, up to 60% of the width (at most `sizes.illustrationMaxSize`), clipped to `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided` while the call rings, which turns a lobe at a time (a ninth of a turn every 1.2 s on `defaultSpatialSpec`, the photo kept upright), morphing to `Cookie12Sided` once answered; an incoming call shows it at full size, calling and talking at 72%, scaled in its graphics layer on `defaultSpatialSpec`; still with reduced motion. Incoming: a "Message" `AssistChip` (`surfaceBright`, no border, chat symbol), which declines and opens the chat after unlocking; below it the answer slider, a pill on `surfaceContainerHighest` (at most 1.4 × `sizes.illustrationMaxSize` wide) with "Decline" at its start and "Answer" at its end in `bodyLarge`, fading as the handle moves, and between them a medium wide handle (`surfaceBright`, full shape) holding the phone in `pingogramColors.online`: sliding it 70% of the way toward either (mirrored right to left, in the layout phase) gives a `TextHandleMove` haptic, and releasing there answers (`Confirm`) or declines (`Reject`), elsewhere it springs back on `defaultSpatialSpec`; the phone turns into a hang-up toward "Decline"; a tap nudges it toward "Answer". Screen readers hear one item with "Answer" and "Decline" as custom actions; with TalkBack (touch exploration) the slider gives way to large (96 dp) `FilledIconButton`s, "Decline" (`error`) at the start and "Answer" (`primary`) at the end, over their labels. During the call: a `surfaceContainerHigh` panel docked to the bottom edge with `extraLarge` top corners (a card of its own in landscape), holding Mute, Speaker (or Audio: an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` of the routes, the one in use with a check, where a headset or Bluetooth is there), Camera (where the device has one; `videocam` / `videocam_off`, disabled while held) and Hold as medium wide `FilledTonalIconToggleButton`s (`surfaceBright` / `onSurface`, checked `primary` / `onPrimary`, `toggleableShapes()`, `ToggleOn` / `ToggleOff` haptics) over their `labelMedium` labels, then "Hang up" as a medium `Button` in `error` / `onError`, 40% of the panel wide, with `shapesFor` press morph. The controls crossfade on the effects springs as the call moves on. Compact height (phones in landscape): the person at the start, the controls at the end. **Video** (ADR 0019): while either side's video shows, the other person's video fills the window (the user's own camera while theirs is off), cropped to it, drawn by WebRTC's `EglRenderer` in a `TextureView`; "Back to the app" becomes a filled `IconButton` and the stage, the timer and the name (`titleLarge`, one line) sit on a `pingogramColors.mediaOverlay` pill (`large`, `onMediaOverlay`), the key's pill below it; "Video paused" on the same pill in the middle while their video is held back; the self-view, mirrored for the front camera, in a `large`-rounded card at the end above the panel (30% of the window's shorter side, 3:4, 4:3 in landscape) with "Switch camera" (`cameraswitch`) as a `FilledTonalIconButton` in the overlay's colors at its bottom end where there are a front and a back camera; the same panel docked at the bottom (at the end in landscape). "Incoming video call" names a ringing video call; it is answered with the camera on where the permission is there, and the camera is asked for when the user turns it on. Videos are read as "Video of Ada" and "Your camera". Leaving a video call (Home, back, "Back to the app") moves it into picture-in-picture (9:16, entered by itself on Android 12+): only the video, the user's own while the other person's is off, the avatar once neither camera sends, with "Hang up" as the window's action; a tap brings the call screen back. **Screen sharing** (`docs/adr/0026-screen-sharing.md`): while the call is connected and not on hold, "Share screen" at the top end, opposite "Back to the app" (an `IconToggleButton` with `toggleableShapes()`, `screen_share`, `stop_screen_share` while sharing, in the overlay's colors over video), which opens Android's consent dialog; once agreed, the screen replaces the camera, and under the header a `primaryContainer` pill (full shape) says "You're sharing your screen" in `labelLarge` after the screen-share symbol, a polite live region, with a default `Button` "Stop" (`labelLargeEmphasized`, `shapes()`) at its end, growing in on `defaultSpatialSpec`; the notification adds "Stop sharing". No preview of the shared screen. The other person's shared screen takes their video's place, fitted to the window rather than cropped, read as "Screen shared by Ada". Microphone missing: why it is needed in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, `TextButton` "Not now" (or "Decline") at the start and a default `Button` "Continue" ("Open settings" once Android no longer asks) at the end. An ended call shows why for 2 s, then the screen closes |
| Call rating | "Rate this call", when Telegram asks for it (td_api.tl `callStateDiscarded.need_rating`) after a call hung up with at least 5 s connected: a `ModalBottomSheet` over the ended call screen (which stays until it closes), never in picture-in-picture. `titleLargeEmphasized` title; five stars centred as one radio group (Material has no rating component): each a 48 dp target the size of a medium icon button with its ripple (`IconButtonDefaults.mediumContainerSize`, circle), the chosen star and those before it filled (`ic_star_filled`) in `primary`, the others outlined (`ic_star`) in `onSurfaceVariant`, the chosen one popping to 120% on `fastSpatialSpec` in its graphics layer, a `SegmentTick` haptic; screen readers hear "3 stars", selected or not. For one to three stars "What went wrong?" (`titleSmallEmphasized` `primary`) grows in (`expandVertically` on `defaultSpatialSpec`, effects-spring fades) over Telegram's problems as `FilterChip`s (`FilterChipDefaults.shapes()`, wrapping, toggle haptics), the video ones only for video calls. Then that the rating goes to Telegram to improve calls in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, and `TextButton` "Not now" at the start and a default `Button` "Send" at the end (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()`, `labelLargeEmphasized`, `Confirm`), enabled once a star is chosen. It never blocks: the scrim, dragging, back and "Not now" close it without rating, and the screen closes with it |
| Call buttons and notifications | The conversation's top bar of a private chat with someone who can be called holds a call `FilledTonalIconButton` (`ic_call_filled`, "Call" for screen readers), like the secret chat's; not in a secret chat, whose two actions are the timer and the key (its profile calls). The profile's pills gain "Call" (`FilledTonalButton`, icon over label). Both are one button for voice and video (owner decision, 2026-10-23, `CallButtonMenu`): where the other user can get a video call and the device has a camera, a tap opens the expressive menu anchored to it, "Voice call" (`ic_call`) and "Video call" (`ic_videocam`) in one group, the same two as screen readers' custom actions on "Call"; otherwise a tap calls at once. A call's service message in the chat ("Call back") calls back the way the call went, a video call with the camera on. Notifications: Android's call style (`NotificationCompat.CallStyle`), incoming in the "Calls" channel with the ringtone, ongoing with "Hang up" and the timer in the silent "Ongoing calls" channel |
| Voice chat screen | `docs/adr/0023-group-voice-chats-and-live-streams.md`, `GroupCallActivity` (`feature:calls`), in the call screen's task and style: full window on `surfaceContainer`. At the top inside the safe area: "Back to the app" (`IconButton`, keyboard arrow down) at the start, then the chat's title in `titleLarge` (user content, one line, a heading) over where the voice chat is in `bodyLarge` `onSurfaceVariant`, a polite live region: "Joining…", "Reconnecting…", why it ended, or once connected "12 participants · 4:05" ("1,284 watching" for a live stream). In the middle the participants, a keyed `LazyColumn` of Material's `ListItem`s on the window's color, at most `contentMaxWidth` wide: a 40 dp avatar (`avatarSmall`) ringed in `primary` while the participant speaks (a `sizes.avatarRing` stroke whose opacity follows on `fastEffectsSpec`, drawn in the draw phase), the name in `bodyLarge` (user content; "Ada (you)" for the user), "Speaking" in `primary`, "Listening", "Muted" or "Wants to speak" in `onSurfaceVariant`, and the matching symbol at the end (mic, mic off, `back_hand`); screen readers hear each row as one ("Grace Hopper, Speaking"). Who speaks comes from Telegram's recent speakers and lags a second or two (ADR 0023, a question for the owner). At the bottom the call screen's panel (`surfaceContainerHigh`, `extraLarge` top corners, docked): Mute, or "Raise hand" (`back_hand`, checked while raised) while an administrator keeps the user muted, crossfading on the effects springs, the audio route, and Camera (`videocam` / `videocam_off`) where Telegram allows video in the call and the device has a camera (`docs/adr/0024-video-in-voice-chats.md`), all the call screen's medium wide `FilledTonalIconToggleButton`s over their `labelMedium` labels; then "Leave", the call screen's red hang-up pill. For one who may end the voice chat, "Leave" opens an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` of "Leave" and "End voice chat" in `error`, which asks with an `AlertDialog` ("End the voice chat?", "It ends for everyone in it.", "Cancel" / "End" in `error`). While the camera sends, the call screen's self-view (mirrored for the front camera, `large` corners, 4:3, "Switch camera" at its bottom end) sits centred between the header and the participants, 60% of the window's width up to `sizes.illustrationMaxSize`; the camera's permission is asked when it is turned on, and it stops while the call is held. Screen sharing (`docs/adr/0026-screen-sharing.md`): "Share screen" at the header's end while connected (not in a live stream), the call screen's toggle; once Android's consent is given, the screen goes out beside the camera and the call screen's "You're sharing your screen" pill with "Stop" sits under the header, growing in on `defaultSpatialSpec`. Others' shared screens are tiles of the grid, fitted rather than cropped, read as "Screen shared by Grace Hopper", first among the videos after the pinned participant's. Others' videos (at most four: the pinned one, then those speaking) head the participants list instead, scrolling with it, at most `contentMaxWidth` wide, `spacing.small` apart: the pinned video across the width at 16:9 (a single video too), the others two to a row, square, the self-view last among them; each tile with `large` corners on `surfaceContainerHighest`, the name (user content, one line) on a `mediaOverlay` pill in `labelLarge` `onMediaOverlay` with the push-pin symbol before it while pinned, a `primary` outline of `sizes.avatarRing` while the participant speaks, and "Video paused" on the same pill in the middle while they hold it back. A tap pins a tile or unpins the pinned one (screen readers hear "Video of Grace Hopper", "Pinned", "Pin video" / "Unpin video"). Leaving the screen while they show moves it into picture-in-picture with the first tile's video (16:9, "Leave" as its action). A live stream shows its video in the participants' place (WebRTC's renderer in a `TextureView`, `large` corners inset `spacing.medium`), the panel only the route and "Leave", and leaving the screen moves it into picture-in-picture (16:9, "Leave" as its action). Before joining without the microphone: the chat's title in `headlineSmall`, why in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` (joined muted, nothing sent until unmuted), `TextButton` "Not now" at the start and a default `Button` "Continue" ("Open settings" once Android no longer asks) at the end. Joining refused (another call, no voice chat anymore): the title and why, centred, for 2 s, then the screen closes; so does an ended voice chat. **Conference calls** (`docs/adr/0025-conference-calls.md`, a `t.me/call/…` link): before joining, "Join the group call?" in `headlineSmall` (a heading), that anyone with its link can join, that it is end-to-end encrypted and that the user joins muted in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, `TextButton` "Not now" at the start and a default `Button` "Join" (`labelLargeEmphasized`) at the end, both with `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`; nothing joins before that tap. In the call the title is "Group call", and once TDLib verified the call's state its four emojis sit under the header in the call screen's key pill (`surfaceBright`, full shape, the encrypted symbol in `primary`, `titleLarge`, growing in on `defaultSpatialSpec`), a tap saying what they are for; screen readers hear "Encryption key: …" |
| Add people to a call | Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-27): in a conference call the user is connected to, "Add people" heads the participants list as their rows do: the add-person symbol in a `primaryContainer` circle at the avatars' size (`avatarSmall`), "Add people" in `bodyLarge`, one button for screen readers. It opens a `ModalBottomSheet` built as the pickers of chats are (§6 "Chat folders"): "Add people" in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading), that each gets an invitation in their chat with the user in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, the search bar's `InputField` ("Search contacts", filtering on the device by the start of a name or username), then the contacts not in the call (people only) as toggleable rows inset `spacing.small` like the chat list's, 48 dp avatars, the name as user content, a picked one `large` in `secondaryContainer` with its avatar turned into a check (§4), checkboxes for screen readers; `TextButton` "Cancel" at the start and a default `Button` "Add" ("Add 2") at the end (`ButtonDefaults.shapes()`, `labelLargeEmphasized`), disabled until someone is picked. Loading a `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; nobody found, no contacts to invite and a failure a line of `bodyMedium` (`error` for the failure); crossfading on the effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`. "Add" closes it; what came of it is a snackbar above the panel ("2 invitations sent", "1 person couldn't be invited.", or why it failed). Telegram sends each invitation as a message in the private chat, to a video call while the user's camera is on. In a connected one-to-one call that both sides can turn into a conference call, the call screen's top bar holds "Add people" at its end before "Share screen" (the add-person symbol in an `IconButton` with `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`, filled in the overlay's colors over video), which opens the same picker without the other person; "Add" ends the call's audio for a moment ("Moving to a group call…" as its stage) and the voice chat's screen takes over with the conference call, the invitations' snackbar there. A call the other person upgrades moves there by itself. An invitation in a chat is a service pill ("Group call", "Missed group call", "Group call ended"); while its call goes on, the pill ends in the call symbol in `primary` and a tap ("Join" for screen readers) opens the voice chat's screen, which asks "Join the group call?" before it joins |
| Voice chat comments | `REQUIREMENTS.md` FR-V2, board 9a/9b (owner design, 2026-11-10). Where the voice chat or conference call has comments on (td_api.tl `groupCall.are_messages_allowed`), or the user may turn them on, its header ends with "Comments", a `ToggleButton` (`ToggleButtonDefaults.shapes()`, the comment symbol, outlined while off and filled while on, `primary` / `onPrimary` checked, a switch for screen readers, `ToggleOn` / `ToggleOff`), which shows or hides the pane on this device (on at first, kept in saved state); never in a live stream. While it shows: the participants become a keyed `LazyVerticalGrid` of tiles (`GridCells.Adaptive(sizes.sharedMediaCell)`, `spacing.small` apart) on `surfaceContainerHigh` with `extraLarge` corners, each the participant's avatar (`sizes.avatarMedium`) ringed in `primary` while they speak (the ring's opacity on `fastEffectsSpec` in the draw phase, still with power saving), the name in `labelLargeEmphasized` and what they are doing in `labelSmall` (`primary` while speaking), one item for screen readers; the videos and, in a conference call, "Invite link" (an `AssistChip` on `surfaceContainerHigh` with the link symbol, opening Android's share sheet with `t.me/call/…`) and "Add people" head the grid across its width. Then the call's controls in one row without labels (the panel's toggles, the route, the camera, and "Leave" as a round `error` `FilledIconButton` at the medium size with its menu), then the pane, which takes 60% of the height: `surfaceContainerHigh` with `extraLarge` top corners to the window's bottom edge, a pane for screen readers. "Comments · 214" (`titleSmallEmphasized` `primary`, a heading; how many came since the user joined) heads it, with an administrator's "Comment options" (`more_vert`, an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup`: "Turn off comments" / "Turn on comments", td_api.tl `toggleGroupCallAreMessagesAllowed`). The comments follow in a keyed `LazyColumn` laid out from the bottom, newest at the bottom (at most 200 kept): the sender's avatar (`avatarExtraSmall`), the name in `labelLargeEmphasized` in their sender hue's ink (§13.6), the text in `bodyMedium` (user content); a paid one (`paid_message_star_count`) on `tertiaryContainer` with `large` corners and a "★ 50" pill (`labelSmallEmphasized`, `onTertiary` on `tertiary`), shown as Telegram sent it; the user's own that Telegram refused keeps its text with "Not sent" in `error`. A long press (`LongPress` haptic) opens an expressive menu of what the user may do, all in `error`: "Delete" (their own, or as an administrator), "Delete and report spam" and "Delete all from Ada" (administrators, td_api.tl `deleteGroupCallMessages`, `deleteGroupCallMessagesBySender`), the same as the row's custom actions; one item for screen readers. Reactions float up from the pane's bottom end: each `headlineMedium` emoji rises three reaction tiles on `slowSpatialSpec` and fades on `slowEffectsSpec`, in its graphics layer, decorative, none with reduced motion or while power saving stops the call screen animations (owner decision 2026-11-11, §5.1). "Add a comment" ends the pane: the rounded tonal `TextField` (`extraLarge`, `surfaceContainerHighest`, no indicator, up to four lines, user content) with the emoji `IconButton` at its end, and the send `FilledIconButton` at the medium size beside it (`Confirm`), disabled while the field is blank; the emoji button opens an expressive menu of six quick reactions (❤ 🔥 👏 😂 😮 👍, "Send ❤" for screen readers, `ToggleOn`), each sent at once as a comment of that emoji alone. Where the user may not write, one line in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant` says why instead, crossfading on the effects springs: "Comments are off", "Too many people are here for comments" (`can_send_messages` false), or, where a comment costs Telegram Stars, "Comments here cost Telegram Stars. Pingogram can't pay yet." (never an offer, `CLAUDE.md` rule 3). New comments are heard politely through the header, at most once every 5 s, "3 new comments. Newest from Ada: …", never the user's own. Refusals are snackbars |
| Voice chat bar | At the top of a conversation's sheet while its chat has a voice chat or live stream (`VoiceChatBarSlot`, `core:ui`), above the pinned messages bar and inset as it is: a tonal strip on `surfaceContainerHigh` with `large` corners: the `graphic_eq` symbol (`live_tv` for a live stream) in `primary`, "Voice chat" / "Live stream" in `labelLarge` `primary` over "12 participants" / "1,284 watching" in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, one line each, and a `FilledTonalButton` "Join" ("Open" once the user is in it, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`). The whole strip joins; screen readers hear one button ("Voice chat, 12 participants", "Join"). It grows in and shrinks away like the pinned bar (`expandVertically` on `defaultSpatialSpec`). A group's or channel's info page has the same as a pill among its actions ("Voice chat", or "Start voice chat" for administrators who may manage voice chats while there is none) |
| Recent calls | `RecentCallsRoute` (`feature:calls`), pushed above the screen it opens from (shared axis X): Settings home's "Recent calls" row (a group of its own above General, `ic_history`) and the Contacts tab's top bar (a `FilledTonalIconButton` with its tooltip, before the QR code buttons). A small `TopAppBar` with back on `surfaceContainer` ("Recent calls", `titleLargeEmphasized`), the content on a `ContentSheet`: "All" and "Missed" as `FilterChip`s (`FilterChipDefaults.shapes()`, one radio group for screen readers) at the window margin, then the calls by day under `titleSmallEmphasized` `primary` headers ("Today", "Yesterday", "Monday, March 17"), newest first. Rows as the chat list's: Material's two-line `ListItem` (72 dp) inset `spacing.small`, the 48 dp circular avatar, the name in `titleMedium` (user content), below it the direction's symbol (`call_received`, `call_made`, `call_missed`, `call_missed_outgoing`, `sizes.inlineIconSmall`) and how the call went with its length ("Outgoing video call · 4:05", `bodyMedium`, tabular figures), both in `error` for a missed call; at the end the time (`labelSmall`) over `call` or `videocam`, what a tap does.
