A tap calls back the way the call went (the call screen opens); a conference call's invitation reads "Group call" ("Missed group call"), and a tap joins it while it goes on (the voice chat's screen, which asks first; "Join the group call" for screen readers) or says in a snackbar that it has ended; a long press opens an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup` with "Delete from call history" in `error` (the row in the list item's selected colors and `large` shape while it shows), which removes the call at once, Telegram's refusal putting it back with a snackbar. Screen readers hear one button ("Grace Hopper, Missed call, 9:41 AM", "Call back") with the delete as its custom action. Owner decision (2026-11-08): "Start a call", an `ExtendedFloatingActionButton` in `primaryContainer` with the filled call symbol at the bottom end, its label folding into the round button once the list scrolls (the list ends a button's height clear of it), opens a `ModalBottomSheet` that opens expanded: "Start a call" in `titleLargeEmphasized` (a heading); "New group call" as a row with the group symbol in a `primaryContainer` circle at the avatars' size over "Start a call and invite people to it", which opens the voice chat's screen on a new conference call (`docs/adr/0025-conference-calls.md`, joined muted, without asking first: the user starts it), where "Add people" invites; the search bar's `InputField` ("Search contacts", filtering on the device by the start of a name or username); then the contacts (people only) as rows inset `spacing.small` like the chat list's, the 48 dp avatar and the name as user content, ending in a voice call `IconButton` (`ic_call`) and, where the device has a camera, a video call one (`ic_videocam`), each with `IconButtonDefaults.shapes()`, a `PlainTooltip` and "Voice call Ada" / "Video call Ada" for screen readers; a tap closes the sheet and calls through the call starter, as "Call back" does. Loading a `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; nobody found, no contacts and a failure a line of `bodyMedium` (`error` for the failure), crossfading on the effects springs with `SizeTransform` on `defaultSpatialSpec`; the query is in the saved state until it closes. The next page loads 5 rows from the end, a `LoadingIndicator` below the rows, a failed page its error in `error` with "Try again". Loading `DelayedLoadingIndicator`; no calls or no missed calls an `EmptyState` (`Cookie9Sided`, call symbol); a failure `Cookie4Sided` in `errorContainer` with "Try again" |
| Lock screen and passcode entry | Full-window `Scaffold` on `surface` (nothing of the app behind it), centered column capped at `contentMaxWidth`: a `MaterialShapes.Gem` badge in `primaryContainer` that morphs to `Cookie4Sided` in `errorContainer` on a wrong passcode, the title, the hint or the error in `error`, then the PIN as dots (`sizes.passcodeDot`, `onSurface`, growing in on `fastSpatialSpec`) in a pill on `surfaceContainerHighest`, or a filled `SecureTextField` for a password. A wrong entry shakes the field on `fastSpatialSpec` with a `Reject` haptic. Keypad: one `ButtonGroup` per row (weights, `animateWidth`), medium (56 dp) `FilledTonalButton` keys with `ButtonDefaults.shapesFor` press morph and `KeyboardTap` haptics, the submit key a filled `Button` with a check (a `LoadingIndicator` while checking), at most `sizes.pinPadMaxWidth`. Below: "Forgot passcode?" `TextButton` at the start, a fingerprint `FilledTonalIconButton` at the end. Compact height: header, dots and actions at the start, keypad at the end. Forgetting asks with an `AlertDialog` (destructive). Owner decision to confirm (2026-10-25): Passcode lock's "Unlock and privacy" adds "Unlock with screen lock" as a switch row (`SwitchItem`, off by default, Android 11+ with a screen lock set), its summary saying that anyone who knows the phone's PIN can then open the chats; while on, the system's prompt takes the phone's PIN, pattern or password too, without its own "Use passcode" button, and where the phone has no strong biometrics the button shows the PIN symbol (`ic_pin`, "Unlock with your screen lock") instead of the fingerprint |
| Locked chats | `docs/adr/0029-locked-chats.md`. **Opening one**: in the conversation's place the lock screen's passcode entry (title "This chat is locked", "Enter your PIN to open your locked chats"), with "Cancel" (`TextButton`, back) at the start instead of "Forgot passcode?" and the fingerprint button at the end; the system's biometric prompt shows by itself once after each lock; a right passcode buzzes `Confirm`. **The page**: Settings → Privacy and security → "Locked chats" (`ic_lock`, in the security group after Passcode lock), the same entry titled "Locked chats" until they are open; then a settings page with "Lock a chat" (`ic_add` in the group's tonal circle) first and each locked chat (40 dp avatar, the title as user content, "3 unread messages" as supporting text) with an "Unlock" `TextButton` named with it, a tap opening the chat; a footer saying what locking hides and that it stays on this device. Without the passcode lock, one row to it saying it is needed. "Lock a chat" opens the privacy exceptions' picker sheet (`PickerParts.kt`): the main list's and the archive's chats as toggleable rows, "Done" locking them. **The chat list**: locked chats never show in it, its folders or the archive row; at the end of the main list, a row as the archive row's (`ic_lock` on the neutral placeholder pair, "Locked chats" in `titleMedium` over "Open with your passcode" in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`), disabled in selection mode, never naming a chat or a count. **Notifications**: one "New message" in the Private chats channel, public on the lock screen as it names nothing, no avatar, shortcut or actions, alerting only for a new message, opening the page |
| Empty states | `EmptyState` (`core:ui`): a `MaterialShapes` illustration (soft shapes; errors `Cookie4Sided`, never `Burst`) + `headlineSmallEmphasized` + `bodyMedium` + a `Button` with `ButtonDefaults.shapes()` where there is a next step (the chat list's and the Contacts tab's: "New chat"; errors: "Try again"). The illustration's shape, not its icon, turns an eighth of a turn every 6 s on `slowSpatialSpec`, in its graphics layer, resting between turns; it stays still with reduced motion |

### 6.1 Floating navigation bar (compact windows)

On compact windows the primary navigation is a floating pill of tabs: it takes less height than a navigation bar and
keeps the tabs within thumb's reach. Search opens from the Chats tab's header (owner decision 2026-11-06). Material has no
component for it; the nearest, the floating toolbar, is meant for page actions. Pingogram builds the pattern from
Material parts (`app/.../ui/PingogramNavigationBar.kt`):

- **Container**: a 56 dp pill (`sizes.floatingNavigationBar`): a `Surface` in the floating
  toolbar's shape, colors (`standardFloatingToolbarColors()`, `surfaceContainer`) and level-3 elevation, holding the
  tabs with `spacing.small` around them (40 dp indicators), `FloatingToolbarDefaults.ScreenOffset` (16 dp) from the
  window sides and above the system navigation bar. Not `HorizontalFloatingToolbar` itself, which is at least 64 dp
  tall.
- **Tabs**: one per top-level destination, `Role.Tab` in a `selectableGroup`. The selected tab shows
  its filled icon and its label (`labelLargeEmphasized`) on a full-pill `secondaryContainer`
  indicator in `onSecondaryContainer`; the others show their label (`labelLarge`) in the toolbar's content color
  (owner decision 2026-11-07, as before 2026-11-06). Where the labels do not fit (narrow phones, large font scales:
  measured with the New chat button's room kept, never truncated), the others show their outlined icon instead, named
  by the label for TalkBack. This deviates on purpose from Material's navigation bar, which wants an icon and a label
  on every item, because the floating bar is a pattern built from Material parts rather than a Material component. Tapping the
  selected tab scrolls
  its screen back to the top instead of navigating (the rail's items too): the shell reports it through
  `LocalTabReselection` (`core:ui`), and a tab's screen follows with `ScrollToTopOnReselect` (the chat list).
- **Motion**: the indicator fades in on `defaultEffectsSpec` and grows along one axis from the tab's center on
  `fastSpatialSpec`, as Material's navigation indicator does (both read in the draw or layout phase); the icon slides
  in with `fastSpatialSpec` + `fastEffectsSpec`; the bar slides away on screens pushed above a tab
  and back on return (`defaultSpatialSpec` in, `fastSpatialSpec` out), by its own height, fading on the
  matching effects springs, in its graphics layer (`FloatingNavigationBar`). It keeps the tab below the
  pushed screen selected on the way, and stays composed while hidden (not placed, not in the semantics
  tree), so coming back does not compose it again.
- **New chat**: on the Chats tab the round "New chat" button sits `spacing.small` beside the pill (§6 "New chat"); the
  pill sits in the same place whether it shows or not: centred, or as far toward the start as leaves the button its
  room.
- **Fade**: on every tab the content's end fades into what it sits on behind the bar (owner decisions 2026-11-06 and
  2026-11-07; Contacts and Settings home added at the owner's request, 2026-11-07): the Chats and Contacts tabs' lists
  into their sheet's `surface`, Settings home into its page's color (`settingsPageColor()`). A gradient from
  transparent to that color that reaches it at 70% of its height, as tall as the bar's room
  (`LocalNavigationBarInsets`) and the bar's height again (`sizes.floatingNavigationBar`), so the rows fade out above
  the bar rather than under it; with AMOLED it fades to black. Drawn with `drawWithCache` over the content
  (`BottomFade`, `core:ui`), never a blur; it takes no touches, and without the bar (rail windows) it takes no height.
- **Layout**: the bar floats over the top-level screens, which draw behind it. Their `Scaffold` uses
  `screenContentWindowInsets()` (`core:ui`), which adds `LocalNavigationBarInsets` (the bar, its
  margin below and the same margin above), so lists end clear of the bar. The insets depend on the
  window only, never on the destination: changing that static local recomposes every screen.
  Screens pushed above a tab, where the bar hides, do not read it.
- **Stepping aside**: a top-level screen that shows a floating toolbar of its own in the bar's place
  (the chat list's selection toolbar, so the two never stack) hides the bar with
  `HideNavigationBar(hidden)` (`core:ui`, honoured by `PingogramApp`). The bar returns as soon as the
  screen stops asking or leaves the composition; `LocalNavigationBarInsets` stays meanwhile, and the
  toolbar sits where the bar was (`floatingToolbarInsets()`).
- Medium and wider windows keep the `WideNavigationRail`, and so do windows of compact height (phones in landscape,
  tabletop), where height is scarce. The bar is for compact-width windows only.

## 7. Layout

- `Scaffold` per screen; `contentWindowInsets`, `innerPadding`; `enableEdgeToEdge()`;
  transparent system bars; `viewport-fit`-style safe handling of display cutouts.
- Spacing scale 4/8/12/16/24/32 dp (Material's 2026 spacing system is built on the same 8 dp base); margins
  16 dp compact, 24 dp medium+, applied on every screen. Feature code reads them as tokens from
  `MaterialTheme.spacing` (`extraSmall`…`extraExtraLarge`, `windowMarginCompact`, `windowMarginMedium`), never as
  `dp` literals; `windowMargin()` (`core:ui`) picks the margin for the window, read once per screen. The chat list's
  title, connection pill and folder tabs keep it, so on medium+ windows they line up with the rows' content (rows sit
  `spacing.small` in from the sheet, their content the list item's padding further in). The conversation keeps its
  12 dp bubble inset at every width (Density, below).
- Breakpoints (Material's name since May 2026 for window size classes), via `currentWindowAdaptiveInfoV2()`, which
  reports large and extra-large widths:
  - **Compact** (< 600 dp): single pane + floating navigation bar (§6.1).
  - **Medium** (600–839 dp): single pane + collapsed rail.
  - **Expanded** (840–1199 dp): `ListDetailPaneScaffold` for chats | conversation and for Settings home | page.
    Global search docks in the chats' list pane (§6).
    The list is a fixed pane of 360–412 dp and the detail is flexible. Each pane is its own rounded container
    (§2.2), and a `VerticalDragHandle` resizes them (`paneExpansionState`, anchors 360 dp, 412 dp and a centered
    split), keeping the width across rotation and process death. Both panes have top bars of the same height.
  - **Large** (1200–1599 dp): as expanded, with the rail expanded.
  - **Extra-large** (≥ 1600 dp): as large, and the Chats tab opens a profile or a group's or channel's info page in a
    third pane beside the conversation (list | conversation | page), in its own rounded container, the conversation
    taking the width left; until one opens, the list and the conversation share the window. The page's pane rests at
    412 dp and a second drag handle, between it and the conversation, moves it between 360 dp, 412 dp and a third of
    the window. Back closes the page first. The page lays itself out as in a window of its width (one pane).
  - **Compact height** (phones in landscape): the rail instead of the floating bar (§6.1).

  `SupportingPaneScaffold` for profile/shared media. Canonical layouts only.
- Auth on medium+ widths and on compact heights: two panes, the step's headline and illustration at the start and
  its form with the actions at the end, so the primary action is always visible. Phones in landscape are medium wide;
  a window that is compact in both (split screen) keeps the compact layout, as two panes would be too narrow. The
  form pane scrolls above its actions when it does not fit. The QR step's code is its form (end pane), its
  instructions go with the headline. Compact portrait: one column that scrolls, the actions pinned above the keyboard
  (at 150% font scale and more they follow the content).
- Pane behaviour on foldables: `FoldingFeature`-aware hinge avoidance (`material3-adaptive`).
- **Density** (owner, 2026-10-19; reworded 2026-10-29: the app must be no bigger than Material's component sizes):
  - Use Material's component sizes, not larger ones: small app bars on dense lists, 40 dp buttons and icon buttons
    (48 dp touch targets), two-line list items at 72 dp.
  - Chat rows: 48 dp avatars (`sizes.avatarRow`) and one preview line.
  - Composer: a 72 dp bar at one line, a 56 dp pill and a 56 dp round button.
  - Chrome: a 56 dp top bar on the archive, and the tabs' large headers (Chats, Contacts, Settings home) collapsing to
    56 dp once the content scrolls, where the dense-list rule applies (owner decisions 2026-11-06 and 2026-11-07: a
    large header at the top of each tab), a 48 dp folder row, a 56 dp
    floating navigation bar with the round 56 dp "New chat" button beside it.
  - Bubbles: 12 dp side padding and one time per group.
  - Check: on a 412 × 915 dp phone at 100% font the chat list shows at least nine rows once its header collapsed, and a
    one-line message bubble is at most 40 dp tall.
- Touch targets ≥ 48 dp (`Modifier.minimumInteractiveComponentSize()`); 8 dp min spacing
  between targets.
- RTL: `start/end`, mirrored icons where M3 says so (`autoMirrored`).

## 8. Iconography & imagery

- **Material Symbols Rounded**, weight 400, filled for selected nav items; import as vector
  drawables from the Material Symbols site with the `ROND`-matching rounded style. Size 24 dp.
- Launcher icon: adaptive (foreground / background / **monochrome** layers), themed icon
  support; splash via `core-splashscreen` using the same vector. Notification small icon:
  monochrome 24 dp glyph.
- Splash screen: the icon (static; the owner's artwork has no animated version) on the white icon circle, on the
  `background` of the theme the app starts in. The manifest's theme has the system's theme mode with dynamic color
  (Android 12+: the wallpaper's neutral palette, the platform's background roles from 14; brand colors before 12); on
  Android 13+ `MainActivity` hands the system the splash theme of the current Appearance settings
  (`SplashScreen.setSplashScreenTheme`, `SplashScreenThemes.kt`: theme mode, dynamic or brand, AMOLED black), which it
  shows from the next start on. Before 13 the splash cannot know the settings: the app has not read them yet. It
  leaves by fading out on `defaultEffectsSpec` while its icon grows on `defaultSpatialSpec`
  (`setOnExitAnimationListener`, the expressive motion scheme outside composition, scaled by the animator duration
  scale), over the app's first frame.
- The owner's artwork lives in `art/` (SVG, the source); the app's vectors are converted from it:
  `app` `ic_launcher_*` (the mark `#2156C4` on white, the icon's own colors, also on the splash
  screen's icon circle), `core:notifications` `ic_notification` and `core:ui` `ic_pingogram` (the
  mark). In the UI the mark is one-color and tinted with theme roles (Welcome: `onPrimaryContainer` on
  its `primaryContainer` shape; About: `onPrimary` on its `primary` badge), so it follows dynamic color.
- App shortcuts (the launcher icon's long press): adaptive icons, a Material Symbol (edit, search, bookmark) at 30 dp
  on the 108 dp layer in the brand's light `onPrimaryContainer` on `primaryContainer`, with a monochrome layer for
  themed icons. Launchers draw them outside the app, so they cannot follow its theme.
- Avatars: circles, photos and placeholders alike, but for forums' rounded squares (§4, `AvatarShape.ofChat`);
  `MaterialShapes` only for states (the selection check, calls).
- Images: inside bubbles they take the bubble's shape (§4); `extraLarge` in media viewer thumbnails.

## 9. Accessibility (Material + Android standards)

- Contrast ≥ 4.5:1 text, ≥ 3:1 large text/icons/UI boundaries in all contrast levels.
- `contentDescription` on every icon-only control; decorative images `null`; merged semantics
  per message bubble ("{sender}, {time}: {content}, {status}"); `customActions` for swipe
  equivalents; `LiveRegion` for connection state (the chat list's pill, then "Connected" once when it is back, held
  briefly by a polite live region behind its top bar and nothing at a start that finds it connected; the conversation's
  title while it says the connection's state); `role` set on custom toggles.
- Font scale to 200% (non-linear on API 34+) without truncation; no fixed heights on text.
- Text direction: content from people or Telegram (messages and captions, link previews, chat titles and previews,
  names, usernames, sponsored messages, formatted times and dates) takes the direction of its first strong character
  (`TextStyle.userContent`, `TextDirection.Content`, `core:ui`), as Android's `TextView` does, so English keeps its
  punctuation at its end in a right-to-left layout and Hebrew reads right to left in a left-to-right one; its default
  `TextAlign.Start` then follows the paragraph. Content that may have no letters (phone numbers, coordinates) reads left
  to right without one (`numericContent`, `TextDirection.ContentOrLtr`). Pingogram's own strings keep the layout's
  direction. A name or other text from people inside one of them ("Grace is typing…", "Forwarded from …", service
  messages, "Alice:" before a preview, "Forwarded to …", a phone number in a sign-in step) is wrapped in Unicode
  first-strong isolates where the string is built (`bidiIsolated`, `core:ui`), so its direction never spills into the
  words and punctuation around it. Such a string then takes its own language's direction, not the layout's (or English
  strings in a right-to-left layout would put the name on the wrong side): its text uses `userContent` (the
  conversation's typing status, service messages, "Forwarded from …", chat list previews), or the whole string is
  isolated too where its text keeps the layout's direction (snackbars, dialogs, sign-in steps, search's "No results
  for …"). Text shown on its own is never isolated: that would hide its first letter from `userContent`.
- Keyboard & mouse: focus order, `Tab` navigation, Enter to send (Ctrl+Enter configurable),
  hover states on large screens. What works now:
  - **Focus**: keyboard focus shows Material 3 Expressive's inset focus ring (§2.3). A message row takes the focus
    where the keyboard moves it, not by touch, and its ring follows the bubble's own shape (`bubbleKeys`,
    `bubbleFocusRing`).
  - **Message actions**: Enter, the Menu key, Shift+F10 or a right click open them in the floating menu (§6 "Message
    actions"). The menu stays anchored to the lifted message, also for a right click, as message, reactions and menu
    move as one. It takes the focus, the keyboard moving on to its first reaction, keeps the focus while it shows, and
    Esc closes it, giving the focus back to the message. In selection mode, Enter selects or deselects.
  - **Senders**: in groups, the sender's name and avatar are buttons ("Open profile") with Material's pressed, hover
    and focus states, on the same 48 dp targets.
  - **Conversation**: Ctrl+F opens the search in the chat. Esc leaves selection mode, or else closes the search. Tab
    in the composer moves the focus on rather than typing a tab.
  - **Chats list-detail** (expanded and wider): Tab goes through the list pane, then the conversation's top bar, its
    messages (newest first, as the reverse list places them) and the composer. A chat opened from the list with Enter
    gives its composer the focus, so typing can start at once; a chat without a composer leaves the focus on
    the row. Esc in the conversation, with nothing in it to close, gives the focus back to the list at that row, and
    the chat stays open.
  - **Media viewer**: Left and Right turn the pages (mirrored in right-to-left layouts), +, − and 0 zoom the photo in,
    out and back to fitted, the wheel with Ctrl zooms around the pointer, Space plays or pauses a video, Esc closes.
  - **Stories viewer**: Left and Right show the previous and next story (mirrored in right-to-left layouts), Space
    pauses and resumes, Esc closes.
  - **Hover**: the chat list rows, the conversation's title, the pinned and translate bars, bot buttons and the media
    viewer's toolbar use Material's clickables and buttons, which show the hover state.
  - **Send key** (`docs/audit/accessibility.md` A2, `docs/audit/feature-gaps-octogram.md` P1): Appearance → Chats has
    two switch rows (`ComposerSettingsRepository`, `SendKeys`). "Send with Enter on a keyboard", on by default: a
    hardware keyboard's Enter sends, Shift+Enter and Ctrl+Enter start a new line; off, Ctrl+Enter sends and Enter starts
    a new line (the "Send with Ctrl+Enter" of before, which carries over). "Send with Enter on the screen keyboard", off
    by default: on, the on-screen keyboard's action key reads "Send" (`ImeAction.Send`) and sends; off, its Enter starts
    a new line. The conversation's route hands them to its fields (`LocalSendKeys`); other fields keep the defaults.
    Where the rows live is the owner's to confirm.
- Reduced motion & reduced transparency respected; no flashing content > 3 Hz.
- Run TalkBack + Accessibility Scanner before marking any screen done.

## 10. Dark theme and contrast rules

- Dark uses the M3 dark scheme (not inverted light); surfaces follow the tonal ladder;
  Google Sans Flex at `GRAD` 0 (25 with high contrast, §3.1); images get a 0.9 `ColorFilter` dim only in media-heavy lists.
- The conversation counts as one: in dark theme, photos, videos, GIFs, video messages and albums in it (their
  minithumbnails too, and the media of sponsored messages) are dimmed to 90% of their brightness with a color matrix
  (`pingogramColors.mediaColorFilter`, null in light theme), so bright pictures do not glare among dark surfaces.
  Stickers, custom emoji and avatars are not dimmed: they are artwork or identity, not photos. The media viewer shows
  media undimmed, at full brightness on its `scrim`, as the place to look at them. Link previews show no image yet;
  when they do, theirs is dimmed too.
- Medium/high contrast schemes change colors only; typography weight is unchanged (Material
  rule). High contrast applies the optional `GRAD +25` for readability (§3.1): heavier strokes without wider text or
  another weight.

## 11. Don'ts

- Don't mix Material 2 (`androidx.compose.material.*`), AppCompat widgets or custom-drawn
  "material-like" components.
- Don't hard-code colors, sizes, durations, easing, or fonts.
- Don't reproduce Telegram's bubble styling, icon set, sounds, or layout; this is a
  Material 3 Expressive app.
- Don't use shadows for hierarchy; use tonal surfaces.
- Don't ship skeleton shimmers; use `LoadingIndicator` or tonal placeholders.
- Don't add `-opt-in` compiler flags for experimental APIs project-wide; one `@OptIn` per composable.

## 12. Per-screen M3 Expressive audit (must pass before "done")

- [ ] Only `MaterialTheme.*` tokens; `PingogramHardcodedToken` detekt rule passes
- [ ] Color roles per §2.2; light, dark, dynamic, AMOLED, medium & high contrast screenshots OK
- [ ] Google Sans Flex styles per §3.3; `opsz` equals size; emphasized styles where specified; `GRAD` 0 (25 with high
      contrast)
- [ ] Density per §7: Material's component sizes; the screen is no bigger than Material's component sizes make it
- [ ] Shapes from the scale or `MaterialShapes`; at least one meaningful shape morph where the screen has a primary action
- [ ] All animations use `motionScheme` specs; shared-element / container transform where listed in §5
- [ ] Components match the §6 mapping (no custom lookalikes)
- [ ] Edge-to-edge, insets, predictive back, adaptive layout at compact/medium/expanded/large and compact height
- [ ] Goldens for light, dark, AMOLED, high contrast, RTL and a 700 dp width
- [ ] Accessibility §9 checks pass; haptics per §5
- [ ] Reduced motion path verified
- [ ] Performance budget in `docs/PERFORMANCE.md` met (no jank in the screen's Macrobenchmark)

## 13. Pingogram's look

The look from the owner's design (`docs/adr/0035-experimental-ui.md`, owner decisions 2026-11-07), first an opt-in
"Experimental UI" and Pingogram's only look since the owner's decision of 2026-11-08: the switch is gone, and where a
line of §1–§12 and a line of this section differ, this section wins. The other look's code is gone; the rows of
§1–§12 are not all edited for it yet. Where the owner's design shows something the owner
has not decided, the line says "Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07)". The design's mock-ups use Roboto Flex as a
stand-in font and literal colors and pixel sizes: each is mapped here to color roles, the shape scale,
`MaterialShapes`, `spacing` and `sizes` tokens and the type scale (Google Sans Flex), never copied into feature code.

**How it is read**: there is one look, without a composition local or a branch (2026-11-08). Its state, events, keys,
content types, semantics and haptics stay those of §1–§12 unless a line below says otherwise.

### 13.1 No switch

| UI element | Pingogram's look |
|---|---|
| Settings home | No "Experimental UI" row or labs card (owner, 2026-11-08). The setting an earlier version kept (`appearance.experimental_ui`) is no longer read |

### 13.2 Shared parts (`core:ui`)

| Part | Experimental UI |
|---|---|
| Avatar shapes | By what the chat is (`AvatarShape.ofChat`, named by the view models, turned into a shape by the composable): people and bots a circle, groups `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided`, channels `MaterialShapes.Cookie6Sided` (the design's six soft lobes), Saved Messages `MaterialShapes.Clover4Leaf`, forums the rounded square of §4 (the design has no rule for them). The selected check morphs from the shape. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): it differs from the 2026-11-06 decision of circles for every chat |
| Avatar placeholders | Vivid fills (`MaterialTheme.pingogramColors.vividAvatars`, `VividAvatarPalette`) in place of §2.2's four container pairs, chosen by `id % 7`: orange, red, teal, blue, lilac, gold and slate, the same in light and dark theme (identity, like a photo). `tools/theme` generates them: a fixed-hue tonal palette at the hue, chroma and tone of the design's fill (tones 70–86), the text at tone 10, which keeps 4.5:1 or more on every fill (`ThemeContrastTest`). On a selected row the placeholder stays `primary` / `onPrimary` |
| Accent | The user's color theme's roles: the design's coral fills are `primary` / `primaryContainer`, its gold FAB, microphone and labs card `tertiaryContainer`. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): Settings → Appearance → Theme gains "Coral", a basic color theme generated from the design's coral (#FF8C7A) with `SchemeContent`, the variant closest to the design (the coral as the light `primaryContainer`, a gold tertiary), so the experimental look can be seen as designed; it shows in the standard look's theme page too |
| Display type | `MaterialTheme.experimentalTypography` (`ExperimentalTypography`): emphasized Material styles in Google Sans Flex at `wdth` 115 for big titles (`displaySmallWide` the tabs' titles, `titleLargeWide` collapsed, `headlineSmallWide` the account card's name, `titleMediumWide` a conversation's title) and `wdth` 80 for big letters and numerals (`headlineLargeCondensed` the Contacts tab's section letters, `displaySmallCondensed`), each with `opsz` equal to its size and the grade of §3.1. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): §3 sets `wdth` 100 for every style |
| Floating navigation bar | `sizes.experimentalNavigationBar` (64 dp) tall, the selected tab a `primary` pill with its filled icon and label (`onPrimary`, `labelLargeEmphasized`), the other tabs their outlined icon only, named by their labels for screen readers; the floating toolbar's level-3 shadow and colors as §6.1. The insets of the screens follow its height (`floatingNavigationBarHeight`). Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the standard look shows labels where they fit |
| New chat | Beside the bar, a 64 dp rounded square (`sizes.experimentalFab`, the shape scale's `largeIncreased`) on `tertiaryContainer` / `onTertiaryContainer`, the filled edit symbol; the container transform into New chat starts from that shape. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the standard look's button is round |
| Bottom fade | `BottomFade` (now in `core:ui`, for every tab): `sizes.experimentalBottomFade` (132 dp, the design's) above the system navigation bar, full sheet color at 70%, over the list and under the bar |
| Tab header | `TabHeader`'s title in `displaySmallWide` (the size of `displaySmall`, the design's 40 sp class at its wide width), collapsing to `titleLargeWide` at the dense height as in the standard look; the status line `bodyMedium`; the actions 48 dp tonal circles (`sizes.headerAction`, `tabHeaderActionModifier`), `spacing.small` apart |

|---|---|
| Header | §13.2's tab header: "Chats" in `displaySmallWide`, the status line ("12 unread · 3 mentions", the unread part in `primary` and the emphasized weight) as in §6; search a 48 dp tonal circle; the user's avatar a 48 dp square with `large` corners on `primary` / `onPrimary` (the placeholder colors of a selected row; a photo fills it), the icon button pressing to `medium` corners, its long press the account menu as in §6 |
| Folder tabs | Chips apart (`spacing.small`) in the same fading horizontal scroll, whatever their number (no tab row past six): `ToggleButton`s, at rest `small` corners on `surfaceContainerHighest` with a `primary` / `onPrimary` count `Badge`, the selected one a full pill on `primary` / `onPrimary` with its count inverted (`onPrimary` / `primary`), pressing to `extraSmall`. Same folder logic, menus, `Role.Tab` semantics and tests as §6. "Edit folders" an `OutlinedIconButton` with `medium` corners (`small` when pressed) |
| Sheet | `extraLarge` top corners, as §6 |
| Archive row | Always the compact style: the archive symbol in `onSurfaceVariant` on a 40 dp (`sizes.avatarSmall`) tile with `medium` corners on `surfaceContainerHighest`, centred in the avatar's width, "Archived chats", and its unread chats as `bodyMedium` text in `onSurfaceVariant` at the end. A hidden row stays hidden. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the menu's "Make compact" / "Show chat names" changes nothing visible (the row is always compact; the choice is kept, and screen readers still hear the chats' names) |
| Pinned group | As §6 (the other stream's decisions): "Pinned" header, segmented rows on `surfaceContainer` |

Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the design's 76 dp rows with 52 dp avatars would show eight rows instead of nine on a 412 × 915 dp phone once the header collapsed, below §7's density check, so the rows keep Material's sizes |
| Row details | The avatar in the chat type's shape (§13.2, `AvatarShape.ofChat` with the chat's kind). Before the name the kind of chat: people (`ic_group`) for a group or forum, a megaphone (`ic_campaign`) for a channel, `sizes.inlineIconSmall` in `onSurfaceVariant`. Appearance → Chat list → "Chat type icons" (a switch row, `SwitchItem`, on by default, "Shows a symbol before the names of groups and channels", kept on the device, shown in the experimental look only; owner request 2026-11-07) hides them. The mute icon only while the chat is muted and nothing is unread. The time in `primary` and `labelSmallEmphasized` while the chat is unread and not muted. Before the preview the kind of message (`ChatPreview.Message.contentIcon`: photo, video, GIF, voice message, song, file, place, poll, link), outlined at `sizes.inlineIconSmall` in `onSurfaceVariant`. The "@" badge, muted badges on `surfaceVariant` and "999+" as §6. Swipes, selection, the long press, keys, content types and the recomposition rules as the standard row |
