| Header | The field and the chips on a `surfaceContainerLow` block with `extraLarge` bottom corners, over the results on `surfaceContainer` (the design's light block over its tinted page); no divider under the chips, the progress indicator keeping its place there. The field the search bar's `InputField` as a 56 dp pill on `surfaceContainerHighest`, `spacing.medium` in from the block's sides, back and clear inside it as in §6 |
| Top people | In the header block, between the field and the chips (`HeaderPeople`): their 56 dp (`sizes.avatarMedium`) avatars in the chat type's shapes (§13.2) over the names, in a `LazyRow`, one button per person for screen readers; all of them before a query, then only those whose name has a word starting with each word typed (`SearchViewModel.topPeople`, filtered on the device, locked chats left out), the row growing in and shrinking away (`expandVertically` / `shrinkVertically` on `defaultSpatialSpec`, effects-spring fades) as matches come and go. The suggestions list then leaves out its own "People" section. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): narrowing the top people by the words typed, as the board does not say whether they follow the query |
| Filter chips | The same `FilterChip`s and counts: selected a full pill on `primary` / `onPrimary`, the others outlined (the chip's own border) with `medium` corners on nothing, their labels in `onSurfaceVariant`, pressing to `small` |
| Chat rows | The segmented groups of §6, each chat row ending in a chevron (`ic_chevron_right`, `onSurfaceVariant`, decorative, mirrored right to left) where it has no remove button; avatars in the chat type's shapes |
| Message rows | The chat's name with the date at the end of its line (`labelSmall`), then two lines: the sender in `onSurface` and the emphasized weight, the text in `onSurfaceVariant`, each match of the query on a `primaryContainer` block with `small` corners in `onPrimaryContainer` and the emphasized weight, drawn from the text's layout as the conversation's search does (`textBlocks`, `withMatches`), so nothing moves. Screen readers hear the row as in §6 |

### 13.5 Conversation: top bar, pinned bar, jump buttons and composer (the owner's boards "Group chat", "Channel")

| UI element | Experimental UI |
|---|---|
| Top bar | Material's center-aligned top bar on `surfaceContainer`: back, then a centred block that is one heading and one button for screen readers ("Group info" / the profile, as §6): the chat's avatar (`sizes.avatarSmall`, in the chat type's shape, §13.2), or in a group up to five people who wrote last among the messages loaded (no request of its own) at `sizes.avatarExtraSmall`, overlapping by a third, each in a `sizes.avatarRing` ring of the bar's color; the name in `titleMediumEmphasized`; the status in `bodySmall` `onSurfaceVariant`, a group's online members after its count ("1,248 members · 36 online", the second part in `pingogramColors.online` and the emphasized weight, from td_api.tl `updateChatOnlineMemberCount`, which TDLib sends for open chats only, so it can come late or not at all); typing dots and the connection's state as §6. As tall as the avatar over the two lines (about 100 dp at 100% font), also in selection mode and for the search bar, so nothing moves. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the bar is taller than §7's 64 dp |
| Top bar actions | Private and secret chats, forums' topic lists and topics as §6 (search, the call button, the timer, the topic action). A group: search, then "More options" (`ic_more_vert`, a `FilledTonalIconButton`) with "Group info" in an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup`. A channel: a notifications toggle (`FilledTonalIconToggleButton`, `toggleableShapes()`, `ic_notifications` / `ic_notifications_off`, "Notifications" with its state for screen readers) in search's place, muting forever or unmuting (`ChatRepository.setMuted`), then "More options" with "Search in chat" and "Channel info". Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the menu, which §6 avoids in app bars, and the channel's toggle in search's place |
| Pinned bar | As §6, as a pill: full corners, at least `sizes.composerField` (56 dp) tall, the segments at its start, "Pinned · 6 of 6" in `labelMedium` `primary` over the message, close, then "All pinned messages" as a `primary` `FilledIconButton` at the end (where there are several) |
| Floating date pill | While the list scrolls, on `surfaceContainerHigh` with its text in `onSurface` and `labelLargeEmphasized`; the inline date separators stay as §6 |
| Jump buttons | Above the jump to the newest message (§6, unchanged), "Jump to mention" while unread messages mention the user (td_api.tl `chat.unread_mention_count`): a round `FloatingActionButton` (56 dp) in `primary` / `onPrimary` with "@" in `titleLargeEmphasized`, "Jump to mention, 2 unread mentions" for screen readers, growing in and out as the jump button does. A tap shows the oldest unread one (`ChatSearchRepository.oldestUnreadMention`: `searchChatMessages` with `searchMessagesFilterUnreadMention`) as a tapped quote's jump does; showing it reads it. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the brief puts the jump button above "Jump to mention", the board below it; the board's order is kept, so the jump button never moves as mentions come and go |
| Composer | The pill on `surfaceContainerHigh`. At its start the attach "+" in a `primary` circle (Material's default `FilledIconButton`, 40 dp in its 48 dp target) and, while the field is empty and the chat takes photos, a camera button (`FilledTonalIconButton`, `ic_photo_camera_filled`) that opens the camera app as the attach menu's "Camera" does; no gallery button (the attach menu's first option). The bot button, the emoji button, recording, the send menu and editing as §6. The send button a rounded square (`IconButtonDefaults.mediumSquareShape`, `primary`) beside the round microphone on `tertiaryContainer`. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the board's 60 dp pill, 44 dp buttons and 60 dp send and microphone are kept at Material's 56 dp and 40 dp (§7 density); the camera takes photos only, as a round video message needs the microphone button held |

### 13.6 Conversation: group bubbles (the owner's board "Group chat")

| UI element | Experimental UI |
|---|---|
| Sender hues | Incoming bubbles in group chats take their sender's hue: one of eight (red, orange, violet, green, teal, blue, pink, gold), Telegram's seven built-in accent colors mapping to the hues of their names (td_api.tl `user.accent_color_id` 0–6), any other sender by their user or chat id (`SenderHues.indexOf`), so a thousand members reuse eight hues: color only tells apart people talking near each other. `tools/theme` generates each hue's vivid fill (tone 90 light, 80 dark) and its text (tone 10), a barely tinted fill (tone 96 light, 15 dark, chroma 12 at most) for `onSurface` text, and an ink (tone 40 light, 80 dark) for names and reply stripes (`SenderHueArgb`, `PingogramColors.senderHues`); text keeps 4.5:1 on every fill and the ink on the tinted fill and on `surface`, in every theme and contrast level (`ThemeContrastTest`). Outgoing bubbles stay `primaryContainer`; selected bubbles `secondaryContainer`; channels and private chats are not colored |
| Group bubbles setting | Chat appearance → "Group bubbles", shown in the experimental look only: Automatic (the default), Vivid and Tinted as radio rows (`SegmentTick`), kept with the other chat settings. **Vivid**: the bubble on the hue's fill, its text, links, quotes and link previews in the fill's text color, the sender's name above the bubble in the hue's ink (`labelLarge`), on the first of their group. **Tinted**: the bubble on the tinted fill, the name inside in the hue's ink. **Automatic**: vivid in groups of up to 50 members, tinted in bigger ones (`GroupBubbles.isVivid`). Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the hues and the setting |
| Role labels | After a sender's name, "Owner", "Admin" or the member's own tag (td_api.tl `chatMemberStatus*`'s tag), from the owner and administrators asked for once per conversation (`GroupInfoRepository.members`, `MemberFilter.Administrators`; nothing per message), as a small full pill in the hue's ink with its text in the hue's tinted fill (`labelSmallEmphasized`); decorative, the bubble's description names the sender |
| Reply quotes | A stripe of `sizes.pinnedIndicator` at the quote's start in the replied sender's ink, its name in the same (on a vivid bubble both in the fill's text color); the standard look has no stripe |
| Mentions | A message that mentions the user (TDLib's unread mention, or a mention of the user by name) is ringed by a `sizes.avatarRing` (2 dp) `primary` border in its bubble's shape. Each mention of the user in its text or caption (by name, td_api.tl `textEntityTypeMentionName`, or by one of their usernames, `textEntityTypeMention`; `ChatItem.Bubble.myMentions`, worked out by the mapper) is a chip: a `primary` block with `small` corners per line it covers, drawn behind the text from its layout like code blocks (`textBlocks`, `TextBlock.MENTION`), its text in `onPrimary` and the style's heaviest weight, so no other glyph moves; added over the cached text only for bubbles with such a mention (`withMentionChips`), the other bubbles' texts untouched; within a hidden spoiler it stays hidden. Mentions of other people keep the link style. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): only the user's own mentions are chips, as on the board |
| Reactions | As §4: round badges overlapping the bubble's bottom edge with a `surface` ring, which the board's pill already matches |
| Typing | While someone types in a group, a row over the bottom of the messages, above the composer: their avatar (`avatarExtraSmall`), a small bubble on their hue's vivid fill with the typing dots (`TypingDots`, its timing), and what the top bar says ("Grace is typing", `labelMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`); the list leaves room for it below the newest message; it grows in and shrinks away on `defaultSpatialSpec`; decorative, as the top bar's title is the live region that announces it |
| Shouts | An incoming group message of one word of at most six characters, all its letters capitals and at least one letter, without formatting, link preview, reply or forward ("YES", "SHIP!"; `ChatItem.Bubble.isShout`, worked out by the mapper) shows without a bubble, as an emoji-only message does: the word in `experimentalTypography.displayLargeCondensed` (`displayLarge`'s size and line height at the condensed width, emphasized) set italic (the font's own slant), in its sender's ink (`onSurface` without a hue), the time and state below it on the surface. Screen readers hear the bubble as any other. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the rule (the board's: up to six characters, no space, in capitals), and Material's 57 sp `displayLarge` rather than the board's 96 sp, the largest size of the type scale |
| Wave | A group's service message for someone else joining or being added (`ChatItem.Service.canWave`; never the user's own joining) ends in "Wave" where the user can write and nothing is being selected: an extra-small `FilledTonalButton` (`ButtonDefaults.ExtraSmallContainerHeight`, `shapesFor` press morph, its text style, a 48 dp target) inside the pill, `spacing.small` after its text. A tap sends one of Telegram's greeting stickers (td_api.tl `getGreetingStickers`, picked at random) in reply to the join message, at once, as a sticker tapped in the panel goes (`AttachmentRepository.sendGreetingSticker`); a failure is a snackbar. The user's own tap only, never sent by itself. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): a random greeting sticker, with no picker |
| Checklists | The board's checklist bubble as §6 "Checklists": outgoing on the bubble's accent colors, incoming on the sender's hue (vivid or tinted as "Group bubbles" says), its round checks filled with the hue's accent, "Add task" on the bubble's block color, the completers' 24 dp avatars in their shapes (§13.2) |
| Polls (boards 4a, 4b, 5a, 5b) | As §6 "Polls" and "New poll", their owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10) written there: the kicker in capitals, as the board sets it; incoming polls on the sender's hue as every group bubble here |

### 13.7 Conversation: channel posts and file albums (the owner's board "Channel")

| UI element | Experimental UI |
|---|---|
| Channel posts | The posts of the channel the conversation is on a warm container, `secondaryContainer` / `onSecondaryContainer` (`PingogramColors.channelPostBubble`; the board's peach with the Coral theme), their links in its accent, their quotes and link previews on a block of their text over the fill, in its text color; the shapes of §4 (large corners, the joined corner small). Selected posts as §2.2. The share button as §6 |
| Channel headline | A channel post whose first line is at most 32 characters, wholly bold, and followed by more text (`ChatItem.Bubble.headlineLength`) sets that line as its headline: a paragraph of its own in `experimentalTypography.displaySmallCondensed` (36 sp at the condensed width, emphasized), the rest of the post in the message text style below it, added over the cached text last (`withHeadline`, which gives the line break after the headline to the paragraph's end). The post's own bold first line, never text Pingogram adds. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the rule, and Material's 36 sp `displaySmall` rather than the board's 48 sp; the board's kicker above it ("DEBUG BUILD") is left out, as it is no part of the post |
| Views | In a channel post's meta, before the time: the visibility symbol (`ic_visibility`, `sizes.inlineIconSmall`) and how many saw it (td_api.tl `interactionInfo.view_count`) in the language's digits ("12,480"), in the meta's color; decorative |
| Unread divider | Two `primary` lines (`sizes.avatarRing` thick) around a `primary` / `onPrimary` pill (`labelMediumEmphasized`) counting the unread messages loaded below it: "4 unread posts" in a channel, "4 unread messages" elsewhere; a heading for screen readers, as §6 |
| File albums | Files sent together already show as a group of bubbles joined by their small corners (§4); each file's download is a button in `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided` filled with the bubble's text color, the download symbol in its fill; while it downloads, the determinate wavy ring around a cancel button as §6 |
| Paid posts and giveaways | As §6 "Paid media" and "Giveaways": a channel post not paid for keeps its blurred mosaic and note on the channel's warm container, the giveaway's prize card its `tertiaryContainer`, and the chips, tiles and "Learn more" the post's block color; the views count shows in their meta as in any post |

### 13.8 Contacts (the owner's board "Contacts")

| UI element | Experimental UI |
|---|---|
| Header | §13.2's tab header: "Contacts" in `displaySmallWide` over "248 on Telegram · 12 online" while expanded (`bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, the second part in `pingogramColors.online` and the emphasized weight, left out while nobody is online; `ContactsContent.Contacts.count` and `online`, from what TDLib reports, no polling); the actions 48 dp tonal circles: search, which puts the focus in the list's search field (while it shows), then Recent calls. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): Recent calls stays in the header rather than joining the group below |
| Contact actions | Under the header, in place of its QR buttons and the list's "Invite friends": Invite, My QR and Scan as a connected group of `FilledTonalButton`s sharing the width (`ButtonGroupDefaults` connected shapes and their press morph, `ConnectedSpaceBetween` apart), each its icon and label (`labelLarge`, one line; from 150% font the label alone), inside the window margin, `spacing.large` above the sheet; screen readers hear the group as a pane, "Contact actions". It folds away with the header as the list scrolls (its height and opacity follow the header's collapse in the layout and draw phases). Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): Material's default 40 dp buttons rather than the board's 52 dp (§7 density) |
| Add contact | Beside the floating navigation bar on the Contacts tab, where "New chat" sits on the Chats tab: the same 64 dp rounded square (`largeIncreased`) on `tertiaryContainer` with the add-person symbol, "Add contact" for screen readers, fading in and out with the tab; on rail windows the rail's header holds it (a round FAB, extended with its label on the expanded rail). The list's "Add contact" row is left out. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07) |
| Contacts sync | Its offer as a filled `Card` on `surfaceContainerHigh` with `extraLarge` corners, inset like the rows: the contacts icon on a `MaterialShapes.Flower` badge (`sizes.leadingIconContainer`) in `tertiaryContainer`, the title in `titleSmallEmphasized` over the text in `bodyMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, "Turn on" as a filled `Button` and a dismiss `IconButton` ("Not now"); from 150% font the two buttons go under the text. Its paused state and its row while on stay as §6 |
| Online now | Below sync, while TDLib reports any contact online: "Online now" in `titleSmallEmphasized` `primary` (a heading) with their count in `labelMedium` `onSurfaceVariant`, over a keyed `LazyRow` of the online contacts in the list's order: each their avatar (`sizes.avatarMedium`, a circle as people's are in §13.2, with the online dot) over their first name in `labelMedium` (user content, one line), `large` ripple bounds; a tap opens their chat; screen readers hear one button per person ("Ada Lovelace, online"). The row leaves while nobody is online. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the board gives each person a playful shape; people keep circles, as everywhere in §13.2 |
| Section letters | The letter in `headlineLargeCondensed` `primary`, then a line in `outlineVariant` (`DividerDefaults.Thickness`) to the end, on the sheet's `surface` (sticky, a heading). "Invite to Telegram" keeps §6's header, as it is no letter |
| Rows | Material's two-line list item at 72 dp, as §6. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the board's 68 dp rows sit below Material's two-line item |
| Fast-scroll index | The letters in a pill on `surfaceContainer` (full corners, `spacing.small` beyond the letters, drawn behind them from their measured place), the letter of the section at the list's top on a `primary` dot in `onPrimary` (the touched one while touched); its touch, haptics and semantics as §6 |

### 13.9 Settings home (the owner's board "Settings")

| UI element | Experimental UI |
|---|---|
| Header | §13.2's tab header: "Settings" in `displaySmallWide`, its one action a 48 dp tonal circle, search |
| Settings search | The search button turns the header into the search bar's `InputField` in its pill on `surfaceContainerHighest` (the page is the bar's own `surfaceContainerHigh`), at most `contentMaxWidth` inside the window margin: back ("Close search") at its start, a clear button while it holds text, "Search settings", the focus when the button opened it (not after a rotation). The page then lists what one registry of Settings home's entries (`SettingsHomeEntries.kt`: every group's entries, Edit profile and the user's QR code) holds in its titles and summaries, every word of the query, ignoring case and accents, as the home's rows in one group; "No settings found" in `bodyLarge` `onSurfaceVariant` (a polite live region). Search also finds the pages above them (`SettingsSearchEntries.kt`, one per `SettingsSubPage`: Theme, Chat appearance, Double-tap to react, Swipe actions, Chats shown first, trending and archived stickers, Emoji, reaction notifications, automatic download per network, Proxy, Network usage, the licenses, each privacy setting, Who can message me, Blocked users, Websites, Two-step verification, Passcode lock, Locked chats, Auto-delete messages and Delete account), each with its own title, its parent page's title as its summary and its parent's icon, so a word of the parent ("privacy") finds them too, after the parent. A tap opens the page; a page above another opens with its parent below it, pushed both on one pane, in the detail pane's own back stack on two, so back returns through the parent. Back and system back close search, which forgets the query. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the parent's title as the summary of these results, and which pages are listed (pages that need a chat, a person or a link to open, such as a folder's editor or a proxy's form, are not) |
| Account card | A card on `primaryContainer` / `onPrimaryContainer` with `extraLargeIncreased` corners, `spacing.large` inside: the avatar (`sizes.experimentalAccountAvatar`, 76 dp) in `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided` with a decorative camera badge (`avatarBadge`) in `tertiaryContainer`; the name in `headlineSmallWide` (two lines, a heading), "@username" in `bodyMediumEmphasized`, a "Premium" label for Telegram Premium subscribers (`labelMediumEmphasized` on `tertiaryContainer`, never an offer), and the phone number masked (`maskPhoneNumber`) with an eye `IconButton` ("Show phone number" / "Hide phone number") that shows it on this screen; while "Hide phone numbers" is on it stays masked, without the button. The demo shows its "Demo" label there. Then "Edit profile" (a filled `Button` in the card's inverse colors, `labelLargeEmphasized`) and "QR code" (`FilledTonalButton`) sharing the row, and, where the device has other accounts or can add one (not in the demo), a `FilledTonalIconButton` ("Switch or add account") opening an expressive `DropdownMenuPopup`: the other accounts (avatar, name, their unread chats in a `primary` `Badge`), then "Add account" in a second group. The other accounts' group below the card is left out, as the menu holds it |
| Groups | `titleSmallEmphasized` `primary` headers over segmented rows (Material's two-line item, at least 72 dp): "Make it yours" (Appearance, Chat folders, Stickers and emoji), "Alerts and privacy" (Notifications, Privacy and security, Data and storage with the storage the account's files take as its value), "More" (Recent calls where the app provides it, Devices with "3 active sessions", Language with the app's language as its value), and "App" (Power saving, About), then "Log out" on its own. Every page the standard Settings home opens stays reachable. Each row's icon sits on a `leadingIconContainer` badge in `Cookie9Sided`, `Flower`, `Clover4Leaf` and `Sunny` in turn, filled with the vivid avatar colors (§13.2); values in `labelLarge` `primary`. The values come from `SettingsHomeViewModel` (`getActiveSessions`, `getStorageStatisticsFast` once, the language as it changes), which only the experimental look asks for. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-07): the grouping, Material's 40 dp leading badge rather than the board's 44 dp, and "App" for the rest |
| Pages | Settings pages keep their look |

### 13.10 Settings pages (the owner's boards "Settings pages · Experimental UI")

Every page above Settings home draws itself from the shared parts (`SettingsPageFrame`, `SettingsGroup`, `SettingsRow`,
`PageItem`), so the look below reaches every page, those the boards show and those they leave out; no setting goes for
the new look. Pages the boards draw with cards of their own get them on top of this in the rows below.

| UI element | Experimental UI |
|---|---|
| Top bar | The medium bar's back and actions; the title in `displaySmallWide` on one line (smaller rather than cut at large font scales or for long titles), collapsing to `titleLargeWide`, as the tabs' headers; the expanded bar as tall as the collapsed row, that line and `spacing.medium` below it |
| Groups | `spacing.large` apart (the standard look's `spacing.extraLarge`), their headers and footers as §6 |
| Row icons | On a `leadingIconContainer` badge in `Cookie9Sided`, `Flower`, `Clover4Leaf` and `Sunny` in turn down a group, filled with the vivid avatar colors (§13.2) in turn, the group's title setting where its colors start, as Settings home's; a destructive row's icon keeps its `errorContainer` circle, a selected row's its `primary` one |
| Rows that open a page | End in Material Symbols' `chevron_right` in `onSurfaceVariant` (`PageChevron`), decorative, mirrored right to left |
| Destructive rows | Their text and icon in `error` as §6, on their container with a 12% tint of `error` over it, so they read as a warning at a glance |
| Appearance | "Reset" at the top bar's end, a `FilledTonalButton` that puts the look's settings (theme mode, AMOLED black, colors, contrast, the chats' text size, corners, wallpaper and group bubbles) back to their defaults, with "Undo" in its snackbar; then the sample conversation (`ChatPreview`) in a segment; "Light and dark" with System, Light and Dark as cards sharing the segment's width, each a sample of the app (title line, bubbles, composer) in the theme's light or dark scheme, System split along the diagonal, the chosen one ringed in `primary` (`sizes.avatarRing`, `largeIncreased` corners) with a check on `primary`, its name in `labelLargeEmphasized` (one radio group, `SegmentTick`), then AMOLED black; "Colors" with every color theme as a chip (`ThemeChip`, the chosen one a cookie) in a row that scrolls sideways, the wallpaper's colors first where offered, the chosen theme's name in the emphasized weight and whether the wallpaper's colors are on, and "All themes" opening the Theme page, then the contrast; "Chat list" as the standard page; "Chats" headed by the message text size's slider and "Group bubbles" (§13.6: Automatic, Vivid, Tinted as connected `ToggleButton`s over what the chosen one does), then its standard rows. Group bubbles live here only (no longer on Chat appearance). Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-08): the contrast keeps its connected buttons rather than the board's value pill |
| Notifications | While Android blocks Pingogram's notifications, a banner (`SettingsBannerCard`) in place of the warning row: the bell-off symbol on a `Flower` badge in `onErrorContainer`, "Notifications are off" in `titleMediumEmphasized` over why in `bodyMedium`, and "Turn on" as a filled `Button` in the inverse colors, on `errorContainer` with `extraLarge` corners. "Show settings for": Private, Groups and Channels as connected buttons (`ChoiceRow`), which pick the one kind of chat whose group shows below (kept across rotation and process death); every other group as the standard page |
| Power saving | A status card (`SettingsStatusCard`) on `primaryContainer` with `extraLarge` corners: the leaf symbol on a `Cookie9Sided` badge (`sizes.headlineBadge`) in `onPrimaryContainer`, "Right now" in `labelMediumEmphasized` over whether Battery Saver is on (`titleMediumEmphasized`), what the chosen mode does (`bodyMedium`) and, after a dot, whether it saves power now (`labelLargeEmphasized`), one item for screen readers; then "Turn on" through `ChoiceRow` (its radio rows on a phone, where "With Battery Saver" does not fit a third of the row), then "While on" as the standard page. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-08): the parts' switches keep no icons, as the standard rows have none |
| Rows that open a page, everywhere | `SettingsRow(opensPage = true)` ends in the chevron where it has nothing else at its end: the privacy settings, "Messages", 2-step verification, the login email, as `PageItem` rows do |
| Privacy and security | "Privacy checkup" first, while any privacy setting is open to everybody: on `primaryContainer` with `extraLarge` corners, "Privacy checkup" in `labelMediumEmphasized` over how many in `experimentalTypography.displaySmallCondensed` beside "things anyone on Telegram can see" (`titleMediumEmphasized`, one heading), those settings' names as pills (`labelMediumEmphasized`, a 12% `onPrimaryContainer` fill, full corners, wrapping), then "Review" (a filled `Button` in the inverse colors, opening the first of them) and "Not now" (a `TextButton`, hiding the card until the page opens again). Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-08): "Not now" lasts until the page closes, not for good; the settings' choices keep their supporting text rather than the board's value pills, which would squeeze long names |
| Data and storage | First, once the storage used is read, a card on the rows' container with `extraLarge` corners: the total in `displaySmallCondensed` (left to right, `numericContent`) beside "used on this device", a bar of the downloaded files (`primary`) and the databases (`tertiary`) by their shares (`spacing.large` tall, `small` corners, `mediaAlbumGap` apart, one item for screen readers saying the sizes), each part's color, name and size, and "Clear cache" as a full-width filled `Button`, which asks first as the row does; the "Storage usage" and "Cache" groups stay below. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-08): the board's four parts (media, files, cache, database) need TDLib's slow full statistics; the card shows the two that `getStorageStatisticsFast` gives, and leaves out the board's "Manage" |
| Active sessions | This device as a status card (`SettingsStatusCard`): "This device" over the device's model, its app and system, and where and when it is active after the dot, its device symbol on the `Cookie9Sided` badge; the group below keeps "Terminate all other sessions", without its header. The other sessions end with a swipe toward the start too (`SwipeToEndSession`, both looks): the row follows the finger over "End session" after the logout symbol on `errorContainer` in the row's shape, a `TextHandleMove` haptic where letting go ends it; letting go there springs the row back and asks first, as a tap does; screen readers use the tap. Last, "Automatically end inactive sessions" (both looks): a week, 1, 3 and 6 months and a year (and a period another app set) as radio rows in a `selectableGroup`, saved at once (td_api.tl `setInactiveSessionTtl`), the rows waiting while Telegram answers, a refusal a snackbar that brings the previous period back |
| Edit profile | "Save" a filled `Button` (`labelLargeEmphasized`, `ButtonDefaults.shapes()`) in place of the text button; the fields Material's filled `TextField`s (its shape, underlined) on the rows' container instead of the rounded tonal ones |
| About | A header in place of the logo: Pingogram's mark in `onPrimary` on a `Sunny` badge in `primary` (`sizes.avatarLarge`), "Pingogram" in `displaySmallWide` `primary` over "Version 0.3.0" on a `surfaceContainerHighest` pill (`labelMediumEmphasized`, left to right; one heading), then "What's new" (a filled `Button`, this version's release on GitHub) and "Check for updates" (a `FilledTonalButton`, the latest release on GitHub), both opening the browser: the app asks no server itself (`REQUIREMENTS.md` §6.2). Owner decision (2026-11-08): "Check for updates" is left out where an app store installed Pingogram (Google Play, Huawei AppGallery, Samsung Galaxy Store: Android's install source, `AppInfo.isFromAppStore`), as the store updates it and GitHub's releases are another build. Open-source licenses and Send feedback end in the chevron |
| Not yet | Nothing: the boards' features the owner decided on 2026-11-08 for the version after 2.0.0 (`docs/ROADMAP.md`) are in: the folder tabs' style (§6 "Folder tabs"), sticker search, "Suggest stickers by emoji" and "Loop animated stickers" (§6 "Stickers"), and Recent calls' "Start a call" (§6 "Recent calls") |

### 13.11 Calls, stories and media (the owner's boards "Session B", 2026-11-10)

The boards of calls, stories and media (9–14). Where they differ from §1–§12, the difference is written here, each an
"Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-10)" until the owner decides.

| UI element | Pingogram's look |
|---|---|
| Voice chat with comments (board 9a) | As §6 "Voice chat comments". Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): (1) the board's participant tiles show while the comments pane shows; without it the screen keeps its list of rows and its docked, labelled panel, so the comments change nothing for those who hide them; (2) the controls above the pane have no labels (the board has none), each named for screen readers; (3) the board's 64 dp `tertiaryContainer` mute square and 52 dp buttons are the call screen's toggles at Material's medium size; (4) the board gives each person a shape (cookie, flower, clover); people stay circles, as §13.2; (5) the board's header "More options" is left out: the call has nothing for it beyond the pane's own "Comment options"; (6) Telegram has no free reactions in group calls (td_api.tl has paid ones only, which Pingogram can't send), so the emoji button sends one of six quick reactions as a comment of its own, and comments that are exactly one of them float up as reactions; (7) "Comments · 214" counts the comments received since the user joined, as TDLib keeps none; (8) comments show as plain text: their formatting and custom emoji are not drawn yet |
| Conference call with comments (board 9b) | As 9a, with the call's video tiles and names (`VideoGrid`) and "Invite link" across the grid's width. Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-10): "Invite link" shares the link through Android's share sheet rather than copying it |
| New story, picking (board 10a) | As §6 "New story". Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): (1) a tap on a tile shows it, and removing is the shown item's "Remove from story" button, where the board's tiles remove on a tap: a tap that removes would leave nothing to show an item large, and removing is undone only by picking again; (2) "Camera" and "Next" at Material's medium height (56 dp), as on the board, rather than the default 40 dp §6 gives forms; (3) "Add more" with a solid `outline` border rather than the board's dashed one, which Compose draws only by hand; (4) from 150% font the tiles leave out a video's length and "Add more"'s label, which no longer fit a 72 dp tile; (5) the photo picker opens by itself as the screen first shows, and cancelling it with nothing picked closes the screen; (6) the count pill is `secondaryContainer`, the board's tonal fill |
| New story, composing (board 10b) | As §6 "New story: composing". Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): (1) the formatting bar holds Bold, Italic, Link and Spoiler; the board's "Aa" on the item, the text tool, is left out with the other editing tools (ADR 0036); (2) a video shows its still, not playing; (3) "Stays for 24 h" is a label, not a menu, as other periods need Telegram Premium; (4) the board's close button ("Discard story") is "Back to picking", which keeps the picks: closing is the picking step's, which deletes them; (5) the caption goes with the first item only; (6) the audience is Everyone, Contacts, Close friends or Selected people (the user's contacts only, picked in a sheet; Telegram's "everyone except" and "contacts except" are left out); (7) "Post" at Material's medium height, as on the board; (8) a refusal of the post keeps the screen and its picks, and says why in a snackbar |
| New story, posting (board 10c) | As §6 "Story rings" (Posting). Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): (1) a tap opens a sheet that shows the progress and offers "Stop posting", rather than cancelling at once, so a stray tap loses nothing; (2) a story TDLib gave up on offers "Remove" only: td_api.tl has no function to post a failed story again, so posting it again means picking it again; (3) the ring is Material's `CircularWavyProgressIndicator` with the user's avatar at 40 dp, rather than the board's plain arc around a dimmed thumbnail of the story; (4) the progress counts every story being posted as one upload, by their bytes; (5) while Appearance → "Show stories" is off, the rings and so the progress are hidden; (6) stories go on posting with the app in the background: the background transfers' notification (`docs/adr/0009-background-transfers.md`) counts each story still sending as a file ("Sending 2 files"), and its "Cancel" stops them, rather than a notification of the stories' own |
| Story rings: your story | Owner decision to confirm (2026-11-10, ADR 0036): the user's own ring is always first, also without a story of theirs, which shows the row whenever stories are shown. Without a story it has no ring and an add badge (the add symbol on a `primary` circle, `sizes.avatarBadge`, ringed in `surface` by `sizes.avatarRing`, at the avatar's bottom end, decorative), "Your story, Add a story" for screen readers, and a tap opens posting; with stories, a tap opens them and a long press, or the custom action "Add a story", opens posting |
| Story albums (boards 11a, 11b) | As §6 "Story albums". Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): (1) the covers show no story count, as td_api.tl's `storyAlbum` has none and counting would ask Telegram once per album; the opened album says how many; (2) the albums are a section of the profile, not a "Stories" tab beside "Posts" and "Media": the profile has no posts tab, and its shared media tabs list message kinds (the profile is Session C's, and the section is placed through one slot); (3) the opened album puts the poster and the count at the top of the sheet rather than under the name in the top bar, so neither is cut at large font scales; (4) the viewer plays an album's first 100 stories (one request); (5) "New album" on the user's own profile is left out: albums are only read for now; (6) the covers are 6:5 with `largeIncreased` corners and the stories 9:16 with `large` corners, the board's 24 px and 18 px |
| Live story (board 12) | As §6 "Live story". Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): (1) a live story is watched on the voice chat screen in a layout of its own, reached from the viewer's "Watch live", rather than inside the stories viewer; (2) the comments are the voice chat's pane below the stream (moderation, paid comments, the "can't pay" field) rather than translucent comments over the video; (3) the board's "★ Donate" is left out: live reactions cost Telegram Stars (td_api.tl `addPendingLiveStoryReaction`), which Pingogram can't pay, and a button that does nothing would be one more thing on screen; (4) the number watching is in the header's status, not a pill of its own, and there is no viewers list; (5) joining asks for the microphone as any voice chat does, though the user only watches; (6) starting a live story is not offered; (7) the stories viewer's live notice moves on after a photo's time, as other stories do; (8) the poster's avatar is a circle, as people's are (§13.2), not the board's flower |
| Profile music (boards 13a, 13b) | As §6 "Profile music". Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): (1) the playlist is a sheet on every profile, another user's without editing, where the board shows it on the user's own only; (2) songs are moved through each song's menu ("Move up", "Move down", also screen readers' custom actions) rather than the board's drag handle; (3) the user's own profile without songs shows a card offering "Add music", which the board does not show; (4) nothing plays once the profile leaves the screen, as playing on in the background needs a media session in a foreground service; (5) the first 100 songs are read; (6) the board's gradient covers are the songs' own album covers, or the music symbol on `tertiaryContainer`, and its coral play square is a round `FilledIconButton` in `primary`, Material's default; (7) "1 of 3 on her profile" is "Lena Fischer · 1 of 3", so it does not depend on the person's pronoun; (8) a song's title and artist are read from its file and cannot be edited |
| Make a sticker (boards 14a, 14b) | As §6 "Make a sticker". Owner decisions to confirm (2026-11-10): (1) the board's cut-out of the subject ("Subject found", "Cut out") is left out, as the brief says: the sticker is the photo cropped to a rounded square; (2) "Outline" draws a white border around that square rather than around a cut-out subject, on by default; (3) the tools are icon before label at Material's default height, as the Contacts tab's group, rather than the board's 56 px icon over label; (4) a layer is removed with "Remove" under the tools or the layer's custom action, rather than a control drawn on the photo, which the sticker would capture; (5) text is white with a near-black edge and emoji are drawn as typed, both the same in every theme (`StickerInk`, as a sticker goes to others); (6) "New sticker set" is the sheet's own second step (a name and a link) rather than Settings' New sticker set page, which takes photos from the picker and cannot take the sticker just made; (7) the sticker's emoji offers five quick ones and "More emoji", the board's five; (8) the board's coral is `primary` (the Coral theme's coral); (9) the editor opens in the viewer's place and closes back to the conversation; (10) after a process death the sheet is not restored: the editor is, and "Next" writes the sticker again |

### 13.12 Profiles, contacts, groups and settings (the owner's boards 15–22, 2026-11-10)

The owner's boards of 2026-11-10 for the Telegram features Pingogram did not have yet, as far as they are built. Where
a board and §1–§12 differ, the board wins and the line says so.

| UI element | Pingogram's look |
|---|---|
| Contact note (boards 15a, 15b) | As §6 "Contact note". Owner decision (2026-11-11, the recommended choice): the note row is the one row of "Info" with a trailing symbol (the board's edit circle, decorative) and a tinted container (8% `primary`), where §6 "Profile info and more" keeps the rows without icons on the rows' color; a contact without a note shows "Add a note" in the row's place, which the board does not show; the dialog's badge is `MaterialShapes.Cookie9Sided` at `avatarRow` (48 dp, the board's 48 px) and its title `titleLargeEmphasized` (the board's 22 px). The board's profile header (the avatar's shape, connected action pills) is the profile's, unchanged here |
| Birthday suggestions (boards 16a, 16b) | As §6 "Birthday suggestions". Owner decision (2026-11-11, the recommended choice): the board's gold is `tertiaryContainer` / `tertiary` (the Coral theme's gold, another hue in other themes); the offer's badge is `MaterialShapes.Sunny` at `avatarRow` and the card's `Cookie9Sided` at `headlineBadge` (the board's 64 px); the date uses the condensed display style at 36 sp, below the board's 44 px; the picker is Edit profile's, with "Show the year", as td_api.tl lets a birthday leave the year out; "Declined" is kept on this device only, as Telegram sends no answer to a declined suggestion; and the card is in `core:ui` for `feature:chat` (Session A) to place among the messages, which this change does not do |
| Passkeys (boards 17a, 17b) | As §6 "Passkeys" and §6 "Sign in with a passkey". Owner decisions (2026-11-11, the recommended choices): see those rows |
| Member tags (boards 18a, 18b) | As §6 "Member tags". Owner decisions (2026-11-11, the recommended choices): (1) the board's "2,341 members · 6 with tags" leaves out the count of tags, which Telegram gives only by loading every member; (2) the board's search "Search members or tags" stays "Search members", as td_api.tl's member search says nothing of tags; (3) the role label ("Owner", "Admin") stays at the row's end beside the tag, as on the board, where the tag used to replace it; (4) the accent is `primaryContainer` (the board's coral in the Coral theme); (5) the preview's sample message, "Hello, everyone!", is no message anyone sent; (6) the board's preview legend ("Admin tag accent · Member tag neutral") is one sentence; (7) the board's sheet buttons share the row's width, here "Remove tag" sits at the start and "Save" at the end at Material's default height, as the app's other sheets; (8) the group setting is the permission "Set their own tag" on the group's permissions page and a member's restrictions |
| Communities (boards 19a, 19b) | As §6 "Community page". Owner decisions (2026-11-11, the recommended choices, updated for TDLib 1.8.70): (1) "Add chats", "Suggest a chat" and "Mute" are in since TDLib 1.8.70; the board's "Invite" is left out, as TDLib has no invite link for a community; (2) the board's member count ("4,812 members") is left out, as td_api.tl gives a community none; (3) the chat list's community entries (board 19b, §6 "Community entry") show only for communities set to "Show as one entry in the chat list" (td_api.tl `are_chats_collapsed`); TDLib keeps no place for a community in the chat list, so the entry stands where its newest chat would be; pinned chats stay in the pinned group rather than in their community's entry; the board's expand and collapse is kept on the device and starts collapsed; its chats are inset by `spacing.large` (the board's 20 px) as segments of the entry's group, on `surfaceContainer` like the pinned group, rather than the board's lighter inner rows; its long press offers "Show chats separately" and no way to the community's page, which opens from a chat's info page; (8) removing a chat is a long press on its row, which the board does not show; (9) the requests to add a chat show above the chats for those who may answer them, and the add sheet's "Visible to admins only" applies to every chat picked; (4) the page opens from a chat's info page only, its "Info" group's community row, rather than from the chat list; (5) "Joined" is a label, not the board's button, as leaving goes through the chat; (6) "Community options" holds "Rename" and "Delete community", what td_api.tl offers its owner and administrators beyond the board's settings gear; (7) the community's photo is drawn from `community.photo` on `MaterialShapes.Sunny`, a new `AvatarShape.Community` |
| Welcome message setup (board 20) | As §6 "Welcome message setup". Owner decisions (2026-11-11, the recommended choices): (1) the page opens from the info page's "Administration" group, not from "Group settings" as on the board: the right to manage welcome messages stands alone, and an administrator with only that right does not reach the settings page; (2) td_api.tl (a94b83d) has no function to reorder welcome messages, so the board's drag handles are left out and new messages come last; (3) the switch has no setting of its own in td_api.tl: it is on while the group has a message, and turning it off deletes them all, after asking; (4) messages are a text or a photo with a caption, written as plain text: a message another app formatted keeps its formatting until its text changes here, and other kinds Telegram allows show as "Can't be shown" and can only be deleted; (5) "Add message" has a solid outline rather than the board's dashed one, as in board 10a; (6) the preview shows the messages without the sender's name or tag, which the new member's side (board 6, Session A) draws; (7) only supergroups offer it, as TDLib refuses welcome messages elsewhere ("Chat can't have welcome messages") |
| Sticker sets (boards 21a–c) | As §6 "New sticker set". Owner decisions (2026-11-11, the recommended choices): (1) a photo becomes a sticker as it is, scaled so its longer side is 512 px, without cutting out its subject or the board 14a sticker maker, which is Session B's and not built yet; "Add sticker" opens the photo picker directly; (2) the board's drag to reorder is a long-press menu of "Move earlier" and "Move later" in a grid of three, which screen readers get as custom actions; (3) each sticker takes one emoji, a smiling face until the user chooses one, as Telegram requires one; (4) the board's "Edit" after publishing is left out: the set opens in its sheet, and editing a published set (`addStickerToSet` and the rest) comes later; (5) the board's close button and its "Step 1 of 2" in the top bar are the settings page's back button and a line of text; (6) only static stickers; (7) the link's check on the device follows the board's "5 to 64 characters", Telegram having the last word |
| Profile color and emoji status (boards 22a, 22b) | As §6 "Your color" and §6 "Profile header". Owner decisions (2026-11-11, the recommended choices): (1) Telegram's color ids are drawn in the theme's eight sender hues generated by `tools/theme` (§13.6), never in Telegram's own colors: a name color by the built-in color it is drawn like, a profile header's color by its id among the eight; (2) the seven built-in name colors are offered to everyone, as the board does, and Telegram decides: its refusal (Telegram Premium required) is a snackbar, never worked around; (3) the board's Premium two-tone colors show as one hue each, dimmed with a lock, as information; (4) setting an emoji status and the board's "Emoji status" row are left out, as only Telegram Premium subscribers may set one; a profile shows the status after the name, without opening its pack on a tap; (5) the board's "In chats her name looks like this" on other people's profiles is left out: groups show it; (6) the header is tinted only for users who set a profile color, with `extraLarge` bottom corners |
